-- File: agent.ts
/**
 * FluxGuard Agent - 杞婚噺绾у畨鍏ㄩ槻鎶や唬鐞?
 * 
 * 鍔熻兘:
 * - 蹇冭烦涓婃姤
 * - 娴侀噺鐩戞帶
 * - 鏀诲嚮妫€娴?
 * - 闃茬伀澧欐帶鍒?
 * - 涓庝腑澶鎺у钩鍙伴€氫俊
 */

import os from 'os';
import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { TrafficMonitor } from './traffic-monitor';
import { AttackDetector } from './attack-detector';
import { FirewallController } from './firewall-controller';

export interface AgentConfig {
    serverUrl: string;       // 涓ぎ绠℃帶骞冲彴鍦板潃
    agentId?: string;        // Agent 鍞竴鏍囪瘑 (鑷姩鐢熸垚)
    heartbeatInterval?: number; // 蹇冭烦闂撮殧 (ms)
}

export interface AgentStatus {
    agentId: string;
    hostname: string;
    platform: string;
    uptime: number;
    cpuUsage: number;
    memoryUsage: number;
    lastHeartbeat: string;
    isOnline: boolean;
}

export class Agent {
    private config: AgentConfig;
    private socket: Socket | null = null;
    private agentId: string;
    private heartbeatTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
    private trafficMonitor: TrafficMonitor;
    private attackDetector: AttackDetector;
    private firewallController: FirewallController;

    constructor(config: AgentConfig) {
        this.config = {
            heartbeatInterval: 30000, // 榛樿 30 绉?
            ...config
        };

        // 鐢熸垚鍞竴 Agent ID (鍩轰簬涓绘満鍚?+ MAC 鍦板潃)
        this.agentId = config.agentId || this.generateAgentId();

        // 鍒濆鍖栧瓙妯″潡
        this.trafficMonitor = new TrafficMonitor();
        this.attackDetector = new AttackDetector();
        this.firewallController = new FirewallController();
    }

    /**
     * 鐢熸垚鍞竴 Agent ID
     */
    private generateAgentId(): string {
        const hostname = os.hostname();
        const interfaces = os.networkInterfaces();
        let mac = 'unknown';

        for (const name of Object.keys(interfaces)) {
            for (const iface of interfaces[name] || []) {
                if (!iface.internal && iface.mac !== '00:00:00:00:00:00') {
                    mac = iface.mac.replace(/:/g, '');
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        return `agent-${hostname}-${mac.substring(0, 8)}`;
    }

    /**
     * 鍚姩 Agent
     */
    async start(): Promise<void> {
        logger.info(`Starting FluxGuard Agent: ${this.agentId}`);

        // 杩炴帴鍒颁腑澶鎺у钩鍙?
        await this.connectToServer();

        // 鍚姩蹇冭烦
        this.startHeartbeat();

        // 鍚姩娴侀噺鐩戞帶
        await this.trafficMonitor.start((event) => {
            // 鍒嗘瀽娴侀噺浜嬩欢
            const threat = this.attackDetector.analyze(event);
            if (threat) {
                this.handleThreat(threat);
            }
        });

        logger.info('Agent started successfully');
    }

    /**
     * 杩炴帴鍒颁腑澶鎺у钩鍙?
     */
    private async connectToServer(): Promise<void> {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            logger.info(`Connecting to server: ${this.config.serverUrl}`);

            this.socket = io(this.config.serverUrl, {
                auth: { agentId: this.agentId },
                reconnection: true,
                reconnectionAttempts: Infinity,
                reconnectionDelay: 5000
            });

            this.socket.on('connect', () => {
                logger.info('Connected to control platform');
                this.registerAgent();
                resolve();
            });

            this.socket.on('disconnect', () => {
                logger.warn('Disconnected from control platform');
            });

            this.socket.on('connect_error', (error) => {
                logger.error('Connection error', { error: error.message });
            });

            // 鐩戝惉鏉ヨ嚜骞冲彴鐨勬寚浠?
            this.socket.on('command', (command) => {
                this.handleCommand(command);
            });

            // 璁剧疆杩炴帴瓒呮椂
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (!this.socket?.connected) {
                    logger.warn('Connection timeout, will retry in background');
                    resolve(); // 涓嶉樆濉炲惎鍔?
                }
            }, 10000);
        });
    }

    /**
     * 鍚戝钩鍙版敞鍐?Agent
     */
    private registerAgent(): void {
        if (!this.socket) return;

        const registration = {
            agentId: this.agentId,
            hostname: os.hostname(),
            platform: os.platform(),
            arch: os.arch(),
            cpus: os.cpus().length,
            totalMemory: os.totalmem(),
            version: '1.0.0'
        };

        this.socket.emit('agent:register', registration);
        logger.info('Agent registered with platform');
    }

    /**
     * 鍚姩蹇冭烦
     */
    private startHeartbeat(): void {
        this.heartbeatTimer = setInterval(() => {
            this.sendHeartbeat();
        }, this.config.heartbeatInterval);

        // 绔嬪嵆鍙戦€佷竴娆?
        this.sendHeartbeat();
    }

    /**
     * 鍙戦€佸績璺?
     */
    private sendHeartbeat(): void {
        if (!this.socket?.connected) return;

        const status: AgentStatus = {
            agentId: this.agentId,
            hostname: os.hostname(),
            platform: os.platform(),
            uptime: os.uptime(),
            cpuUsage: this.getCpuUsage(),
            memoryUsage: this.getMemoryUsage(),
            lastHeartbeat: new Date().toISOString(),
            isOnline: true
        };

        this.socket.emit('agent:heartbeat', status);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇 CPU 浣跨敤鐜?
     */
    private getCpuUsage(): number {
        const cpus = os.cpus();
        let totalIdle = 0;
        let totalTick = 0;

        for (const cpu of cpus) {
            for (const type in cpu.times) {
                totalTick += cpu.times[type as keyof typeof cpu.times];
            }
            totalIdle += cpu.times.idle;
        }

        return Math.round((1 - totalIdle / totalTick) * 100);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍐呭瓨浣跨敤鐜?
     */
    private getMemoryUsage(): number {
        const total = os.totalmem();
        const free = os.freemem();
        return Math.round((1 - free / total) * 100);
    }

    /**
     * 澶勭悊鏉ヨ嚜骞冲彴鐨勬寚浠?
     */
    private async handleCommand(command: { type: string; payload?: any }): Promise<void> {
        logger.info(`Received command: ${command.type}`);

        switch (command.type) {
            case 'scan':
                // 瑙﹀彂鏈湴鎵弿
                await this.runLocalScan();
                break;

            case 'block_ip':
                // 灏佺 IP
                if (command.payload?.ip) {
                    await this.firewallController.blockIP(command.payload.ip);
                }
                break;

            case 'unblock_ip':
                // 瑙ｅ皝 IP
                if (command.payload?.ip) {
                    await this.firewallController.unblockIP(command.payload.ip);
                }
                break;

            case 'update_rules':
                // 鏇存柊妫€娴嬭鍒?
                if (command.payload?.rules) {
                    this.attackDetector.updateRules(command.payload.rules);
                }
                break;

            default:
                logger.warn(`Unknown command: ${command.type}`);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 澶勭悊妫€娴嬪埌鐨勫▉鑳?
     */
    private handleThreat(threat: any): void {
        logger.warn('Threat detected', { threat });

        // 涓婃姤濞佽儊鍒板钩鍙?
        if (this.socket?.connected) {
            this.socket.emit('agent:threat', {
                agentId: this.agentId,
                threat,
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
            });
        }

        // 鑷姩闃绘柇 (濡傛灉閰嶇疆浜?
        if (threat.autoBlock && threat.sourceIP) {
            this.firewallController.blockIP(threat.sourceIP, threat.blockDuration || 3600);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 杩愯鏈湴鎵弿
     */
    private async runLocalScan(): Promise<void> {
        logger.info('Running local security scan...');

        // 杩欓噷鍙互璋冪敤鐜版湁鐨?Scanner 妯″潡
        // 缁撴灉閫氳繃 WebSocket 涓婃姤

        if (this.socket?.connected) {
            this.socket.emit('agent:scan_started', { agentId: this.agentId });
        }

        // TODO: 闆嗘垚鐜版湁 Scanner
    }

    /**
     * 鍋滄 Agent
     */
    async stop(): Promise<void> {
        logger.info('Stopping Agent...');

        if (this.heartbeatTimer) {
            clearInterval(this.heartbeatTimer);
        }

        await this.trafficMonitor.stop();

        if (this.socket) {
            this.socket.disconnect();
        }

        logger.info('Agent stopped');
    }
}

// 鍚姩鍏ュ彛
export async function startAgent(serverUrl: string): Promise<Agent> {
    const agent = new Agent({ serverUrl });
    await agent.start();
    return agent;
}
-- File: attack-detector.ts
/**
 * 鏀诲嚮妫€娴嬪紩鎿?
 * 
 * 瀹炴椂鍒嗘瀽娴侀噺锛屾娴嬪悇绫绘敾鍑绘ā寮忥細
 * - SQL 娉ㄥ叆
 * - XSS 鏀诲嚮
 * - 鐩綍閬嶅巻
 * - 鍛戒护娉ㄥ叆
 * - 鏆村姏鐮磋В
 * - DDoS 妯″紡
 */

import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { TrafficEvent } from './traffic-monitor';

export interface ThreatAlert {
    type: string;
    severity: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical';
    sourceIP: string;
    description: string;
    evidence: string;
    ruleId: string;
    autoBlock: boolean;
    blockDuration?: number; // 绉?
    timestamp: Date;
}

interface DetectionRule {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    type: string;
    severity: ThreatAlert['severity'];
    patterns: RegExp[];
    autoBlock: boolean;
    blockDuration?: number;
}

export class AttackDetector {
    private rules: DetectionRule[] = [];

    // IP 璇锋眰璁℃暟鍣?(鐢ㄤ簬妫€娴嬫毚鍔涚牬瑙ｅ拰 DDoS)
    private ipRequestCount: Map<string, { count: number; firstSeen: number }> = new Map();

    // 娓呯悊璁℃暟鍣ㄧ殑闂撮殧
    private cleanupInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

    constructor() {
        this.initializeDefaultRules();
        this.startCleanupTimer();
    }

    /**
     * 鍒濆鍖栭粯璁ゆ娴嬭鍒?
     */
    private initializeDefaultRules(): void {
        this.rules = [
            // SQL 娉ㄥ叆妫€娴?
            {
                id: 'SQL-001',
                name: 'SQL Injection - UNION SELECT',
                type: 'sql_injection',
                severity: 'critical',
                patterns: [
                    /(\s|%20)union(\s|%20)+select/i,
                    /(\s|%20)union(\s|%20)+all(\s|%20)+select/i
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 3600
            },
            {
                id: 'SQL-002',
                name: 'SQL Injection - Comment Bypass',
                type: 'sql_injection',
                severity: 'high',
                patterns: [
                    /'\s*or\s*'1'\s*=\s*'1/i,
                    /'\s*or\s*1\s*=\s*1/i,
                    /'\s*--/,
                    /'\s*#/
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 3600
            },
            {
                id: 'SQL-003',
                name: 'SQL Injection - Stacked Queries',
                type: 'sql_injection',
                severity: 'critical',
                patterns: [
                    /;\s*(drop|delete|insert|update|create|alter|truncate)/i
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 7200
            },

            // XSS 妫€娴?
            {
                id: 'XSS-001',
                name: 'XSS - Script Tag',
                type: 'xss',
                severity: 'high',
                patterns: [
                    /<script[^>]*>/i,
                    /<\/script>/i,
                    /javascript:/i
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 1800
            },
            {
                id: 'XSS-002',
                name: 'XSS - Event Handler',
                type: 'xss',
                severity: 'medium',
                patterns: [
                    /on(load|error|click|mouseover|submit|focus|blur)\s*=/i,
                    /onerror\s*=/i
                ],
                autoBlock: false
            },
            {
                id: 'XSS-003',
                name: 'XSS - Data URI',
                type: 'xss',
                severity: 'high',
                patterns: [
                    /data:\s*text\/html/i,
                    /data:\s*application\/javascript/i
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 1800
            },

            // 鐩綍閬嶅巻妫€娴?
            {
                id: 'LFI-001',
                name: 'Path Traversal',
                type: 'lfi',
                severity: 'high',
                patterns: [
                    /\.\.\//,
                    /\.\.%2[fF]/,
                    /%2e%2e%2f/i,
                    /\.\.\\/,
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 3600
            },
            {
                id: 'LFI-002',
                name: 'Sensitive File Access',
                type: 'lfi',
                severity: 'critical',
                patterns: [
                    /\/etc\/passwd/i,
                    /\/etc\/shadow/i,
                    /\/proc\/self/i,
                    /web\.config/i,
                    /\.env/,
                    /\.git\//
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 7200
            },

            // 鍛戒护娉ㄥ叆妫€娴?
            {
                id: 'CMD-001',
                name: 'Command Injection',
                type: 'command_injection',
                severity: 'critical',
                patterns: [
                    /;\s*(ls|cat|whoami|id|uname|pwd|wget|curl)/i,
                    /\|\s*(ls|cat|whoami|id|uname|pwd|wget|curl)/i,
                    /`[^`]+`/,
                    /\$\([^)]+\)/
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 7200
            },

            // 鎭舵剰 Bot 妫€娴?
            {
                id: 'BOT-001',
                name: 'Malicious Bot User-Agent',
                type: 'bad_bot',
                severity: 'medium',
                patterns: [
                    /sqlmap/i,
                    /nikto/i,
                    /nessus/i,
                    /masscan/i,
                    /zgrab/i,
                    /python-requests/i,
                    /libwww-perl/i
                ],
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 86400
            }
        ];

        logger.info(`Loaded ${this.rules.length} detection rules`);
    }

    /**
     * 鍒嗘瀽娴侀噺浜嬩欢
     */
    analyze(event: TrafficEvent): ThreatAlert | null {
        // 鏇存柊 IP 璇锋眰璁℃暟
        this.updateIPCounter(event.sourceIP);

        // 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚︿负 DDoS/鏆村姏鐮磋В
        const rateThreat = this.checkRateLimit(event.sourceIP);
        if (rateThreat) {
            return rateThreat;
        }

        // 鏋勫缓寰呮娴嬬殑鍐呭
        const contentToCheck = [
            event.path || '',
            event.requestBody || '',
            event.userAgent || '',
            event.rawLog || ''
        ].join(' ');

        // 閬嶅巻瑙勫垯杩涜妫€娴?
        for (const rule of this.rules) {
            for (const pattern of rule.patterns) {
                if (pattern.test(contentToCheck)) {
                    const threat: ThreatAlert = {
                        type: rule.type,
                        severity: rule.severity,
                        sourceIP: event.sourceIP,
                        description: rule.name,
                        evidence: this.extractEvidence(contentToCheck, pattern),
                        ruleId: rule.id,
                        autoBlock: rule.autoBlock,
                        blockDuration: rule.blockDuration,
                        timestamp: new Date()
                    };

                    logger.warn(`Attack detected: ${rule.name}`, {
                        ip: event.sourceIP,
                        ruleId: rule.id
                    });

                    return threat;
                }
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 鏇存柊 IP 璇锋眰璁℃暟鍣?
     */
    private updateIPCounter(ip: string): void {
        const now = Date.now();
        const existing = this.ipRequestCount.get(ip);

        if (existing) {
            existing.count++;
        } else {
            this.ipRequestCount.set(ip, { count: 1, firstSeen: now });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ヨ姹傞€熺巼闄愬埗
     */
    private checkRateLimit(ip: string): ThreatAlert | null {
        const data = this.ipRequestCount.get(ip);
        if (!data) return null;

        const elapsed = (Date.now() - data.firstSeen) / 1000; // 绉?
        const ratePerSecond = data.count / Math.max(elapsed, 1);

        // 瓒呰繃 100 璇锋眰/绉?瑙嗕负 DDoS
        if (ratePerSecond > 100) {
            return {
                type: 'ddos',
                severity: 'critical',
                sourceIP: ip,
                description: 'DDoS Attack Detected - High Request Rate',
                evidence: `${data.count} requests in ${elapsed.toFixed(1)}s (${ratePerSecond.toFixed(1)}/s)`,
                ruleId: 'DDOS-001',
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 3600,
                timestamp: new Date()
            };
        }

        // 瓒呰繃 30 璇锋眰/绉?瑙嗕负鏆村姏鐮磋В
        if (ratePerSecond > 30) {
            return {
                type: 'brute_force',
                severity: 'high',
                sourceIP: ip,
                description: 'Possible Brute Force Attack',
                evidence: `${data.count} requests in ${elapsed.toFixed(1)}s (${ratePerSecond.toFixed(1)}/s)`,
                ruleId: 'BF-001',
                autoBlock: true,
                blockDuration: 1800,
                timestamp: new Date()
            };
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 鎻愬彇鏀诲嚮璇佹嵁
     */
    private extractEvidence(content: string, pattern: RegExp): string {
        const match = content.match(pattern);
        if (match) {
            // 杩斿洖鍖归厤鍐呭鍓嶅悗鍚?20 涓瓧绗?
            const index = content.indexOf(match[0]);
            const start = Math.max(0, index - 20);
            const end = Math.min(content.length, index + match[0].length + 20);
            return `...${content.substring(start, end)}...`;
        }
        return '';
    }

    /**
     * 鍚姩娓呯悊璁℃椂鍣?
     */
    private startCleanupTimer(): void {
        // 姣忓垎閽熸竻鐞嗚繃鏈熺殑璁℃暟鍣?
        this.cleanupInterval = setInterval(() => {
            const now = Date.now();
            const expiryTime = 60000; // 1 鍒嗛挓

            for (const [ip, data] of this.ipRequestCount) {
                if (now - data.firstSeen > expiryTime) {
                    this.ipRequestCount.delete(ip);
                }
            }
        }, 60000);
    }

    /**
     * 鏇存柊妫€娴嬭鍒?
     */
    updateRules(newRules: DetectionRule[]): void {
        this.rules = newRules;
        logger.info(`Updated detection rules: ${newRules.length} rules loaded`);
    }

    /**
     * 娣诲姞鑷畾涔夎鍒?
     */
    addRule(rule: DetectionRule): void {
        this.rules.push(rule);
        logger.info(`Added detection rule: ${rule.id}`);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈夎鍒?
     */
    getRules(): DetectionRule[] {
        return [...this.rules];
    }

    /**
     * 鍋滄妫€娴嬪櫒
     */
    stop(): void {
        if (this.cleanupInterval) {
            clearInterval(this.cleanupInterval);
        }
        this.ipRequestCount.clear();
    }
}
-- File: firewall-controller.ts
/**
 * 闃茬伀澧欐帶鍒跺櫒
 * 
 * 璋冪敤绯荤粺闃茬伀澧欏伐鍏峰皝绂?瑙ｅ皝 IP:
 * - Linux: iptables / firewalld / ufw
 * - Windows: netsh
 */

import { exec } from 'child_process';
import { promisify } from 'util';
import os from 'os';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

interface BlockedIP {
    ip: string;
    reason: string;
    blockedAt: Date;
    expiresAt?: Date;
    timerId?: NodeJS.Timeout;
}

type FirewallBackend = 'iptables' | 'firewalld' | 'ufw' | 'netsh' | 'none';

export class FirewallController {
    private blockedIPs: Map<string, BlockedIP> = new Map();
    private backend: FirewallBackend = 'none';

    constructor() {
        this.detectBackend();
    }

    /**
     * 妫€娴嬪彲鐢ㄧ殑闃茬伀澧欏悗绔?
     */
    private async detectBackend(): Promise<void> {
        const platform = os.platform();

        if (platform === 'win32') {
            this.backend = 'netsh';
            logger.info('Firewall backend: Windows netsh');
            return;
        }

        // Linux: 鎸変紭鍏堢骇妫€娴?
        try {
            await execAsync('which firewall-cmd');
            this.backend = 'firewalld';
            logger.info('Firewall backend: firewalld');
            return;
        } catch { }

        try {
            await execAsync('which ufw');
            this.backend = 'ufw';
            logger.info('Firewall backend: ufw');
            return;
        } catch { }

        try {
            await execAsync('which iptables');
            this.backend = 'iptables';
            logger.info('Firewall backend: iptables');
            return;
        } catch { }

        logger.warn('No firewall backend detected');
        this.backend = 'none';
    }

    /**
     * 灏佺 IP
     * @param ip 瑕佸皝绂佺殑 IP 鍦板潃
     * @param duration 灏佺鏃堕暱 (绉?, 0 琛ㄧず姘镐箙
     * @param reason 灏佺鍘熷洜
     */
    async blockIP(ip: string, duration: number = 3600, reason: string = 'Threat detected'): Promise<boolean> {
        // 楠岃瘉 IP 鏍煎紡
        if (!this.isValidIP(ip)) {
            logger.error(`Invalid IP address: ${ip}`);
            return false;
        }

        // 璺宠繃鏈湴鍦板潃
        if (this.isLocalIP(ip)) {
            logger.warn(`Skipping local IP: ${ip}`);
            return false;
        }

        // 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚﹀凡灏佺
        if (this.blockedIPs.has(ip)) {
            logger.info(`IP already blocked: ${ip}`);
            return true;
        }

        let success = false;

        try {
            switch (this.backend) {
                case 'iptables':
                    success = await this.blockWithIptables(ip);
                    break;
                case 'firewalld':
                    success = await this.blockWithFirewalld(ip);
                    break;
                case 'ufw':
                    success = await this.blockWithUfw(ip);
                    break;
                case 'netsh':
                    success = await this.blockWithNetsh(ip);
                    break;
                default:
                    logger.warn(`No firewall backend available, IP not blocked: ${ip}`);
                    // 鍗充娇娌℃湁闃茬伀澧欙紝涔熻褰曞埌鍐呭瓨
                    success = true;
            }

            if (success) {
                const blockedIP: BlockedIP = {
                    ip,
                    reason,
                    blockedAt: new Date(),
                    expiresAt: duration > 0 ? new Date(Date.now() + duration * 1000) : undefined
                };

                // 璁剧疆鑷姩瑙ｅ皝璁℃椂鍣?
                if (duration > 0) {
                    blockedIP.timerId = setTimeout(() => {
                        this.unblockIP(ip);
                    }, duration * 1000);
                }

                this.blockedIPs.set(ip, blockedIP);
                logger.info(`Blocked IP: ${ip} for ${duration}s (${reason})`);
            }

            return success;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`Failed to block IP: ${ip}`, { error: error.message });
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｅ皝 IP
     */
    async unblockIP(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        const blocked = this.blockedIPs.get(ip);
        if (!blocked) {
            logger.info(`IP not in block list: ${ip}`);
            return true;
        }

        // 娓呴櫎璁℃椂鍣?
        if (blocked.timerId) {
            clearTimeout(blocked.timerId);
        }

        let success = false;

        try {
            switch (this.backend) {
                case 'iptables':
                    success = await this.unblockWithIptables(ip);
                    break;
                case 'firewalld':
                    success = await this.unblockWithFirewalld(ip);
                    break;
                case 'ufw':
                    success = await this.unblockWithUfw(ip);
                    break;
                case 'netsh':
                    success = await this.unblockWithNetsh(ip);
                    break;
                default:
                    success = true;
            }

            if (success) {
                this.blockedIPs.delete(ip);
                logger.info(`Unblocked IP: ${ip}`);
            }

            return success;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`Failed to unblock IP: ${ip}`, { error: error.message });
            return false;
        }
    }

    // === iptables 瀹炵幇 ===

    private async blockWithIptables(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            await execAsync(`iptables -A INPUT -s ${ip} -j DROP`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`iptables block failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private async unblockWithIptables(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            await execAsync(`iptables -D INPUT -s ${ip} -j DROP`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`iptables unblock failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    // === firewalld 瀹炵幇 ===

    private async blockWithFirewalld(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            await execAsync(`firewall-cmd --add-rich-rule='rule family="ipv4" source address="${ip}" reject'`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`firewalld block failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private async unblockWithFirewalld(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            await execAsync(`firewall-cmd --remove-rich-rule='rule family="ipv4" source address="${ip}" reject'`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`firewalld unblock failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    // === ufw 瀹炵幇 ===

    private async blockWithUfw(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            await execAsync(`ufw deny from ${ip}`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`ufw block failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private async unblockWithUfw(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            await execAsync(`ufw delete deny from ${ip}`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`ufw unblock failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    // === Windows netsh 瀹炵幇 ===

    private async blockWithNetsh(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            const ruleName = `FluxGuard_Block_${ip.replace(/\./g, '_')}`;
            await execAsync(`netsh advfirewall firewall add rule name="${ruleName}" dir=in action=block remoteip=${ip}`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`netsh block failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private async unblockWithNetsh(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            const ruleName = `FluxGuard_Block_${ip.replace(/\./g, '_')}`;
            await execAsync(`netsh advfirewall firewall delete rule name="${ruleName}"`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`netsh unblock failed: ${error.message}`);
            return false;
        }
    }

    // === 杈呭姪鏂规硶 ===

    /**
     * 楠岃瘉 IP 鏍煎紡
     */
    private isValidIP(ip: string): boolean {
        const ipv4Regex = /^(\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$/;
        const ipv6Regex = /^([0-9a-fA-F]{1,4}:){7}[0-9a-fA-F]{1,4}$/;
        return ipv4Regex.test(ip) || ipv6Regex.test(ip);
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚︿负鏈湴 IP
     */
    private isLocalIP(ip: string): boolean {
        return ip === '127.0.0.1' ||
            ip === '::1' ||
            ip.startsWith('192.168.') ||
            ip.startsWith('10.') ||
            ip.startsWith('172.16.');
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈夎灏佺鐨?IP
     */
    getBlockedIPs(): BlockedIP[] {
        return Array.from(this.blockedIPs.values());
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌?IP 鏄惁琚皝绂?
     */
    isBlocked(ip: string): boolean {
        return this.blockedIPs.has(ip);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇褰撳墠浣跨敤鐨勯槻鐏鍚庣
     */
    getBackend(): FirewallBackend {
        return this.backend;
    }

    /**
     * 娓呴櫎鎵€鏈夊皝绂?
     */
    async clearAll(): Promise<void> {
        for (const ip of this.blockedIPs.keys()) {
            await this.unblockIP(ip);
        }
    }
}

/**

 */

export { Agent, AgentConfig, AgentStatus, startAgent } from './agent';
export { TrafficMonitor, TrafficEvent, TrafficCallback } from './traffic-monitor';
export { AttackDetector, ThreatAlert } from './attack-detector';
export { FirewallController } from './firewall-controller';
-- File: log-collector.ts
/**
 * 鏃ュ織閲囬泦妯″潡
 * 
 * 閲囬泦绯荤粺鍜屽簲鐢ㄦ棩蹇楋紝妫€娴嬪紓甯告ā寮?
 */

import fs from 'fs';
import readline from 'readline';
import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

export interface LogEntry {
    source: string;
    timestamp: Date;
    level: 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error' | 'critical';
    message: string;
    raw: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface LogPattern {
    name: string;
    pattern: RegExp;
    severity: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical';
    description: string;
}

export class LogCollector extends EventEmitter {
    private watchers: Map<string, fs.FSWatcher> = new Map();
    private filePositions: Map<string, number> = new Map();
    private isRunning: boolean = false;

    // 闇€瑕佹娴嬬殑鍗遍櫓妯″紡
    private dangerousPatterns: LogPattern[] = [
        {
            name: 'SSH Brute Force',
            pattern: /Failed password for (?:invalid user )?(\S+) from (\S+)/i,
            severity: 'high',
            description: 'SSH login failure detected'
        },
        {
            name: 'SSH Root Login',
            pattern: /Accepted password for root from/i,
            severity: 'medium',
            description: 'Root SSH login detected'
        },
        {
            name: 'Sudo Failure',
            pattern: /sudo:.*authentication failure/i,
            severity: 'high',
            description: 'Sudo authentication failure'
        },
        {
            name: 'Kernel Panic',
            pattern: /Kernel panic/i,
            severity: 'critical',
            description: 'Kernel panic detected'
        },
        {
            name: 'OOM Killer',
            pattern: /Out of memory: Kill process/i,
            severity: 'high',
            description: 'Out of memory condition'
        },
        {
            name: 'Disk Error',
            pattern: /I\/O error|sector error|disk error/i,
            severity: 'high',
            description: 'Disk I/O error detected'
        },
        {
            name: 'Service Crash',
            pattern: /segfault|core dumped|killed/i,
            severity: 'high',
            description: 'Service crash detected'
        },
        {
            name: 'PHP Error',
            pattern: /PHP Fatal error|PHP Parse error/i,
            severity: 'medium',
            description: 'PHP error in application'
        },
        {
            name: 'SQL Error',
            pattern: /SQL syntax error|mysql.*error/i,
            severity: 'medium',
            description: 'Database SQL error'
        },
        {
            name: 'Connection Refused',
            pattern: /Connection refused|ECONNREFUSED/i,
            severity: 'medium',
            description: 'Service connection refused'
        }
    ];

    // 甯歌鏃ュ織鏂囦欢璺緞
    private readonly LOG_SOURCES = {
        syslog: ['/var/log/syslog', '/var/log/messages'],
        auth: ['/var/log/auth.log', '/var/log/secure'],
        nginx: ['/var/log/nginx/error.log'],
        apache: ['/var/log/apache2/error.log', '/var/log/httpd/error_log'],
        mysql: ['/var/log/mysql/error.log', '/var/log/mariadb/mariadb.log'],
        postgresql: ['/var/log/postgresql/postgresql.log'],
        docker: ['/var/log/docker.log'],
    };

    /**
     * 鍚姩鏃ュ織閲囬泦
     */
    async start(): Promise<void> {
        if (this.isRunning) {
            logger.warn('Log collector already running');
            return;
        }

        this.isRunning = true;
        logger.info('Starting log collector...');

        // 鏌ユ壘骞剁洃鎺ф墍鏈夊彲鐢ㄧ殑鏃ュ織鏂囦欢
        for (const [source, paths] of Object.entries(this.LOG_SOURCES)) {
            for (const logPath of paths) {
                if (fs.existsSync(logPath)) {
                    await this.watchLogFile(logPath, source);
                    break; // 姣忎釜婧愬彧鐩戞帶绗竴涓壘鍒扮殑鏂囦欢
                }
            }
        }

        // 娣诲姞鑷畾涔夋棩蹇楄矾寰?
        const customLogPath = process.env.CUSTOM_LOG_PATH;
        if (customLogPath && fs.existsSync(customLogPath)) {
            await this.watchLogFile(customLogPath, 'custom');
        }

        logger.info(`Log collector started, watching ${this.watchers.size} log files`);
    }

    /**
     * 鐩戞帶鏃ュ織鏂囦欢
     */
    private async watchLogFile(logPath: string, source: string): Promise<void> {
        try {
            // 鑾峰彇鏂囦欢褰撳墠澶у皬锛堜粠鏈熬寮€濮嬭鍙栨柊鍐呭锛?
            const stats = fs.statSync(logPath);
            this.filePositions.set(logPath, stats.size);

            // 鐩戞帶鏂囦欢鍙樺寲
            const watcher = fs.watch(logPath, (eventType) => {
                if (eventType === 'change') {
                    this.readNewEntries(logPath, source);
                }
            });

            this.watchers.set(logPath, watcher);
            logger.info(`Watching log file: ${logPath} (${source})`);

        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`Failed to watch log file: ${logPath}`, { error: error.message });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 璇诲彇鏂扮殑鏃ュ織鏉＄洰
     */
    private readNewEntries(logPath: string, source: string): void {
        try {
            const stats = fs.statSync(logPath);
            const currentPosition = this.filePositions.get(logPath) || 0;

            // 妫€娴嬫棩蹇楄疆杞?
            if (stats.size < currentPosition) {
                this.filePositions.set(logPath, 0);
                return;
            }

            if (stats.size === currentPosition) {
                return; // 鏃犳柊鍐呭
            }

            // 璇诲彇鏂板唴瀹?
            const stream = fs.createReadStream(logPath, {
                start: currentPosition,
                end: stats.size
            });

            const rl = readline.createInterface({ input: stream });

            rl.on('line', (line) => {
                const entry = this.parseLogLine(line, source);
                if (entry) {
                    this.processEntry(entry);
                }
            });

            this.filePositions.set(logPath, stats.size);

        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error(`Failed to read log entries: ${logPath}`, { error: error.message });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鏃ュ織琛?
     */
    private parseLogLine(line: string, source: string): LogEntry | null {
        if (!line.trim()) return null;

        // 灏濊瘯瑙ｆ瀽鏃堕棿鎴?
        let timestamp = new Date();
        let level: LogEntry['level'] = 'info';
        let message = line;

        // Syslog 鏍煎紡: "Jan 24 10:30:45 hostname service: message"
        const syslogMatch = line.match(/^(\w{3}\s+\d{1,2}\s+\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})\s+(\S+)\s+(\S+?):\s*(.*)$/);
        if (syslogMatch) {
            const [, timeStr, , , msg] = syslogMatch;
            timestamp = new Date(`${new Date().getFullYear()} ${timeStr}`);
            message = msg;
        }

        // 妫€娴嬫棩蹇楃骇鍒?
        const lowerLine = line.toLowerCase();
        if (lowerLine.includes('error') || lowerLine.includes('fail')) {
            level = 'error';
        } else if (lowerLine.includes('warn')) {
            level = 'warn';
        } else if (lowerLine.includes('critical') || lowerLine.includes('fatal') || lowerLine.includes('panic')) {
            level = 'critical';
        } else if (lowerLine.includes('debug')) {
            level = 'debug';
        }

        return {
            source,
            timestamp,
            level,
            message,
            raw: line
        };
    }

    /**
     * 澶勭悊鏃ュ織鏉＄洰
     */
    private processEntry(entry: LogEntry): void {
        // 鍙戦€侀€氱敤鏃ュ織浜嬩欢
        this.emit('log', entry);

        // 妫€娴嬪嵄闄╂ā寮?
        for (const pattern of this.dangerousPatterns) {
            const match = entry.raw.match(pattern.pattern);
            if (match) {
                const alert = {
                    type: 'log_pattern',
                    pattern: pattern.name,
                    severity: pattern.severity,
                    description: pattern.description,
                    entry,
                    match: match[0],
                    timestamp: new Date()
                };

                logger.warn(`Dangerous pattern detected: ${pattern.name}`, {
                    source: entry.source,
                    message: entry.message.substring(0, 100)
                });

                this.emit('alert', alert);
            }
        }

        // 鍙戦€佹寜绾у埆鍒嗙被鐨勪簨浠?
        if (entry.level === 'error' || entry.level === 'critical') {
            this.emit('error', entry);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 娣诲姞鑷畾涔夋娴嬫ā寮?
     */
    addPattern(pattern: LogPattern): void {
        this.dangerousPatterns.push(pattern);
        logger.info(`Added log pattern: ${pattern.name}`);
    }

    /**
     * 鎵嬪姩娣诲姞鏃ュ織鏂囦欢鐩戞帶
     */
    async addLogFile(logPath: string, source: string): Promise<boolean> {
        if (!fs.existsSync(logPath)) {
            logger.warn(`Log file not found: ${logPath}`);
            return false;
        }

        if (this.watchers.has(logPath)) {
            logger.warn(`Already watching: ${logPath}`);
            return false;
        }

        await this.watchLogFile(logPath, source);
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鐩戞帶鐨勬枃浠跺垪琛?
     */
    getWatchedFiles(): string[] {
        return Array.from(this.watchers.keys());
    }

    /**
     * 鍋滄鏃ュ織閲囬泦
     */
    stop(): void {
        this.isRunning = false;

        for (const [path, watcher] of this.watchers) {
            watcher.close();
            logger.info(`Stopped watching: ${path}`);
        }

        this.watchers.clear();
        this.filePositions.clear();

        logger.info('Log collector stopped');
    }
}

// 瀵煎嚭鍗曚緥
export const logCollector = new LogCollector();
-- File: traffic-monitor.ts
/**
 * 娴侀噺鐩戞帶妯″潡
 * 
 * 鐩戞帶鏈嶅姟鍣ㄥ叆绔欐祦閲忥紝鍒嗘瀽璇锋眰妯″紡
 */

import { exec } from 'child_process';
import { promisify } from 'util';
import fs from 'fs';
import readline from 'readline';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

export interface TrafficEvent {
    timestamp: Date;
    sourceIP: string;
    destPort: number;
    method?: string;
    path?: string;
    userAgent?: string;
    requestBody?: string;
    rawLog?: string;
}

export type TrafficCallback = (event: TrafficEvent) => void;

export class TrafficMonitor {
    private isRunning = false;
    private callback: TrafficCallback | null = null;
    private logWatcher: fs.FSWatcher | null = null;
    private logStream: readline.Interface | null = null;
    private lastLogPosition = 0;

    // 甯歌鏃ュ織璺緞
    private readonly LOG_PATHS = {
        nginx: ['/var/log/nginx/access.log', '/var/log/nginx/error.log'],
        apache: ['/var/log/apache2/access.log', '/var/log/httpd/access_log'],
        custom: process.env.ACCESS_LOG_PATH
    };

    /**
     * 鍚姩娴侀噺鐩戞帶
     */
    async start(callback: TrafficCallback): Promise<void> {
        if (this.isRunning) {
            logger.warn('Traffic monitor already running');
            return;
        }

        this.callback = callback;
        this.isRunning = true;

        // 灏濊瘯鎵惧埌鍙敤鐨勬棩蹇楁枃浠?
        const logPath = await this.findAccessLog();

        if (logPath) {
            await this.watchLogFile(logPath);
        } else {
            logger.warn('No access log found, using netstat monitoring');
            await this.fallbackToNetstat();
        }

        logger.info('Traffic monitoring started');
    }

    /**
     * 鏌ユ壘鍙敤鐨勮闂棩蹇?
     */
    private async findAccessLog(): Promise<string | null> {
        // 浼樺厛浣跨敤鑷畾涔夎矾寰?
        if (this.LOG_PATHS.custom && fs.existsSync(this.LOG_PATHS.custom)) {
            return this.LOG_PATHS.custom;
        }

        // 妫€鏌?Nginx 鏃ュ織
        for (const path of this.LOG_PATHS.nginx) {
            if (fs.existsSync(path)) {
                logger.info(`Found Nginx log: ${path}`);
                return path;
            }
        }

        // 妫€鏌?Apache 鏃ュ織
        for (const path of this.LOG_PATHS.apache) {
            if (fs.existsSync(path)) {
                logger.info(`Found Apache log: ${path}`);
                return path;
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 鐩戞帶鏃ュ織鏂囦欢
     */
    private async watchLogFile(logPath: string): Promise<void> {
        logger.info(`Watching log file: ${logPath}`);

        // 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鏂囦欢浣嶇疆 (鍙鍙栨柊鍐呭)
        const stats = fs.statSync(logPath);
        this.lastLogPosition = stats.size;

        // 浣跨敤 fs.watch 鐩戞帶鏂囦欢鍙樺寲
        this.logWatcher = fs.watch(logPath, (eventType) => {
            if (eventType === 'change') {
                this.readNewLogEntries(logPath);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * 璇诲彇鏂扮殑鏃ュ織鏉＄洰
     */
    private readNewLogEntries(logPath: string): void {
        const stats = fs.statSync(logPath);

        if (stats.size < this.lastLogPosition) {
            // 鏃ュ織琚疆杞紝浠庡ご寮€濮?
            this.lastLogPosition = 0;
        }

        if (stats.size === this.lastLogPosition) {
            return; // 鏃犳柊鍐呭
        }

        const stream = fs.createReadStream(logPath, {
            start: this.lastLogPosition,
            end: stats.size
        });

        const rl = readline.createInterface({ input: stream });

        rl.on('line', (line) => {
            const event = this.parseLogLine(line);
            if (event && this.callback) {
                this.callback(event);
            }
        });

        this.lastLogPosition = stats.size;
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鏃ュ織琛?
     */
    private parseLogLine(line: string): TrafficEvent | null {
        // 灏濊瘯瑙ｆ瀽 Nginx/Apache Combined Log Format
        // 鏍煎紡: IP - - [timestamp] "METHOD PATH HTTP/1.1" status size "referer" "user-agent"
        const combinedLogRegex = /^(\S+) \S+ \S+ \[([^\]]+)\] "(\S+) (\S+) \S+" (\d+) \S+ "[^"]*" "([^"]*)"/;

        const match = line.match(combinedLogRegex);
        if (match) {
            return {
                timestamp: new Date(),
                sourceIP: match[1],
                destPort: 80, // 浠庢棩蹇楁棤娉曠洿鎺ヨ幏鍙栫鍙?
                method: match[3],
                path: match[4],
                userAgent: match[6],
                rawLog: line
            };
        }

        // 灏濊瘯瑙ｆ瀽鍏朵粬鏍煎紡
        // TODO: 鏀寔鏇村鏃ュ織鏍煎紡

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 澶囩敤鏂规锛氫娇鐢?netstat 鐩戞帶
     */
    private async fallbackToNetstat(): Promise<void> {
        // 瀹氭湡鎵ц netstat 鑾峰彇杩炴帴淇℃伅
        const checkConnections = async () => {
            if (!this.isRunning) return;

            try {
                const { stdout } = await execAsync('netstat -tn 2>/dev/null || ss -tn');
                const connections = this.parseNetstatOutput(stdout);

                for (const conn of connections) {
                    if (this.callback) {
                        this.callback(conn);
                    }
                }
            } catch (error) {
                // 闈欓粯澶勭悊閿欒
            }

            // 姣?5 绉掓鏌ヤ竴娆?
            if (this.isRunning) {
                setTimeout(checkConnections, 5000);
            }
        };

        checkConnections();
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽 netstat 杈撳嚭
     */
    private parseNetstatOutput(output: string): TrafficEvent[] {
        const events: TrafficEvent[] = [];
        const lines = output.split('\n');

        for (const line of lines) {
            // 鍖归厤 ESTABLISHED 杩炴帴
            const match = line.match(/(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+):(\d+)\s+(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+):(\d+)/);
            if (match) {
                events.push({
                    timestamp: new Date(),
                    sourceIP: match[1],
                    destPort: parseInt(match[4])
                });
            }
        }

        return events;
    }

    /**
     * 鍋滄鐩戞帶
     */
    async stop(): Promise<void> {
        this.isRunning = false;

        if (this.logWatcher) {
            this.logWatcher.close();
            this.logWatcher = null;
        }

        if (this.logStream) {
            this.logStream.close();
            this.logStream = null;
        }

        logger.info('Traffic monitoring stopped');
    }
}

import { Request, Response } from 'express';

export class AdminController {
    // 妯℃嫙鐢ㄦ埛閿欒鏁版嵁
    static getErrors(req: Request, res: Response) {
        const errors = [
            { userId: 'user_001', errorCode: '10001', message: 'EACCES: permission denied', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() },
            { userId: 'user_002', errorCode: '11003', message: 'ENOENT: no such file or directory', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() },
            { userId: 'user_003', errorCode: '10001', message: 'EACCES: permission denied', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() },
        ];
        res.json(errors);
    }

    // 妯℃嫙绯荤粺鎬ц兘鏁版嵁
    static getPerformance(req: Request, res: Response) {
        const performance = {
            server: {
                cpu: Math.random() * 30 + 10,
                memory: Math.random() * 40 + 30,
            },
            clients: {
                total_agents: Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 10,
                avg_cpu_overhead: (Math.random() * 2 + 0.5).toFixed(2),
                avg_latency_added: `${(Math.random() * 5 + 1).toFixed(1)}ms`,
            },
        };
        res.json(performance);
    }

    // 鎺ユ敹鏃ュ織鐨勭鐐?
    static submitLogs(req: Request, res: Response) {
        try {
            const { data } = req.body;

            if (!data) {
                return res.status(400).json({ error: '缂哄皯鏁版嵁' });
            }

            console.log('[ADMIN] 鏀跺埌鏃ュ織:', data);

            res.json({ success: true, message: '鏃ュ織宸叉帴鏀? });
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('[ADMIN] 澶勭悊澶辫触:', error);
            res.status(400).json({ error: '澶勭悊澶辫触' });
        }
    }
}

/**

import { Router, Request, Response } from 'express';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

const router = Router();

// Agent Gateway 寮曠敤 (灏嗙敱 index.ts 娉ㄥ叆)
let agentGateway: any = null;

export function setAgentGateway(gateway: any): void {
    agentGateway = gateway;
}

/**
 * GET /api/agents
 * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈夊凡杩炴帴鐨?Agent 鍒楄〃
 */
router.get('/', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        if (!agentGateway) {
            return res.status(503).json({ error: 'Agent gateway not available' });
        }

        const agents = agentGateway.getAgentList();
        res.json({
            success: true,
            count: agents.length,
            agents
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to get agents', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * GET /api/agents/:id
 * 鑾峰彇鎸囧畾 Agent 璇︽儏
 */
router.get('/:id', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        if (!agentGateway) {
            return res.status(503).json({ error: 'Agent gateway not available' });
        }

        const agent = agentGateway.getAgent(req.params.id);
        if (!agent) {
            return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Agent not found' });
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            agent: {
                agentId: agent.agentId,
                registration: agent.registration,
                lastHeartbeat: agent.lastHeartbeat,
                status: agent.status,
                isOnline: true
            }
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to get agent', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * POST /api/agents/:id/scan
 * 瑙﹀彂鎸囧畾 Agent 杩愯瀹夊叏鎵弿
 */
router.post('/:id/scan', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        if (!agentGateway) {
            return res.status(503).json({ error: 'Agent gateway not available' });
        }

        const success = agentGateway.sendCommandToAgent(req.params.id, { type: 'scan' });

        if (!success) {
            return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Agent not found or offline' });
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: 'Scan command sent'
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to trigger scan', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

router.post('/:id/block', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        if (!agentGateway) {
            return res.status(503).json({ error: 'Agent gateway not available' });
        }

        const { ip, duration } = req.body;

        if (!ip) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: 'IP address required' });
        }

        const success = agentGateway.sendCommandToAgent(req.params.id, {
            type: 'block_ip',
            payload: { ip, duration: duration || 3600 }
        });

        if (!success) {
            return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Agent not found or offline' });
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: `Block command sent for IP: ${ip}`
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to block IP', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

    }

/**
 * FluxGuard - AI 妯″瀷绔偣閰嶇疆 API
 * 
 * 涓?ESP32 绂荤嚎妯″紡鎻愪緵澶氭ā鍨嬬鐐归厤缃?
 * 浼樺厛绾? 鐢ㄦ埛鑷畾涔夋湇鍔″櫒 > 绗旇鏈湰鍦版ā鍨?
 */

import { Router, Request, Response } from 'express';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

const router = Router();

// AI 绔偣閰嶇疆缁撴瀯
interface AIEndpoint {
    id: string;
    name: string;           // 鏄剧ず鍚嶇О
    url: string;            // API 绔偣 URL
    modelName: string;      // 妯″瀷鍚嶇О
    apiKey: string;         // API 瀵嗛挜 (鍔犲瘑瀛樺偍)
    enabled: boolean;       // 鏄惁鍚敤
    priority: number;       // 浼樺厛绾?(鏁板瓧瓒婂皬瓒婁紭鍏?
    timeout: number;        // 瓒呮椂鏃堕棿 (ms)
    type: 'ollama' | 'openai' | 'custom'; // 绔偣绫诲瀷
    lastTest?: {
        success: boolean;
        latency: number;
        timestamp: string;
    };
}

// 瀛樺偍 (鍐呭瓨 + 鍚庣画鍙寔涔呭寲)
const endpoints = new Map<string, AIEndpoint>();

// 鍒濆鍖栭粯璁ょ鐐?
function initDefaults() {
    // 榛樿绗旇鏈鐐?
    endpoints.set('default-laptop', {
        id: 'default-laptop',
        name: '鏈湴绗旇鏈?(Ollama)',
        url: 'http://192.168.4.1:5000/api/analyze',
        modelName: 'llama3',
        apiKey: '',
        enabled: true,
        priority: 100, // 鏈€浣庝紭鍏堢骇 (浣滀负鍏滃簳)
        timeout: 10000,
        type: 'ollama'
    });
}

initDefaults();

/**
 * GET /api/ai-endpoints
 * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈?AI 绔偣閰嶇疆
 */
router.get('/', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const sortedEndpoints = Array.from(endpoints.values())
            .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority)
            .map(ep => ({
                ...ep,
                apiKey: ep.apiKey ? '******' : '', // 闅愯棌瀵嗛挜
            }));

        res.json({
            success: true,
            endpoints: sortedEndpoints,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to get AI endpoints', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * POST /api/ai-endpoints
 * 娣诲姞鏂扮殑 AI 绔偣
 */
router.post('/', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { name, url, modelName, apiKey, type, timeout, priority } = req.body;

        if (!url) {
            return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'URL is required' });
        }

        const id = `custom-${Date.now()}`;

        const endpoint: AIEndpoint = {
            id,
            name: name || `Custom Endpoint ${endpoints.size}`,
            url,
            modelName: modelName || 'default',
            apiKey: apiKey || '',
            enabled: true,
            priority: priority ?? 10,
            timeout: timeout || 15000,
            type: type || 'custom',
        };

        endpoints.set(id, endpoint);

        logger.info('AI endpoint added', { id, url });

        res.json({
            success: true,
            endpoint: {
                ...endpoint,
                apiKey: endpoint.apiKey ? '******' : '',
            },
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to add AI endpoint', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * PUT /api/ai-endpoints/:id
 * 鏇存柊 AI 绔偣
 */
router.put('/:id', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;
        const endpoint = endpoints.get(id);

        if (!endpoint) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Endpoint not found' });
        }

        // 鏇存柊瀛楁
        if (req.body.name) endpoint.name = req.body.name;
        if (req.body.url) endpoint.url = req.body.url;
        if (req.body.modelName) endpoint.modelName = req.body.modelName;
        if (req.body.apiKey) endpoint.apiKey = req.body.apiKey;
        if (req.body.enabled !== undefined) endpoint.enabled = req.body.enabled;
        if (req.body.priority !== undefined) endpoint.priority = req.body.priority;
        if (req.body.timeout) endpoint.timeout = req.body.timeout;

        endpoints.set(id, endpoint);

        logger.info('AI endpoint updated', { id });

        res.json({
            success: true,
            endpoint: {
                ...endpoint,
                apiKey: endpoint.apiKey ? '******' : '',
            },
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to update AI endpoint', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * DELETE /api/ai-endpoints/:id
 * 鍒犻櫎 AI 绔偣
 */
router.delete('/:id', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;

        // 涓嶅厑璁稿垹闄ら粯璁ょ鐐?
        if (id === 'default-laptop') {
            return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Cannot delete default endpoint' });
        }

        if (!endpoints.has(id)) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Endpoint not found' });
        }

        endpoints.delete(id);

        logger.info('AI endpoint deleted', { id });

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: `Endpoint ${id} deleted`,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to delete AI endpoint', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * POST /api/ai-endpoints/:id/test
 * 娴嬭瘯 AI 绔偣杩為€氭€?
 */
router.post('/:id/test', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;
        const endpoint = endpoints.get(id);

        if (!endpoint) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Endpoint not found' });
        }

        const startTime = Date.now();

        try {
            // 鍙戦€佹祴璇曡姹?
            const controller = new AbortController();
            const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), endpoint.timeout);

            const response = await fetch(endpoint.url, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    ...(endpoint.apiKey && { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${endpoint.apiKey}` }),
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    model: endpoint.modelName,
                    prompt: 'test connection',
                    stream: false,
                }),
                signal: controller.signal,
            });

            clearTimeout(timeoutId);

            const latency = Date.now() - startTime;
            const success = response.ok;

            // 鏇存柊娴嬭瘯缁撴灉
            endpoint.lastTest = {
                success,
                latency,
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            };
            endpoints.set(id, endpoint);

            res.json({
                success,
                latency,
                status: response.status,
            });
        } catch (fetchError: any) {
            const latency = Date.now() - startTime;

            endpoint.lastTest = {
                success: false,
                latency,
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            };
            endpoints.set(id, endpoint);

            res.json({
                success: false,
                latency,
                error: fetchError.message,
            });
        }
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to test AI endpoint', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

 * GET /api/ai-endpoints/sync-config

router.get('/sync-config', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        // 鍙繑鍥炲惎鐢ㄧ殑绔偣锛屾寜浼樺厛绾ф帓搴?
        const enabledEndpoints = Array.from(endpoints.values())
            .filter(ep => ep.enabled)
            .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority)
            .map(ep => ({
                url: ep.url,
                model: ep.modelName,
                key: ep.apiKey,
            }));

        res.json({
            success: true,
            endpoints: enabledEndpoints,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to get sync config', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

    return Array.from(endpoints.values())
        .filter(ep => ep.enabled)
        .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);
}

-- File: auth.routes.ts
/**
 * Authentication Routes
 * 
 * Handles user registration, login, and token management
 */

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import {
    AuthenticatedRequest,
    generateTokens,
    verifyRefreshToken,
    authenticate
} from '../../middleware/auth.middleware';
import { getDatabase } from '../../config/database';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

const router = Router();

// Password validation
const PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH = 8;
const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

/**
 * POST /api/auth/register
 * Register a new user
 */
router.post('/register', async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { email, password, username } = req.body;

        // Validate input
        if (!email || !password || !username) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                error: 'Email, password, and username are required',
                code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR'
            });
        }

        if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(email)) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                error: 'Invalid email format',
                code: 'INVALID_EMAIL'
            });
        }

        if (password.length < PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                error: `Password must be at least ${PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH} characters`,
                code: 'PASSWORD_TOO_SHORT'
            });
        }

        const db = getDatabase();

        // Check if user already exists
        const existingUser = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?').get(email);
        if (existingUser) {
            return res.status(409).json({
                error: 'Email already registered',
                code: 'USER_EXISTS'
            });
        }

        // Hash password
        const saltRounds = 12;
        const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, saltRounds);

        // Create user
        const userId = uuidv4();
        const tenantId = uuidv4(); // Each user gets their own tenant by default
        const role = 'tenant'; // Default role

        db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO users (id, email, username, password_hash, role, tenant_id, created_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
    `).run(userId, email, username, passwordHash, role, tenantId);

        // Generate tokens
        const tokens = generateTokens({
            userId,
            email,
            username,
            role,
            tenantId
        });

        logger.info(`New user registered: ${email}`);

        res.status(201).json({
            message: 'User registered successfully',
            user: {
                userId,
                email,
                username,
                role,
                tenantId
            },
            ...tokens
        });

    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Registration error', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({
            error: 'Registration failed',
            code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR'
        });
    }
});

/**
 * POST /api/auth/login
 * Authenticate user and return tokens
 */
router.post('/login', async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { email, password } = req.body;

        if (!email || !password) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                error: 'Email and password are required',
                code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR'
            });
        }

        const db = getDatabase();

        // Find user
        const user = db.prepare(`
      SELECT id, email, username, password_hash, role, tenant_id, status
      FROM users WHERE email = ?
    `).get(email) as any;

        if (!user) {
            return res.status(401).json({
                error: 'Invalid credentials',
                code: 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS'
            });
        }

        // Check if account is active
        if (user.status === 'suspended') {
            return res.status(403).json({
                error: 'Account suspended',
                code: 'ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED'
            });
        }

        // Verify password
        const passwordValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password_hash);
        if (!passwordValid) {
            logger.warn(`Failed login attempt for: ${email}`);
            return res.status(401).json({
                error: 'Invalid credentials',
                code: 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS'
            });
        }

        // Update last login
        db.prepare(`UPDATE users SET last_login = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?`).run(user.id);

        // Generate tokens
        const tokens = generateTokens({
            userId: user.id,
            email: user.email,
            username: user.username,
            role: user.role,
            tenantId: user.tenant_id
        });

        logger.info(`User logged in: ${email}`);

        res.json({
            message: 'Login successful',
            user: {
                userId: user.id,
                email: user.email,
                username: user.username,
                role: user.role,
                tenantId: user.tenant_id
            },
            ...tokens
        });

    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Login error', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({
            error: 'Login failed',
            code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR'
        });
    }
});

/**
 * POST /api/auth/refresh
 * Refresh access token using refresh token
 */
router.post('/refresh', async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { refreshToken } = req.body;

        if (!refreshToken) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                error: 'Refresh token required',
                code: 'TOKEN_MISSING'
            });
        }

        const decoded = verifyRefreshToken(refreshToken);
        if (!decoded) {
            return res.status(401).json({
                error: 'Invalid or expired refresh token',
                code: 'TOKEN_INVALID'
            });
        }

        const db = getDatabase();

        // Get user info
        const user = db.prepare(`
      SELECT id, email, username, role, tenant_id, status
      FROM users WHERE id = ?
    `).get(decoded.userId) as any;

        if (!user || user.status === 'suspended') {
            return res.status(401).json({
                error: 'User not found or suspended',
                code: 'USER_INVALID'
            });
        }

        // Generate new tokens
        const tokens = generateTokens({
            userId: user.id,
            email: user.email,
            username: user.username,
            role: user.role,
            tenantId: user.tenant_id
        });

        res.json({
            message: 'Token refreshed',
            ...tokens
        });

    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Token refresh error', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({
            error: 'Token refresh failed',
            code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR'
        });
    }
});

/**
 * GET /api/auth/me
 * Get current user info (requires authentication)
 */
router.get('/me', authenticate, (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
    res.json({
        user: req.user
    });
});

/**
 * POST /api/auth/logout
 * Logout user (client should discard tokens)
 */
router.post('/logout', authenticate, (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
    // In a production system, you might want to:
    // 1. Add token to a blacklist in Redis
    // 2. Invalidate all refresh tokens for this user
    // For now, we just acknowledge the logout

    logger.info(`User logged out: ${req.user?.email}`);

    res.json({
        message: 'Logout successful'
    });
});

/**
 * PUT /api/auth/password
 * Change user password
 */
router.put('/password', authenticate, async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { currentPassword, newPassword } = req.body;

        if (!currentPassword || !newPassword) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                error: 'Current and new password required',
                code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR'
            });
        }

        if (newPassword.length < PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                error: `Password must be at least ${PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH} characters`,
                code: 'PASSWORD_TOO_SHORT'
            });
        }

        const db = getDatabase();

        // Get current password hash
        const user = db.prepare('SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE id = ?')
            .get(req.user!.userId) as any;

        if (!user) {
            return res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' });
        }

        // Verify current password
        const passwordValid = await bcrypt.compare(currentPassword, user.password_hash);
        if (!passwordValid) {
            return res.status(401).json({
                error: 'Current password is incorrect',
                code: 'INVALID_PASSWORD'
            });
        }

        // Hash new password
        const newPasswordHash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, 12);

        // Update password
        db.prepare('UPDATE users SET password_hash = ?, updated_at = datetime(\'now\') WHERE id = ?')
            .run(newPasswordHash, req.user!.userId);

        logger.info(`Password changed for user: ${req.user?.email}`);

        res.json({
            message: 'Password changed successfully'
        });

    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Password change error', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({
            error: 'Password change failed',
            code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR'
        });
    }
});

export default router;

-- File: devices.routes.ts
/**
 * FluxGuard - ESP32 璁惧绠＄悊 API
 * 
 * 鎻愪緵 ESP32 纭欢缃戝叧鐨?REST API 鎺ュ彛
 */

import { Router, Request, Response } from 'express';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

const router = Router();

// 璁惧瀛樺偍 (鍐呭瓨 + 鍚庣画鍙墿灞曞埌鏁版嵁搴?
interface DeviceInfo {
    deviceId: string;
    version: string;
    chipModel: string;
    flashSize: number;
    freeHeap: number;
    sdkVersion: string;
    mode: 'online' | 'offline';
    isOnline: boolean;
    lastHeartbeat: Date;
    connectedAgents: string[];
    status?: {
        cpuUsage: number;
        memoryUsage: number;
        uptime: number;
        wifiRssi: number;
    };
    systemInfo?: {
        hostname: string;
        os: string;
        kernel: string;
        arch: string;
    };
}

const devices = new Map<string, DeviceInfo>();

// 瀵嗙爜鏈?
const codebook: Record<string, string> = {
    'E001': 'Connection refused',
    'E002': 'Permission denied',
    'E003': 'Out of memory',
    'E004': 'Disk full',
    'E005': 'Service unavailable',
    'S001': 'CPU usage critical (>90%)',
    'S002': 'Memory usage critical (>85%)',
    'S003': 'Disk usage critical (>90%)',
    'A001': 'SQL Injection detected',
    'A002': 'XSS attempt blocked',
    'A003': 'Path traversal attempt',
    'A004': 'Command injection detected',
    'A005': 'Brute force attack',
    'A006': 'DDoS pattern detected',
};

/**
 * GET /api/devices
 * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈?ESP32 璁惧鍒楄〃
 */
router.get('/', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const deviceList = Array.from(devices.values()).map(device => ({
            ...device,
            lastHeartbeat: device.lastHeartbeat.toISOString(),
        }));

        res.json({
            success: true,
            total: deviceList.length,
            online: deviceList.filter(d => d.isOnline).length,
            devices: deviceList,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to get devices', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * GET /api/devices/:id
 * 鑾峰彇鍗曚釜璁惧璇︽儏
 */
router.get('/:id', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const device = devices.get(req.params.id);

        if (!device) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Device not found' });
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            device: {
                ...device,
                lastHeartbeat: device.lastHeartbeat.toISOString(),
            },
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to get device', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * POST /api/devices/:id/cmd
 * 鍙戦€佸懡浠ゅ埌璁惧
 */
router.post('/:id/cmd', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;
        const { command, params } = req.body;

        const device = devices.get(id);

        if (!device) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Device not found' });
        }

        if (!device.isOnline) {
            return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Device is offline' });
        }

        // 璁板綍鍛戒护鏃ュ織
        logger.info('Command sent to device', {
            deviceId: id,
            command,
            params
        });

        // 瀹為檯鍙戦€佸懡浠ら渶瑕侀€氳繃 WebSocket Gateway
        // 杩欓噷杩斿洖鎴愬姛锛屽疄闄呭彂閫佸湪 device-gateway.ts 涓鐞?
        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: `Command "${command}" queued for device ${id}`,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to send command', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * PUT /api/devices/:id
 * 鏇存柊璁惧閰嶇疆
 */
router.put('/:id', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;
        const device = devices.get(id);

        if (!device) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Device not found' });
        }

        // 鏇存柊鍙厤缃瓧娈?
        if (req.body.connectedAgents) {
            device.connectedAgents = req.body.connectedAgents;
        }

        devices.set(id, device);

        logger.info('Device updated', { deviceId: id });

        res.json({
            success: true,
            device: {
                ...device,
                lastHeartbeat: device.lastHeartbeat.toISOString(),
            },
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to update device', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * DELETE /api/devices/:id
 * 娉ㄩ攢璁惧
 */
router.delete('/:id', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { id } = req.params;

        if (!devices.has(id)) {
            return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Device not found' });
        }

        devices.delete(id);

        logger.info('Device unregistered', { deviceId: id });

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: `Device ${id} unregistered`,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to delete device', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

/**
 * GET /api/devices/codebook
 * 鑾峰彇瀵嗙爜鏈?
 */
router.get('/codebook', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    res.json({
        success: true,
        codebook,
    });
});

/**
 * POST /api/devices/codebook
 * 鏇存柊瀵嗙爜鏈?
 */
router.post('/codebook', (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
        const { entries } = req.body;

        if (!entries || typeof entries !== 'object') {
            return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Invalid entries' });
        }

        Object.assign(codebook, entries);

        logger.info('Codebook updated', { entriesAdded: Object.keys(entries).length });

        res.json({
            success: true,
            totalEntries: Object.keys(codebook).length,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Failed to update codebook', { error: error.message });
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Internal server error' });
    }
});

// 瀵煎嚭璁惧绠＄悊鍑芥暟渚?WebSocket Gateway 浣跨敤
export function registerDevice(info: Omit<DeviceInfo, 'isOnline' | 'lastHeartbeat' | 'connectedAgents'>): void {
    const device: DeviceInfo = {
        ...info,
        isOnline: true,
        lastHeartbeat: new Date(),
        connectedAgents: [],
    };
    devices.set(info.deviceId, device);
    logger.info('Device registered', { deviceId: info.deviceId });
}

export function updateDeviceStatus(deviceId: string, status: DeviceInfo['status']): void {
    const device = devices.get(deviceId);
    if (device) {
        device.status = status;
        device.lastHeartbeat = new Date();
        devices.set(deviceId, device);
    }
}

export function setDeviceOffline(deviceId: string): void {
    const device = devices.get(deviceId);
    if (device) {
        device.isOnline = false;
        devices.set(deviceId, device);
        logger.info('Device went offline', { deviceId });
    }
}

export function getDeviceList(): DeviceInfo[] {
    return Array.from(devices.values());
}

export function getCodebook(): Record<string, string> {
    return codebook;
}

-- File: scanner.ts
import { Router, Request, Response } from 'express';
import { OptimizationEngine } from '../../scanner/optimization-engine';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

const router = Router();
const optimizationEngine = new OptimizationEngine();

/**
 * POST /api/scanner/run
 * Run server scan
 */
router.post('/run', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const options = req.body.options || {};

    logger.info('Starting server scan...');

    const result = await optimizationEngine.runFullScan(options);

    // Save scan result to database
    const db = require('../../config/database').getDatabase();
    const stmt = db.getDb().prepare(`
      INSERT INTO scan_results (siteId, scanType, result, recommendations, status)
      VALUES (NULL, 'all', ?, ?, 'completed')
    `);

    const resultId = stmt.run(
      JSON.stringify(result.systemInfo),
      JSON.stringify(result.recommendations)
    ).lastInsertRowid;

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        id: resultId,
        ...result
      }
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Scan failed', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * POST /api/scanner/apply
 * Apply an optimization
 */
router.post('/apply', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { recommendation } = req.body;

    if (!recommendation) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'recommendation is required' });
    }

    logger.info(`Applying optimization: ${recommendation.title}`);

    const result = await optimizationEngine.applyOptimization(recommendation);

    res.json(result);
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to apply optimization', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * GET /api/scanner/results/:id
 * Get scan result by ID
 */
router.get('/results/:id', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const db = require('../../config/database').getDatabase();
    const result = db.getDb().prepare('SELECT * FROM scan_results WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);

    if (!result) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Scan result not found' });
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        ...result,
        result: JSON.parse(result.result),
        recommendations: JSON.parse(result.recommendations)
      }
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to get scan result', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * GET /api/scanner/report/:id
 * Generate scan report
 */
router.get('/report/:id', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const db = require('../../config/database').getDatabase();
    const result = db.getDb().prepare('SELECT * FROM scan_results WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);

    if (!result) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Scan result not found' });
    }

    const systemInfo = JSON.parse(result.result);
    const recommendations = JSON.parse(result.recommendations);

    const report = optimizationEngine.generateReport(systemInfo, recommendations);

    res.set('Content-Type', 'text/plain');
    res.send(report);
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to generate report', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});
