-- File: sites.ts
import { Router, Request, Response } from 'express';
import { getDatabase } from '../../config/database';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

const router = Router();

/**
 * GET /api/sites
 * Get all sites
 */
router.get('/', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const db = getDatabase().getDb();
    const sites = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM sites ORDER BY createdAt DESC').all();

    // Convert integer booleans
    const formattedSites = sites.map((site: any) => ({
      ...site,
      sslEnabled: Boolean(site.sslEnabled),
      antiBotEnabled: Boolean(site.antiBotEnabled),
      rateLimitEnabled: Boolean(site.rateLimitEnabled),
      concurrencyEnabled: Boolean(site.concurrencyEnabled)
    }));

    res.json({ success: true, data: formattedSites });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to get sites', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * GET /api/sites/:id
 * Get site by ID
 */
router.get('/:id', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const db = getDatabase().getDb();
    const site = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM sites WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);

    if (!site) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Site not found' });
    }

    res.json({ success: true, data: site });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to get site', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * POST /api/sites
 * Create new site
 */
router.post('/', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { domain, target, sslEnabled = true, security } = req.body;

    if (!domain || !target) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'domain and target are required' });
    }

    const db = getDatabase().getDb();

    const stmt = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO sites (
        domain, target, sslEnabled,
        antiBotEnabled, rateLimitEnabled, concurrencyEnabled,
        rateLimitRequests, concurrencyMax
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `);

    const result = stmt.run(
      domain,
      target,
      sslEnabled ? 1 : 0,
      security?.antiBot ? 1 : 0,
      security?.rateLimit?.enabled ? 1 : 0,
      security?.concurrency?.enabled ? 1 : 0,
      security?.rateLimit?.requestsPerMinute || 60,
      security?.concurrency?.maxConcurrent || 5
    );

    logger.info(`Site created: ${domain} -> ${target}`);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: { id: result.lastInsertRowid, domain, target }
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to create site', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * PUT /api/sites/:id
 * Update site
 */
router.put('/:id', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { domain, target, sslEnabled, security } = req.body;

    const db = getDatabase().getDb();

    const stmt = db.prepare(`
      UPDATE sites SET
        domain = COALESCE(?, domain),
        target = COALESCE(?, target),
        sslEnabled = COALESCE(?, sslEnabled),
        antiBotEnabled = COALESCE(?, antiBotEnabled),
        rateLimitEnabled = COALESCE(?, rateLimitEnabled),
        concurrencyEnabled = COALESCE(?, concurrencyEnabled),
        rateLimitRequests = COALESCE(?, rateLimitRequests),
        concurrencyMax = COALESCE(?, concurrencyMax),
        updatedAt = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
      WHERE id = ?
    `);

    stmt.run(
      domain,
      target,
      sslEnabled !== undefined ? (sslEnabled ? 1 : 0) : null,
      security?.antiBot !== undefined ? (security.antiBot ? 1 : 0) : null,
      security?.rateLimit?.enabled !== undefined ? (security.rateLimit.enabled ? 1 : 0) : null,
      security?.concurrency?.enabled !== undefined ? (security.concurrency.enabled ? 1 : 0) : null,
      security?.rateLimit?.requestsPerMinute || null,
      security?.concurrency?.maxConcurrent || null,
      req.params.id
    );

    logger.info(`Site updated: ${req.params.id}`);

    res.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to update site', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * DELETE /api/sites/:id
 * Delete site
 */
router.delete('/:id', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const db = getDatabase().getDb();
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM sites WHERE id = ?').run(req.params.id);

    logger.info(`Site deleted: ${req.params.id}`);

    res.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Failed to delete site', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

-- File: smart-fix.ts
import { Router, Request, Response } from 'express';
import { SmartFixer } from '../../scanner/smart-fixer';
import { OptimizationEngine } from '../../scanner/optimization-engine';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

const router = Router();
const optimizationEngine = new OptimizationEngine();

/**
 * POST /api/scanner/smart-fix
 * Fix a problem using AI
 */
router.post('/smart-fix', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { recommendation, context, options } = req.body;

    if (!recommendation) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'recommendation is required' });
    }

    logger.info(`Starting smart fix for: ${recommendation.title}`);

    // Create smart fixer with options
    const smartFixer = new SmartFixer({
      enableAI: options?.enableAI !== false,
      aiProvider: options?.aiProvider || {
        name: process.env.AI_PROVIDER || 'ollama',
        apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY || process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
        baseURL: process.env.AI_BASE_URL,
        model: process.env.AI_MODEL || 'llama2'
      },
      maxAttempts: options?.maxAttempts || 3,
      executionTimeout: options?.executionTimeout || 60000,
      enableRealTimeMonitoring: options?.enableRealTimeMonitoring !== false
    });

    // Run smart fix
    const result = await smartFixer.fixProblem(recommendation, context);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: result
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Smart fix failed', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * POST /api/scanner/batch-fix
 * Fix multiple problems in batch
 */
router.post('/batch-fix', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { recommendations, context, options } = req.body;

    if (!recommendations || !Array.isArray(recommendations)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'recommendations array is required' });
    }

    logger.info(`Batch fixing ${recommendations.length} problems`);

    const smartFixer = new SmartFixer(options);

    const results = await smartFixer.batchFix(recommendations, context);

    const summary = {
      total: results.length,
      successful: results.filter(r => r.success).length,
      failed: results.filter(r => !r.success).length,
      totalAttempts: results.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.attempts.length, 0),
      totalTime: results.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.totalTime, 0)
    };

    logger.info('Batch fix complete', { summary });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        results,
        summary
      }
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Batch fix failed', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * POST /api/scanner/dry-fix
 * Simulate fixing without executing
 */
router.post('/dry-fix', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { recommendation, context, options } = req.body;

    if (!recommendation) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'recommendation is required' });
    }

    logger.info(`Dry run for: ${recommendation.title}`);

    const smartFixer = new SmartFixer(options);

    const dryRunResult = await smartFixer.dryFix(recommendation, context);

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: dryRunResult
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('Dry fix failed', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * GET /api/scanner/ai-status
 * Check AI provider status
 */
router.get('/ai-status', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const provider = {
      name: process.env.AI_PROVIDER || 'ollama',
      model: process.env.AI_MODEL || 'llama2',
      baseURL: process.env.AI_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:11434',
      configured: !!(process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY || process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY || process.env.AI_BASE_URL)
    };

    // Test connection if configured
    let status = 'not_configured';
    let error = null;

    if (provider.configured) {
      try {
        if (provider.name === 'ollama') {
          // Test Ollama connection
          const axios = require('axios');
          await axios.get(`${provider.baseURL}/api/tags`, { timeout: 5000 });
          status = 'available';
        } else if (provider.name === 'openai' || provider.name === 'anthropic') {
          // Just check if API key is present
          status = 'available';
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        status = 'unavailable';
        error = err.message;
      }
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        provider,
        status,
        error
      }
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('AI status check failed', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

/**
 * POST /api/scanner/ai-analyze
 * Analyze a problem using AI
 */
router.post('/ai-analyze', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { problem, context } = req.body;

    if (!problem) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'problem is required' });
    }

    const { AIEngine } = await import('../../scanner/ai-engine');

    const aiEngine = new AIEngine({
      name: (process.env.AI_PROVIDER || 'ollama') as any,
      apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY || process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
      baseURL: process.env.AI_BASE_URL,
      model: process.env.AI_MODEL || 'llama2'
    });

    const analysis = await aiEngine.analyzeProblem({
      problem,
      context: {
        systemInfo: context?.systemInfo || {},
        logs: context?.logs || [],
        configFiles: context?.configFiles || {}
      }
    });

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: analysis
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    logger.error('AI analysis failed', { error });
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
  }
});

export default router;

-- File: database.ts
import initSqlJs, { Database as SqlJsDatabase } from 'sql.js';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

/**
 * Database Manager
 * Handles SQLite database operations using sql.js (pure JavaScript)
 */
export class DatabaseManager {
  private db: SqlJsDatabase | null = null;
  private dbPath: string;
  private saveInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

  constructor() {
    const dataDir = process.env.DATA_DIR || path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
    this.dbPath = path.join(dataDir, 'newsecurity.db');
    this.ensureDataDir(dataDir);
  }

  /**
   * Initialize database (async because sql.js needs to load WASM)
   */
  public async init(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const SQL = await initSqlJs();

      // Try to load existing database
      if (fs.existsSync(this.dbPath)) {
        const fileBuffer = fs.readFileSync(this.dbPath);
        this.db = new SQL.Database(fileBuffer);
        logger.info(`Loaded existing database: ${this.dbPath}`);
      } else {
        this.db = new SQL.Database();
        logger.info(`Created new database: ${this.dbPath}`);
      }

      this.initSchema();
      this.startAutoSave();
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to initialize database', { error: error.message });
      throw error;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Ensure data directory exists
   */
  private ensureDataDir(dir: string): void {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      logger.info(`Created data directory: ${dir}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Initialize database schema
   */
  private initSchema(): void {
    if (!this.db) {
      throw new Error('Database not connected');
    }

    try {
      // Sites table
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sites (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          domain TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
          target TEXT NOT NULL,
          sslEnabled INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
          antiBotEnabled INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
          rateLimitEnabled INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
          rateLimitRequests INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
          concurrencyEnabled INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
          concurrencyMax INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
          createdAt DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          updatedAt DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
      `);

      // Security rules table
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS security_rules (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          siteId INTEGER NOT NULL,
          ruleType TEXT NOT NULL,
          config TEXT NOT NULL,
          enabled INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
          createdAt DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          FOREIGN KEY (siteId) REFERENCES sites(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
      `);

      // Scan results table
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scan_results (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          siteId INTEGER,
          scanType TEXT NOT NULL,
          result TEXT NOT NULL,
          recommendations TEXT,
          status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
          createdAt DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          FOREIGN KEY (siteId) REFERENCES sites(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
      `);

      // Backups table
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS backups (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          description TEXT,
          data TEXT NOT NULL,
          size INTEGER,
          isAutomatic INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
          createdAt DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
      `);

      // Monitoring data table (time-series)
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metrics (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          siteId INTEGER NOT NULL,
          metricType TEXT NOT NULL,
          value REAL NOT NULL,
          metadata TEXT,
          timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          FOREIGN KEY (siteId) REFERENCES sites(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
      `);

      // Agents table
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agents (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          hostname TEXT NOT NULL,
          platform TEXT,
          arch TEXT,
          version TEXT,
          lastHeartbeat DATETIME,
          status TEXT DEFAULT 'offline',
          createdAt DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
      `);

      // Users table (for authentication)
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
          username TEXT NOT NULL,
          password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
          role TEXT DEFAULT 'viewer' CHECK(role IN ('admin', 'tenant', 'viewer')),
          tenant_id TEXT,
          status TEXT DEFAULT 'active' CHECK(status IN ('active', 'suspended', 'pending')),
          last_login DATETIME,
          created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
      `);

      // API Keys table (for agent/device authentication)
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          key_hash TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
          user_id TEXT,
          tenant_id TEXT,
          permissions TEXT DEFAULT '["read"]',
          last_used DATETIME,
          expires_at DATETIME,
          created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
      `);

      // Devices table (for ESP32 dongles)
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS devices (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          device_type TEXT DEFAULT 'esp32',
          mac_address TEXT UNIQUE,
          firmware_version TEXT,
          status TEXT DEFAULT 'offline',
          last_seen DATETIME,
          config TEXT,
          created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
      `);

      // Alerts table (for security alerts)
      this.db.run(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS alerts (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          type TEXT NOT NULL,
          severity TEXT DEFAULT 'info' CHECK(severity IN ('info', 'warning', 'critical')),
          source TEXT,
          message TEXT NOT NULL,
          details TEXT,
          acknowledged INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
          acknowledged_by TEXT,
          acknowledged_at DATETIME,
          created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
      `);

      // Create indexes
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sites_domain ON sites(domain)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scan_results_siteId ON scan_results(siteId)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_metrics_siteId ON metrics(siteId)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_metrics_timestamp ON metrics(timestamp)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_email ON users(email)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_tenant ON users(tenant_id)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_api_keys_user ON api_keys(user_id)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_alerts_severity ON alerts(severity)`);
      this.db.run(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_alerts_created ON alerts(created_at)`);

      logger.info('Database schema initialized');
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to initialize database schema', { error: error.message });
      throw error;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Start auto-save interval
   */
  private startAutoSave(): void {
    // Save to disk every 30 seconds
    this.saveInterval = setInterval(() => {
      this.save();
    }, 30000);
  }

  /**
   * Save database to disk
   */
  public save(): void {
    if (!this.db) return;

    try {
      const data = this.db.export();
      const buffer = Buffer.from(data);
      fs.writeFileSync(this.dbPath, buffer);
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to save database', { error: error.message });
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get database instance
   */
  public getDb(): SqlJsDatabase {
    if (!this.db) {
      throw new Error('Database not connected');
    }
    return this.db;
  }

  /**
   * Execute SQL and return results
   */
  public exec(sql: string): void {
    if (!this.db) throw new Error('Database not connected');
    this.db.run(sql);
  }

  /**
   * Prepare and run a statement
   */
  public prepare(sql: string): {
    run: (...params: any[]) => void;
    all: (...params: any[]) => any[];
    get: (...params: any[]) => any;
  } {
    if (!this.db) throw new Error('Database not connected');

    const db = this.db;

    return {
      run: (...params: any[]) => {
        db.run(sql, params);
      },
      all: (...params: any[]) => {
        const stmt = db.prepare(sql);
        stmt.bind(params);
        const results: any[] = [];
        while (stmt.step()) {
          results.push(stmt.getAsObject());
        }
        stmt.free();
        return results;
      },
      get: (...params: any[]) => {
        const stmt = db.prepare(sql);
        stmt.bind(params);
        const result = stmt.step() ? stmt.getAsObject() : null;
        stmt.free();
        return result;
      }
    };
  }

  /**
   * Close database connection
   */
  public close(): void {
    if (this.saveInterval) {
      clearInterval(this.saveInterval);
      this.saveInterval = null;
    }

    if (this.db) {
      this.save(); // Final save
      this.db.close();
      this.db = null;
      logger.info('Database connection closed');
    }
  }
}

// Singleton instance
let dbManager: DatabaseManager | null = null;
let initPromise: Promise<DatabaseManager> | null = null;

export async function initDatabase(): Promise<DatabaseManager> {
  if (dbManager) return dbManager;

  if (!initPromise) {
    initPromise = (async () => {
      dbManager = new DatabaseManager();
      await dbManager.init();
      return dbManager;
    })();
  }

  return initPromise;
}

export function getDatabase(): DatabaseManager {
  if (!dbManager) {
    throw new Error('Database not initialized. Call initDatabase() first.');
  }
  return dbManager;
}

export function closeDatabase(): void {
  if (dbManager) {
    dbManager.close();
    dbManager = null;
    initPromise = null;
  }
}

// Type Definitions for Security Middleware

export interface SecurityConfig {
    // Environment
    env: 'development' | 'production' | 'test';

    // Rate Limiting
    rateLimit: {
        requestsPerMinute: number;
        windowSeconds: number;
    };

    // Anti-Bot
    antiBot: {
        enabled: boolean;
        requestThreshold: number;
        fastRequestThreshold: number;
        blockDuration: number;
        allowKnownBots: boolean;
    };

    // Concurrency
    concurrency: {
        maxConcurrentRequestsPerUser: number;
        maxQueueSize: number;
    };

    // Auth Security
    authSecurity: {
        maxLoginAttempts: number;
        lockoutDuration: number;
        attemptWindow: number;
        passwordPolicy: PasswordPolicy;
    };

    // Monitoring
    monitoring: {
        enablePerformanceTracking: boolean;
        slowQueryThreshold: number;
        memoryWarningThreshold: number;
    };
}

export interface PasswordPolicy {
    minLength: number;
    requireUppercase: boolean;
    requireLowercase: boolean;
    requireNumbers: boolean;
    requireSpecialChars: boolean;
    maxLength?: number;
}

export interface LoginAttempt {
    email: string;
    ip: string;
    success: boolean;
    timestamp: number;
}

export interface RequestFingerprint {
    ip: string;
    userAgent: string;
    acceptLanguage: string;
    acceptEncoding: string;
}

export interface RateLimitInfo {
    limit: number;
    remaining: number;
    reset: number;
}

export interface SecurityMetrics {
    requestsPerMinute: number;
    blockedRequests: number;
    activeConnections: number;
    memoryUsage: number;
    cpuUsage: number;
}

  priority: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical';

  description: string;

  risk: string; // Potential risks
}

-- File: demo_data.ts
/**
 * Database Seed Script
 * 
 * Creates demo data for development and testing
 */

import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import { initDatabase, getDatabase, closeDatabase } from '../../config/database';
import logger from '../../utils/logger';

interface SeedOptions {
    clearExisting?: boolean;
    verbose?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Seed the database with demo data
 */
export async function seedDatabase(options: SeedOptions = {}): Promise<void> {
    const { clearExisting = false, verbose = true } = options;

    try {
        await initDatabase();
        const db = getDatabase();

        if (verbose) logger.info('Starting database seeding...');

        // Clear existing data if requested
        if (clearExisting) {
            if (verbose) logger.info('Clearing existing data...');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM alerts');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM api_keys');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM scan_results');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM metrics');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM security_rules');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM sites');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM agents');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM devices');
            db.exec('DELETE FROM users');
        }

        // Create admin user
        const adminId = uuidv4();
        const adminTenantId = uuidv4();
        const adminPasswordHash = await bcrypt.hash('admin123', 12);

        db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (id, email, username, password_hash, role, tenant_id, status)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(adminId, 'admin@fluxguard.local', 'Administrator', adminPasswordHash, 'admin', adminTenantId, 'active');

        if (verbose) logger.info('Created admin user: admin@fluxguard.local / admin123');

        // Create demo tenant user
        const tenantId = uuidv4();
        const tenantUserId = uuidv4();
        const tenantPasswordHash = await bcrypt.hash('demo123', 12);

        db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (id, email, username, password_hash, role, tenant_id, status)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(tenantUserId, 'demo@example.com', 'Demo User', tenantPasswordHash, 'tenant', tenantId, 'active');

        if (verbose) logger.info('Created demo user: demo@example.com / demo123');

        // Create demo sites
        const sites = [
            { domain: 'example.com', target: 'http://localhost:8080' },
            { domain: 'api.example.com', target: 'http://localhost:3000' },
            { domain: 'admin.example.com', target: 'http://localhost:4000' }
        ];

        for (const site of sites) {
            db.prepare(`
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sites (domain, target, sslEnabled, antiBotEnabled, rateLimitEnabled)
        VALUES (?, ?, 1, 1, 1)
      `).run(site.domain, site.target);
        }

        if (verbose) logger.info(`Created ${sites.length} demo sites`);

        // Create demo agents
        const agents = [
            { id: uuidv4(), hostname: 'web-server-01', platform: 'linux', arch: 'x64', version: '2.0.0', status: 'online' },
            { id: uuidv4(), hostname: 'api-server-01', platform: 'linux', arch: 'x64', version: '2.0.0', status: 'online' },
            { id: uuidv4(), hostname: 'db-server-01', platform: 'linux', arch: 'x64', version: '1.9.5', status: 'offline' }
        ];

        for (const agent of agents) {
            db.prepare(`
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO agents (id, hostname, platform, arch, version, status, lastHeartbeat)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
      `).run(agent.id, agent.hostname, agent.platform, agent.arch, agent.version, agent.status);
        }

        if (verbose) logger.info(`Created ${agents.length} demo agents`);

        // Create demo devices (ESP32)
        const devices = [
            { id: uuidv4(), mac: 'AA:BB:CC:DD:EE:01', firmware: '1.2.0', status: 'online' },
            { id: uuidv4(), mac: 'AA:BB:CC:DD:EE:02', firmware: '1.2.0', status: 'offline' }
        ];

        for (const device of devices) {
            db.prepare(`
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO devices (id, device_type, mac_address, firmware_version, status, last_seen)
        VALUES (?, 'esp32', ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
      `).run(device.id, device.mac, device.firmware, device.status);
        }

        if (verbose) logger.info(`Created ${devices.length} demo devices`);

        // Create demo alerts
        const alerts = [
            { type: 'intrusion_attempt', severity: 'critical', source: 'web-server-01', message: 'SQL injection attempt detected from 192.168.1.100' },
            { type: 'brute_force', severity: 'warning', source: 'api-server-01', message: 'Multiple failed login attempts from 10.0.0.5' },
            { type: 'port_scan', severity: 'info', source: 'web-server-01', message: 'Port scan activity detected from 172.16.0.50' },
            { type: 'vulnerability', severity: 'critical', source: 'db-server-01', message: 'CVE-2024-1234 vulnerability detected in OpenSSL' }
        ];

        for (const alert of alerts) {
            db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO alerts (type, severity, source, message)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
      `).run(alert.type, alert.severity, alert.source, alert.message);
        }

        if (verbose) logger.info(`Created ${alerts.length} demo alerts`);

        // Create demo API key
        const apiKeyId = uuidv4();
        const apiKey = 'fluxguard_' + uuidv4().replace(/-/g, '');
        const apiKeyHash = await bcrypt.hash(apiKey, 10);

        db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR IGNORE INTO api_keys (id, name, key_hash, user_id, tenant_id, permissions)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(apiKeyId, 'Demo API Key', apiKeyHash, adminId, adminTenantId, '["read", "write"]');

        if (verbose) {
            logger.info('Created demo API key');
            logger.info(`API Key (save this): ${apiKey}`);
        }

        // Save changes
        db.save();

        if (verbose) {
            logger.info('Database seeding completed successfully!');
            logger.info('---');
            logger.info('Demo Credentials:');
            logger.info('  Admin: admin@fluxguard.local / admin123');
            logger.info('  User:  demo@example.com / demo123');
        }

    } catch (error: any) {
        logger.error('Database seeding failed', { error: error.message });
        throw error;
    }
}

/**
 * Run seeder as standalone script
 */
async function main() {
    console.log('馃尡 FluxGuard Database Seeder\n');

    const clearExisting = process.argv.includes('--clear');

    if (clearExisting) {
        console.log('鈿狅笍  Warning: --clear flag detected, existing data will be removed!\n');
    }

    try {
        await seedDatabase({ clearExisting, verbose: true });
        console.log('\n鉁?Seeding completed!');
        process.exit(0);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('\n鉂?Seeding failed:', error);
        process.exit(1);
    } finally {
        closeDatabase();
    }
}

// Run if executed directly
if (require.main === module) {
    main();
}

export default seedDatabase;
-- File: init.ts

import { initDatabase, getDatabase } from '../config/database';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

async function seedDatabase() {
    try {
        await initDatabase();
        const db = getDatabase();

        const adminEmail = process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL || 'admin@fluxguard.com';
        const adminPassword = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || 'admin123';

        // Check if admin exists
        const existingAdmin = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = ?').get(adminEmail);

        if (existingAdmin) {
            logger.info('Admin user already exists');
            return;
        }

        logger.info('Creating default admin user...');

        const saltRounds = 12;
        const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(adminPassword, saltRounds);
        const userId = uuidv4();
        const tenantId = uuidv4();

        db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO users (id, email, username, password_hash, role, tenant_id, created_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
    `).run(userId, adminEmail, 'Admin', passwordHash, 'admin', tenantId);

        logger.info(`Admin user created: ${adminEmail}`);

    } catch (error) {
        logger.error('Failed to seed database', { error });
        process.exit(1);
    }
}

// Run if called directly
if (require.main === module) {
    seedDatabase().then(() => {
        logger.info('Seeding complete');
        process.exit(0);
    });
}

export { seedDatabase };
-- File: auth.middleware.ts
/**
 * JWT Authentication Middleware
 * 
 * Provides secure authentication for FluxGuard API endpoints
 */

import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

// JWT Configuration
const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'fluxguard-dev-secret-change-in-production';
const JWT_EXPIRES_IN = process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN || '24h';
const JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN = process.env.JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN || '7d';

/**
 * Authenticated user payload stored in JWT
 */
export interface AuthenticatedUser {
    userId: string;
    email: string;
    username: string;
    role: 'admin' | 'tenant' | 'viewer';
    tenantId?: string;
}

/**
 * Extended Express Request with user info
 */
export interface AuthenticatedRequest extends Request {
    user?: AuthenticatedUser;
}

/**
 * Token pair for authentication
 */
export interface TokenPair {
    accessToken: string;
    refreshToken: string;
    expiresIn: number;
}

/**
 * Generate JWT token pair
 */
export function generateTokens(user: AuthenticatedUser): TokenPair {
    const accessToken = jwt.sign(user, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: JWT_EXPIRES_IN } as jwt.SignOptions);
    const refreshToken = jwt.sign(
        { userId: user.userId, type: 'refresh' },
        JWT_SECRET,
        { expiresIn: JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN } as jwt.SignOptions
    );

    // Calculate expiration in seconds
    const decoded = jwt.decode(accessToken) as jwt.JwtPayload;
    const expiresIn = (decoded?.exp || 0) - Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    return { accessToken, refreshToken, expiresIn };
}

/**
 * Verify and decode JWT token
 */
export function verifyToken(token: string): AuthenticatedUser | null {
    try {
        const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as AuthenticatedUser;
        return decoded;
    } catch (error) {
        return null;
    }
}

/**
 * Verify refresh token
 */
export function verifyRefreshToken(token: string): { userId: string } | null {
    try {
        const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as { userId: string; type: string };
        if (decoded.type !== 'refresh') {
            return null;
        }
        return { userId: decoded.userId };
    } catch (error) {
        return null;
    }
}

/**
 * Authentication middleware - requires valid JWT token
 */
export function authenticate(req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;

    if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        res.status(401).json({
            error: 'Authentication required',
            code: 'AUTH_TOKEN_MISSING'
        });
        return;
    }

    const token = authHeader.split(' ')[1];
    const user = verifyToken(token);

    if (!user) {
        res.status(401).json({
            error: 'Invalid or expired token',
            code: 'AUTH_TOKEN_INVALID'
        });
        return;
    }

    req.user = user;
    logger.debug(`Authenticated user: ${user.email}`);
    next();
}

/**
 * Optional authentication - attaches user if token present, continues otherwise
 */
export function optionalAuth(req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;

    if (authHeader && authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        const token = authHeader.split(' ')[1];
        const user = verifyToken(token);
        if (user) {
            req.user = user;
        }
    }

    next();
}

/**
 * Role-based access control middleware
 */
export function requireRole(...allowedRoles: Array<'admin' | 'tenant' | 'viewer'>) {
    return (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
        if (!req.user) {
            res.status(401).json({
                error: 'Authentication required',
                code: 'AUTH_REQUIRED'
            });
            return;
        }

        if (!allowedRoles.includes(req.user.role)) {
            logger.warn(`Access denied for user ${req.user.email} (role: ${req.user.role}), required: ${allowedRoles.join('/')}`);
            res.status(403).json({
                error: 'Insufficient permissions',
                code: 'AUTH_FORBIDDEN',
                requiredRoles: allowedRoles
            });
            return;
        }

        next();
    };
}

/**
 * Tenant isolation middleware - ensures users can only access their tenant's resources
 */
export function requireTenantAccess(req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    if (!req.user) {
        res.status(401).json({ error: 'Authentication required' });
        return;
    }

    // Admins can access all tenants
    if (req.user.role === 'admin') {
        next();
        return;
    }

    // Check if requested tenant matches user's tenant
    const requestedTenantId = req.params.tenantId || req.query.tenantId || req.body?.tenantId;

    if (requestedTenantId && requestedTenantId !== req.user.tenantId) {
        res.status(403).json({
            error: 'Cannot access resources from another tenant',
            code: 'TENANT_MISMATCH'
        });
        return;
    }

    next();
}

/**
 * API Key authentication for agent/device communication
 */
export function authenticateApiKey(req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    const apiKey = req.headers['x-api-key'] as string;

    if (!apiKey) {
        res.status(401).json({
            error: 'API key required',
            code: 'API_KEY_MISSING'
        });
        return;
    }

    // In production, validate against database
    // For now, use environment variable
    const validApiKeys = (process.env.VALID_API_KEYS || 'demo-api-key').split(',');

    if (!validApiKeys.includes(apiKey)) {
        logger.warn(`Invalid API key attempt: ${apiKey.substring(0, 8)}...`);
        res.status(401).json({
            error: 'Invalid API key',
            code: 'API_KEY_INVALID'
        });
        return;
    }

    // Attach a system user for API key requests
    req.user = {
        userId: 'api-key-user',
        email: 'api@fluxguard.local',
        username: 'API Client',
        role: 'tenant'
    };

    next();
}

/**
 * Combined auth - accepts either JWT or API key
 */
export function authenticateAny(req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
    const apiKey = req.headers['x-api-key'] as string;

    if (authHeader && authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        return authenticate(req, res, next);
    }

    if (apiKey) {
        return authenticateApiKey(req, res, next);
    }

    res.status(401).json({
        error: 'Authentication required (JWT or API key)',
        code: 'AUTH_REQUIRED'
    });
}
-- File: ai-engine.ts
/**
 * AI Engine - LLM Integration
 * Integrates with various LLM providers for intelligent problem analysis and fixing
 */

export interface AIProvider {
  name: 'openai' | 'anthropic' | 'ollama' | 'zhipu' | 'custom';
  apiKey?: string;
  baseURL?: string;
  model: string;
}

export interface AIMessage {
  role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string;
}

export interface AIAnalysisRequest {
  problem: {
    title: string;
    description: string;
    category: string;
    priority: string;
  };
  context: {
    systemInfo: any;
    logs: string[];
    configFiles: Record<string, string>;
    commandOutput?: string;
    errorOutput?: string;
  };
  previousAttempts?: Array<{
    solution: string;
    output: string;
    error: string;
  }>;
}

export interface AIAnalysisResponse {
  diagnosis: {
    rootCause: string;
    severity: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical';
    confidence: number; // 0-1
  };
  solutions: Array<{
    command: string;
    explanation: string;
    riskLevel: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
    estimatedSuccess: number; // 0-100
    prerequisites?: string[]; // Commands to run first
  }>;
  alternativeApproaches?: string[];
  warnings?: string[];
}

export class AIEngine {
  private provider: AIProvider;

  constructor(provider: AIProvider) {
    this.provider = provider;
  }

  /**
   * Analyze a problem and suggest solutions using AI
   */
  async analyzeProblem(request: AIAnalysisRequest): Promise<AIAnalysisResponse> {
    const prompt = this.buildAnalysisPrompt(request);

    try {
      const response = await this.callLLM(prompt);

      return this.parseResponse(response);
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('AI analysis failed:', error);

      // Fallback to rule-based analysis
      return this.fallbackAnalysis(request);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get AI suggestion when a command fails
   */
  async diagnoseFailure(
    originalProblem: any,
    attemptedSolution: string,
    commandOutput: string,
    errorOutput: string
  ): Promise<{
    diagnosis: string;
    alternativeSolution: string;
    explanation: string;
  }> {
    const prompt = `
Original Problem:
${JSON.stringify(originalProblem, null, 2)}

Attempted Solution:
${attemptedSolution}

Command Output:
${commandOutput}

Error Output:
${errorOutput}

The command failed. Please:
1. Diagnose why it failed
2. Suggest an alternative approach
3. Explain what went wrong

Provide your response in JSON format:
{
  "diagnosis": "why it failed",
  "alternativeSolution": "alternative command or approach",
  "explanation": "detailed explanation"
}
`;

    try {
      const response = await this.callLLM(prompt);
      return JSON.parse(response);
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('AI diagnosis failed:', error);

      return {
        diagnosis: 'Command failed with unknown error',
        alternativeSolution: 'Check system logs for more details',
        explanation: 'AI analysis unavailable, manual intervention required'
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Monitor execution and provide real-time suggestions
   */
  async analyzeExecutionOutput(
    command: string,
    output: string,
    isComplete: boolean
  ): Promise<{
    status: 'normal' | 'warning' | 'error' | 'success';
    message: string;
    suggestions?: string[];
    shouldAbort?: boolean;
  }> {
    const prompt = `
Command Being Executed:
${command}

Current Output:
${output}

Execution Status: ${isComplete ? 'Complete' : 'In Progress'}

Analyze the output and provide:
1. Status assessment (normal/warning/error/success)
2. Brief message explaining what's happening
3. Suggestions if there are issues
4. Whether to abort if critical error detected

Response in JSON format:
{
  "status": "normal|warning|error|success",
  "message": "what's happening",
  "suggestions": ["suggestion1", "suggestion2"],
  "shouldAbort": false
}
`;

    try {
      const response = await this.callLLM(prompt);
      return JSON.parse(response);
    } catch (error: any) {
      // Simple fallback analysis
      if (output.toLowerCase().includes('error')) {
        return {
          status: 'error',
          message: 'Error detected in output',
          suggestions: ['Check error messages', 'Review logs']
        };
      }

      return {
        status: 'normal',
        message: isComplete ? 'Command completed' : 'Command running...'
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Build prompt for AI analysis
   */
  private buildAnalysisPrompt(request: AIAnalysisRequest): string {
    const { problem, context, previousAttempts } = request;

    let prompt = `You are an expert Linux system administrator and DevOps engineer. Analyze this problem and suggest solutions.

PROBLEM:
Title: ${problem.title}
Description: ${problem.description}
Category: ${problem.category}
Priority: ${problem.priority}

SYSTEM CONTEXT:
`;

    // Add system info
    if (context.systemInfo) {
      prompt += `
OS: ${context.systemInfo.platform} ${context.systemInfo.release}
CPUs: ${context.systemInfo.cpus}
Memory: ${context.systemInfo.totalMemory}
Uptime: ${context.systemInfo.uptime}
`;
    }

    // Add previous attempts
    if (previousAttempts && previousAttempts.length > 0) {
      prompt += `
PREVIOUS ATTEMPTS (all failed):
`;
      previousAttempts.forEach((attempt, i) => {
        prompt += `
Attempt ${i + 1}:
Solution: ${attempt.solution}
Output: ${attempt.output}
Error: ${attempt.error}
`;
      });
    }

    // Add relevant logs
    if (context.logs && context.logs.length > 0) {
      prompt += `
RELEVANT LOGS:
${context.logs.join('\n')}
`;
    }

    // Add command output if available
    if (context.commandOutput) {
      prompt += `
COMMAND OUTPUT:
${context.commandOutput}
`;
    }

    if (context.errorOutput) {
      prompt += `
ERROR OUTPUT:
${context.errorOutput}
`;
    }

    prompt += `
Please analyze and provide:
1. Root cause diagnosis
2. Severity assessment
3. Multiple solutions (from safest to most aggressive)
4. Risk level for each solution
5. Estimated success rate
6. Any warnings

Response in JSON format:
{
  "diagnosis": {
    "rootCause": "primary cause",
    "severity": "low|medium|high|critical",
    "confidence": 0.85
  },
  "solutions": [
    {
      "command": "exact command to execute",
      "explanation": "why this will work",
      "riskLevel": "low|medium|high",
      "estimatedSuccess": 85,
      "prerequisites": ["commands to run first"]
    }
  ],
  "alternativeApproaches": ["alternative 1", "alternative 2"],
  "warnings": ["warning 1", "warning 2"]
}
`;

    return prompt;
  }

  /**
   * Call LLM API
   */
  private async callLLM(prompt: string): Promise<string> {
    const { name, apiKey, baseURL, model } = this.provider;

    switch (name) {
      case 'openai':
        return this.callOpenAI(prompt, model, apiKey);
      case 'anthropic':
        return this.callAnthropic(prompt, model, apiKey);
      case 'ollama':
        return this.callOllama(prompt, model, baseURL);
      case 'zhipu':
        return this.callZhipu(prompt, model, apiKey, baseURL);
      default:
        throw new Error(`Unsupported AI provider: ${name}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Call OpenAI API
   */
  private async callOpenAI(prompt: string, model: string, apiKey?: string): Promise<string> {
    const axios = require('axios');

    const response = await axios.post(
      'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions',
      {
        model: model || 'gpt-4',
        messages: [
          {
            role: 'system',
            content: 'You are an expert Linux system administrator. Always respond with valid JSON.'
          },
          {
            role: 'user',
            content: prompt
          }
        ],
        temperature: 0.3,
        max_tokens: 2000
      },
      {
        headers: {
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${apiKey || process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        }
      }
    );

    return response.data.choices[0].message.content;
  }

  /**
   * Call Anthropic Claude API
   */
  private async callAnthropic(prompt: string, model: string, apiKey?: string): Promise<string> {
    const axios = require('axios');

    const response = await axios.post(
      'https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',
      {
        model: model || 'claude-3-sonnet-20240229',
        max_tokens: 2000,
        messages: [
          {
            role: 'user',
            content: prompt
          }
        ]
      },
      {
        headers: {
          'x-api-key': apiKey || process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01'
        }
      }
    );

    return response.data.content[0].text;
  }

  /**
   * Call Ollama (local LLM)
   */
  private async callOllama(prompt: string, model: string, baseURL?: string): Promise<string> {
    const axios = require('axios');

    const response = await axios.post(
      `${baseURL || 'http://localhost:11434'}/api/generate`,
      {
        model: model || 'llama2',
        prompt: prompt,
        stream: false
      }
    );

    return response.data.response;
  }

  /**
   * Parse AI response
   */
  private parseResponse(response: string): AIAnalysisResponse {
    try {
      // Try to extract JSON from markdown code blocks
      const jsonMatch = response.match(/```json\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/) ||
        response.match(/```\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/);

      if (jsonMatch) {
        return JSON.parse(jsonMatch[1]);
      }

      // Try parsing directly
      return JSON.parse(response);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to parse AI response:', error);
      throw new Error('Invalid JSON response from AI');
    }
  }

  /**
   * Fallback analysis when AI is unavailable
   */
  private fallbackAnalysis(request: AIAnalysisRequest): AIAnalysisResponse {
    const { problem } = request;

    // Simple rule-based analysis
    const solutions: Array<{
      command: string;
      explanation: string;
      riskLevel: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
      estimatedSuccess: number;
    }> = [];

    // Add common solutions based on problem category
    if (problem.category === 'security') {
      if (problem.title.toLowerCase().includes('firewall')) {
        solutions.push({
          command: 'sudo ufw enable',
          explanation: 'Enable UFW firewall',
          riskLevel: 'low',
          estimatedSuccess: 90
        });
      }
    }

    if (problem.category === 'performance') {
      if (problem.title.toLowerCase().includes('file descriptor')) {
        solutions.push({
          command: 'ulimit -n 65535',
          explanation: 'Increase file descriptor limit',
          riskLevel: 'low',
          estimatedSuccess: 85
        });
      }
    }

    return {
      diagnosis: {
        rootCause: 'Unable to determine without AI',
        severity: problem.priority === 'critical' ? 'critical' : 'medium',
        confidence: 0.5
      },
      solutions: solutions.length > 0 ? solutions : [{
        command: '# Manual intervention required',
        explanation: 'AI analysis unavailable, please review logs',
        riskLevel: 'medium',
        estimatedSuccess: 50
      }],
      warnings: ['AI analysis unavailable, using rule-based fallback']
    };
  }
/**   * Call Zhipu AI (BigModel) API   */  private async callZhipu(prompt: string, model: string, apiKey?: string, baseURL?: string): Promise<string> { const { ZhipuClient } = require("./zhipu-client"); const client = new ZhipuClient(apiKey || process.env.ZHIPU_API_KEY || "", baseURL || "https://open.bigmodel.cn/api/anthropic"); return await client.generateCompletion(prompt, model); }
}

// Singleton instances for different providers
export const createAIEngine = (provider: AIProvider): AIEngine => {
  return new AIEngine(provider);
};
-- File: execution-monitor.ts
import { spawn, ChildProcess } from 'child_process';
import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import { AIEngine } from './ai-engine';

/**
 * Execution Monitor
 * Real-time monitoring of command execution with AI-powered analysis
 */

export interface ExecutionOptions {
  timeout?: number; // milliseconds
  env?: Record<string, string>;
  cwd?: string;
  aiEngine?: AIEngine;
  enableAIMonitoring?: boolean;
}

export interface ExecutionResult {
  command: string;
  exitCode: number | null;
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
  duration: number;
  success: boolean;
  aiAnalysis?: any;
}

export interface ExecutionEvent {
  type: 'start' | 'stdout' | 'stderr' | 'ai-analysis' | 'complete' | 'error' | 'timeout';
  timestamp: number;
  data: any;
}

export class ExecutionMonitor extends EventEmitter {
  private currentProcess: ChildProcess | null = null;
  private startTime: number = 0;
  private stdoutBuffer: string[] = [];
  private stderrBuffer: string[] = [];
  private aiAnalysisInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
  private executionId: string;

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.executionId = `exec-${Date.now()}`;
  }

  /**
   * Execute command with real-time monitoring
   */
  async execute(command: string, options: ExecutionOptions = {}): Promise<ExecutionResult> {
    const {
      timeout = 300000, // 5 minutes default
      env = process.env,
      cwd = process.cwd(),
      aiEngine,
      enableAIMonitoring = false
    } = options;

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.startTime = Date.now();
      this.stdoutBuffer = [];
      this.stderrBuffer = [];

      // Emit start event
      this.emitEvent('start', { command, executionId: this.executionId });

      // Spawn process
      const [cmd, ...args] = command.split(/\s+/);
      this.currentProcess = spawn(cmd, args, {
        env,
        cwd,
        timeout
      });

      // Setup AI monitoring if enabled
      if (enableAIMonitoring && aiEngine) {
        this.startAIMonitoring(command, aiEngine);
      }

      // Collect stdout
      this.currentProcess.stdout?.on('data', (data) => {
        const output = data.toString();
        this.stdoutBuffer.push(output);
        this.emitEvent('stdout', { output: output.trim() });

        // Real-time AI analysis on suspicious output
        if (enableAIMonitoring && aiEngine) {
          this.analyzeOutputInRealTime(command, output, aiEngine);
        }
      });

      // Collect stderr
      this.currentProcess.stderr?.on('data', (data) => {
        const error = data.toString();
        this.stderrBuffer.push(error);
        this.emitEvent('stderr', { error: error.trim() });

        // AI analysis on errors
        if (enableAIMonitoring && aiEngine) {
          this.analyzeErrorInRealTime(command, error, aiEngine);
        }
      });

      // Handle process exit
      this.currentProcess.on('close', async (code) => {
        this.stopAIMonitoring();

        const duration = Date.now() - this.startTime;
        const stdout = this.stdoutBuffer.join('\n');
        const stderr = this.stderrBuffer.join('\n');

        const result: ExecutionResult = {
          command,
          exitCode: code,
          stdout,
          stderr,
          duration,
          success: code === 0
        };

        // Final AI analysis if enabled
        if (enableAIMonitoring && aiEngine && !result.success) {
          try {
            result.aiAnalysis = await this.analyzeFailure(command, result, aiEngine);
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Final AI analysis failed:', error);
          }
        }

        this.emitEvent('complete', result);

        if (result.success) {
          resolve(result);
        } else {
          reject(result);
        }
      });

      // Handle error
      this.currentProcess.on('error', (error) => {
        this.stopAIMonitoring();
        this.emitEvent('error', { error: error.message });
        reject({
          command,
          exitCode: null,
          stdout: this.stdoutBuffer.join('\n'),
          stderr: this.stderrBuffer.join('\n'),
          duration: Date.now() - this.startTime,
          success: false,
          error: error.message
        });
      });

      // Handle timeout
      if (timeout) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (this.currentProcess && !this.currentProcess.killed) {
            this.currentProcess.kill('SIGTERM');
            this.emitEvent('timeout', { timeout });
            this.stopAIMonitoring();
          }
        }, timeout);
      }
    });
  }

  /**
   * Start periodic AI monitoring
   */
  private startAIMonitoring(command: string, aiEngine: AIEngine): void {
    // Analyze execution every 10 seconds
    this.aiAnalysisInterval = setInterval(async () => {
      const currentOutput = this.stdoutBuffer.join('\n');
      const currentError = this.stderrBuffer.join('\n');

      if (currentOutput || currentError) {
        try {
          const analysis = await aiEngine.analyzeExecutionOutput(
            command,
            currentOutput + '\n' + currentError,
            false // Not complete yet
          );

          this.emitEvent('ai-analysis', analysis);

          // Abort if AI recommends it
          if (analysis.shouldAbort) {
            console.warn('AI recommends aborting execution:', analysis.message);
            this.abortExecution();
          }
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Real-time AI analysis failed:', error);
        }
      }
    }, 10000);
  }

  /**
   * Stop AI monitoring
   */
  private stopAIMonitoring(): void {
    if (this.aiAnalysisInterval) {
      clearInterval(this.aiAnalysisInterval);
      this.aiAnalysisInterval = null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Real-time analysis of output
   */
  private async analyzeOutputInRealTime(command: string, output: string, aiEngine: AIEngine): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const analysis = await aiEngine.analyzeExecutionOutput(command, output, false);

      if (analysis.status === 'warning' || analysis.status === 'error') {
        this.emitEvent('ai-analysis', {
          type: 'warning',
          message: analysis.message,
          suggestions: analysis.suggestions
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      // Silently fail for real-time analysis
      console.debug('Real-time output analysis failed:', error);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Real-time analysis of errors
   */
  private async analyzeErrorInRealTime(command: string, error: string, aiEngine: AIEngine): Promise<void> {
    try {
      // Check if error looks critical
      const criticalPatterns = [
        'permission denied',
        'access denied',
        'no space left',
        'segmentation fault',
        'killed'
      ];

      const isCritical = criticalPatterns.some(pattern =>
        error.toLowerCase().includes(pattern)
      );

      if (isCritical) {
        this.emitEvent('ai-analysis', {
          type: 'critical-error',
          message: 'Critical error detected',
          error: error.trim(),
          shouldAbort: true
        });

        // Optionally abort on critical errors
        // this.abortExecution();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.debug('Real-time error analysis failed:', error);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Analyze failure after execution completes
   */
  private async analyzeFailure(command: string, result: ExecutionResult, aiEngine: AIEngine): Promise<any> {
    try {
      return await aiEngine.diagnoseFailure(
        { command },
        command,
        result.stdout,
        result.stderr
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failure analysis failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Abort current execution
   */
  abortExecution(): void {
    if (this.currentProcess && !this.currentProcess.killed) {
      this.currentProcess.kill('SIGKILL');
      this.emitEvent('error', { message: 'Execution aborted' });
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get execution ID
   */
  getExecutionId(): string {
    return this.executionId;
  }

  /**
   * Check if process is running
   */
  isRunning(): boolean {
    return this.currentProcess !== null && !this.currentProcess.killed;
  }

  /**
   * Get current output
   */
  getCurrentOutput(): { stdout: string; stderr: string } {
    return {
      stdout: this.stdoutBuffer.join('\n'),
      stderr: this.stderrBuffer.join('\n')
    };
  }

  /**
   * Emit event
   */
  private emitEvent(type: string, data: any): void {
    this.emit('execution-event', {
      type,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      executionId: this.executionId,
      ...data
    } as ExecutionEvent);
  }
}
-- File: nuclei-scanner.ts
/**
 * Nuclei 婕忔礊鎵弿鍣ㄩ泦鎴?
 * 
 * 闆嗘垚 ProjectDiscovery Nuclei 杩涜婕忔礊鎵弿
 * https://github.com/projectdiscovery/nuclei
 */

import { exec, spawn } from 'child_process';
import { promisify } from 'util';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs/promises';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

export interface VulnerabilityResult {
    templateId: string;
    name: string;
    severity: 'critical' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'low' | 'info';
    host: string;
    matched: string;
    timestamp: Date;
    description?: string;
    reference?: string[];
    tags?: string[];
}

export interface ScanProgress {
    status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'completed' | 'failed';
    progress: number; // 0-100
    templatesTotal: number;
    templatesCompleted: number;
    vulnerabilitiesFound: number;
    startTime: Date;
    endTime?: Date;
    error?: string;
}

export class NucleiScanner {
    private nucleiPath: string = 'nuclei'; // 榛樿浣跨敤 PATH 涓殑 nuclei
    private templatesPath: string = '';
    private outputDir: string = '';
    private isAvailable: boolean = false;

    constructor(options?: { nucleiPath?: string; templatesPath?: string; outputDir?: string }) {
        if (options?.nucleiPath) this.nucleiPath = options.nucleiPath;
        if (options?.templatesPath) this.templatesPath = options.templatesPath;
        this.outputDir = options?.outputDir || path.join(process.cwd(), 'scan-results');
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌?Nuclei 鏄惁宸插畨瑁?
     */
    async checkAvailability(): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            const { stdout } = await execAsync(`${this.nucleiPath} -version`);
            logger.info(`Nuclei available: ${stdout.trim()}`);
            this.isAvailable = true;
            return true;
        } catch (error) {
            logger.warn('Nuclei not found. Install from: https://github.com/projectdiscovery/nuclei');
            this.isAvailable = false;
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鏇存柊 Nuclei 妯℃澘
     */
    async updateTemplates(): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            logger.info('Updating Nuclei templates...');
            const { stdout } = await execAsync(`${this.nucleiPath} -update-templates`);
            logger.info(`Templates updated: ${stdout.trim()}`);
            return true;
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error('Failed to update templates', { error: error.message });
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 杩愯婕忔礊鎵弿
     * @param target 鐩爣 URL 鎴?IP
     * @param options 鎵弿閫夐」
     */
    async scan(
        target: string,
        options?: {
            severity?: ('critical' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'low' | 'info')[];
            tags?: string[];
            templates?: string[];
            timeout?: number; // 鍒嗛挓
            rateLimit?: number; // 璇锋眰/绉?
            onProgress?: (progress: ScanProgress) => void;
        }
    ): Promise<VulnerabilityResult[]> {
        if (!this.isAvailable) {
            await this.checkAvailability();
            if (!this.isAvailable) {
                throw new Error('Nuclei is not installed');
            }
        }

        const results: VulnerabilityResult[] = [];
        const scanId = `scan-${Date.now()}`;
        const outputFile = path.join(this.outputDir, `${scanId}.json`);

        // 纭繚杈撳嚭鐩綍瀛樺湪
        await fs.mkdir(this.outputDir, { recursive: true });

        // 鏋勫缓鍛戒护鍙傛暟
        const args: string[] = [
            '-target', target,
            '-json',
            '-output', outputFile,
            '-silent'
        ];

        // 涓ラ噸鎬ц繃婊?
        if (options?.severity && options.severity.length > 0) {
            args.push('-severity', options.severity.join(','));
        }

        // 鏍囩杩囨护
        if (options?.tags && options.tags.length > 0) {
            args.push('-tags', options.tags.join(','));
        }

        // 鎸囧畾妯℃澘
        if (options?.templates && options.templates.length > 0) {
            for (const template of options.templates) {
                args.push('-t', template);
            }
        }

        // 閫熺巼闄愬埗
        if (options?.rateLimit) {
            args.push('-rate-limit', options.rateLimit.toString());
        }

        // 瓒呮椂
        if (options?.timeout) {
            args.push('-timeout', (options.timeout * 60).toString());
        }

        // 鑷畾涔夋ā鏉胯矾寰?
        if (this.templatesPath) {
            args.push('-t', this.templatesPath);
        }

        logger.info(`Starting Nuclei scan: ${target}`, { args });

        const progress: ScanProgress = {
            status: 'running',
            progress: 0,
            templatesTotal: 0,
            templatesCompleted: 0,
            vulnerabilitiesFound: 0,
            startTime: new Date()
        };

        options?.onProgress?.(progress);

        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const nucleiProcess = spawn(this.nucleiPath, args, {
                stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe']
            });

            let stderrData = '';

            nucleiProcess.stdout.on('data', (data: Buffer) => {
                const lines = data.toString().split('\n').filter(line => line.trim());

                for (const line of lines) {
                    try {
                        const result = JSON.parse(line);
                        if (result.info) {
                            const vuln: VulnerabilityResult = {
                                templateId: result['template-id'] || result.templateID,
                                name: result.info.name,
                                severity: result.info.severity || 'info',
                                host: result.host,
                                matched: result.matched || result['matched-at'],
                                timestamp: new Date(result.timestamp || Date.now()),
                                description: result.info.description,
                                reference: result.info.reference,
                                tags: result.info.tags
                            };
                            results.push(vuln);
                            progress.vulnerabilitiesFound = results.length;
                            options?.onProgress?.(progress);
                        }
                    } catch {
                        // 闈?JSON 杈撳嚭锛屽拷鐣?
                    }
                }
            });

            nucleiProcess.stderr.on('data', (data: Buffer) => {
                stderrData += data.toString();

                // 灏濊瘯瑙ｆ瀽杩涘害淇℃伅
                const progressMatch = stderrData.match(/Templates: (\d+)\/(\d+)/);
                if (progressMatch) {
                    progress.templatesCompleted = parseInt(progressMatch[1]);
                    progress.templatesTotal = parseInt(progressMatch[2]);
                    progress.progress = Math.round((progress.templatesCompleted / progress.templatesTotal) * 100);
                    options?.onProgress?.(progress);
                }
            });

            nucleiProcess.on('close', async (code) => {
                progress.endTime = new Date();

                if (code === 0) {
                    progress.status = 'completed';
                    progress.progress = 100;
                    logger.info(`Nuclei scan completed: ${results.length} vulnerabilities found`);

                    // 灏濊瘯璇诲彇杈撳嚭鏂囦欢鑾峰彇瀹屾暣缁撴灉
                    try {
                        const fileContent = await fs.readFile(outputFile, 'utf-8');
                        const fileResults = fileContent
                            .split('\n')
                            .filter(line => line.trim())
                            .map(line => {
                                try {
                                    return JSON.parse(line);
                                } catch {
                                    return null;
                                }
                            })
                            .filter(Boolean);

                        // 鍚堝苟缁撴灉
                        for (const result of fileResults) {
                            if (!results.find(r => r.templateId === result['template-id'] && r.host === result.host)) {
                                results.push({
                                    templateId: result['template-id'],
                                    name: result.info?.name || 'Unknown',
                                    severity: result.info?.severity || 'info',
                                    host: result.host,
                                    matched: result.matched || result['matched-at'],
                                    timestamp: new Date(result.timestamp || Date.now()),
                                    description: result.info?.description,
                                    reference: result.info?.reference,
                                    tags: result.info?.tags
                                });
                            }
                        }
                    } catch {
                        // 鏂囦欢涓嶅瓨鍦ㄦ垨璇诲彇澶辫触
                    }

                    options?.onProgress?.(progress);
                    resolve(results);
                } else {
                    progress.status = 'failed';
                    progress.error = stderrData || `Exit code: ${code}`;
                    logger.error(`Nuclei scan failed`, { code, stderr: stderrData });
                    options?.onProgress?.(progress);
                    reject(new Error(progress.error));
                }
            });

            nucleiProcess.on('error', (error) => {
                progress.status = 'failed';
                progress.error = error.message;
                progress.endTime = new Date();
                options?.onProgress?.(progress);
                reject(error);
            });
        });
    }

    /**
     * 蹇€熸壂鎻?- 浠呴珮鍗卞拰涓ラ噸婕忔礊
     */
    async quickScan(target: string, onProgress?: (progress: ScanProgress) => void): Promise<VulnerabilityResult[]> {
        return this.scan(target, {
            severity: ['critical', 'high'],
            rateLimit: 100,
            timeout: 10,
            onProgress
        });
    }

    /**
     * 瀹屾暣鎵弿 - 鎵€鏈夌骇鍒?
     */
    async fullScan(target: string, onProgress?: (progress: ScanProgress) => void): Promise<VulnerabilityResult[]> {
        return this.scan(target, {
            severity: ['critical', 'high', 'medium', 'low'],
            rateLimit: 50,
            timeout: 60,
            onProgress
        });
    }

    /**
     * Web 搴旂敤鎵弿 - 涓撴敞浜?Web 婕忔礊
     */
    async webAppScan(target: string, onProgress?: (progress: ScanProgress) => void): Promise<VulnerabilityResult[]> {
        return this.scan(target, {
            tags: ['xss', 'sqli', 'ssrf', 'lfi', 'rfi', 'rce', 'injection'],
            severity: ['critical', 'high', 'medium'],
            rateLimit: 30,
            timeout: 30,
            onProgress
        });
    }

    /**
     * CVE 鎵弿 - 宸茬煡婕忔礊妫€娴?
     */
    async cveScan(target: string, onProgress?: (progress: ScanProgress) => void): Promise<VulnerabilityResult[]> {
        return this.scan(target, {
            tags: ['cve'],
            severity: ['critical', 'high'],
            rateLimit: 50,
            timeout: 30,
            onProgress
        });
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎵弿缁撴灉鎽樿
     */
    getSummary(results: VulnerabilityResult[]): {
        total: number;
        bySeverity: Record<string, number>;
        byType: Record<string, number>;
    } {
        const bySeverity: Record<string, number> = {
            critical: 0,
            high: 0,
            medium: 0,
            low: 0,
            info: 0
        };

        const byType: Record<string, number> = {};

        for (const result of results) {
            bySeverity[result.severity] = (bySeverity[result.severity] || 0) + 1;

            const type = result.tags?.[0] || 'other';
            byType[type] = (byType[type] || 0) + 1;
        }

        return {
            total: results.length,
            bySeverity,
            byType
        };
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚﹀彲鐢?
     */
    getIsAvailable(): boolean {
        return this.isAvailable;
    }
}

// 瀵煎嚭鍗曚緥
export const nucleiScanner = new NucleiScanner();
-- File: optimization-engine.ts
import { PortScanner } from './port-scanner';
import { SystemScanner } from './system-scanner';
import { WebScanner } from './web-scanner';
import { Recommendation, ScanOptions } from '../database/models/scan-result';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

/**
 * Optimization Engine
 * Orchestrates all scanners and generates comprehensive optimization recommendations
 */
export class OptimizationEngine {
  private portScanner: PortScanner;
  private systemScanner: SystemScanner;
  private webScanner: WebScanner;

  constructor() {
    this.portScanner = new PortScanner();
    this.systemScanner = new SystemScanner();
    this.webScanner = new WebScanner();
  }

  /**
   * Run full server scan
   */
  async runFullScan(options: ScanOptions = {}): Promise<{
    systemInfo: any;
    recommendations: Recommendation[];
    summary: {
      critical: number;
      high: number;
      medium: number;
      low: number;
    };
  }> {
    const allRecommendations: Recommendation[] = [];
    const systemInfo: any = {};

    logger.info('Starting full server scan...');

    // 1. Port scanning
    if (options.scanPorts !== false) {
      logger.info('Running port scan...');
      const portRecs = await this.portScanner.scanCommonPorts();
      allRecommendations.push(...portRecs);
    }

    // 2. System scanning
    if (options.scanSystem !== false) {
      logger.info('Running system scan...');
      const { info, recommendations } = await this.systemScanner.scanSystem();
      Object.assign(systemInfo, info);
      allRecommendations.push(...recommendations);
    }

    // 3. Web server scanning
    if (options.scanWebServer !== false) {
      logger.info('Running web server scan...');
      const { server, recommendations } = await this.webScanner.scanWebServer();
      systemInfo.webServer = server;
      allRecommendations.push(...recommendations);
    }

    // Sort recommendations by priority
    const sortedRecs = this.sortRecommendations(allRecommendations);

    // Generate summary
    const summary = this.generateSummary(sortedRecs);

    logger.info('Full server scan complete', { summary });

    return {
      systemInfo,
      recommendations: sortedRecs,
      summary
    };
  }

  /**
   * Apply an optimization
   */
  async applyOptimization(recommendation: Recommendation): Promise<{ success: boolean; output?: string; error?: string }> {
    if (!recommendation.autoApplicable) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'This recommendation cannot be automatically applied'
      };
    }

    logger.info(`Applying optimization: ${recommendation.title}`);

    try {
      const { exec } = require('child_process');
      const util = require('util');
      const execAsync = util.promisify(exec);

      // Execute the action
      const { stdout, stderr } = await execAsync(recommendation.action);

      logger.info(`Optimization applied successfully`, { output: stdout });

      return {
        success: true,
        output: stdout || stderr
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error(`Failed to apply optimization`, { error });

      return {
        success: false,
        error: error.message
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Sort recommendations by priority
   */
  private sortRecommendations(recommendations: Recommendation[]): Recommendation[] {
    const priorityOrder = {
      critical: 0,
      high: 1,
      medium: 2,
      low: 3
    };

    return recommendations.sort((a, b) => {
      return priorityOrder[a.priority] - priorityOrder[b.priority];
    });
  }

  /**
   * Generate summary statistics
   */
  private generateSummary(recommendations: Recommendation[]): {
    critical: number;
    high: number;
    medium: number;
    low: number;
  } {
    return {
      critical: recommendations.filter(r => r.priority === 'critical').length,
      high: recommendations.filter(r => r.priority === 'high').length,
      medium: recommendations.filter(r => r.priority === 'medium').length,
      low: recommendations.filter(r => r.priority === 'low').length
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get optimization preview (what would be done)
   */
  async previewOptimizations(recommendations: Recommendation[]): Promise<{
    autoApplicable: Recommendation[];
    manual: Recommendation[];
  }> {
    const autoApplicable = recommendations.filter(r => r.autoApplicable);
    const manual = recommendations.filter(r => !r.autoApplicable);

    return { autoApplicable, manual };
  }

  /**
   * Export recommendations as report
   */
  generateReport(systemInfo: any, recommendations: Recommendation[]): string {
    let report = '# NewSecurity Server Optimization Report\n\n';
    report += `Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}\n\n`;

    report += '## System Information\n\n';
    report += `- Platform: ${systemInfo.platform} ${systemInfo.release}\n`;
    report += `- Architecture: ${systemInfo.arch}\n`;
    report += `- CPUs: ${systemInfo.cpus}\n`;
    report += `- Total Memory: ${systemInfo.totalMemory}\n`;
    report += `- Uptime: ${systemInfo.uptime}\n`;
    if (systemInfo.webServer) {
      report += `- Web Server: ${systemInfo.webServer}\n`;
    }
    report += '\n';

    report += '## Recommendations\n\n';

    const byPriority = recommendations.reduce((acc, rec) => {
      if (!acc[rec.priority]) acc[rec.priority] = [];
      acc[rec.priority].push(rec);
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, Recommendation[]>);

    const priorities = ['critical', 'high', 'medium', 'low'];

    for (const priority of priorities) {
      if (byPriority[priority] && byPriority[priority].length > 0) {
        report += `### ${priority.toUpperCase()} Priority (${byPriority[priority].length})\n\n`;

        for (const rec of byPriority[priority]) {
          report += `#### ${rec.title}\n`;
          report += `- **Category**: ${rec.category}\n`;
          report += `- **Description**: ${rec.description}\n`;
          report += `- **Action**: \`${rec.action}\`\n`;
          report += `- **Risk**: ${rec.risk}\n`;
          report += `- **Auto-Applicable**: ${rec.autoApplicable ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\n\n`;
        }
      }
    }

    report += '\n---\n';
    report += 'Generated by NewSecurity v1.0.0\n';

    return report;
  }
}
-- File: port-scanner.ts
import { exec } from 'child_process';
import { promisify } from 'util';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { Recommendation } from '../database/models/scan-result';

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

/**
 * Port Scanner
 * Scans for open ports and services
 */
export class PortScanner {
  /**
   * Scan common ports
   */
  async scanCommonPorts(): Promise<Recommendation[]> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];

    try {
      // Common ports to scan
      const commonPorts = [21, 22, 23, 25, 53, 80, 110, 143, 443, 993, 995, 3306, 3389, 5432, 6379, 8000, 8080, 27017];

      logger.info('Scanning common ports...');

      // Use netstat to check listening ports
      const { stdout } = await execAsync('netstat -tulpn 2>/dev/null || ss -tulpn 2>/dev/null || lsof -i -P -n | grep LISTEN');

      const openPorts = this.parseOpenPorts(stdout);

      // Check for security issues
      for (const port of commonPorts) {
        if (openPorts.includes(port)) {
          const recommendation = this.analyzePort(port);
          if (recommendation) {
            recommendations.push(recommendation);
          }
        }
      }

      logger.info(`Port scan complete. Found ${openPorts.length} open ports`);
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Port scan failed', { error });
    }

    return recommendations;
  }

  /**
   * Parse open ports from netstat output
   */
  private parseOpenPorts(output: string): number[] {
    const ports: number[] = [];
    const lines = output.split('\n');

    for (const line of lines) {
      // Match port numbers in the output
      const match = line.match(/:(\d+)\s/);
      if (match) {
        const port = parseInt(match[1]);
        if (!ports.includes(port)) {
          ports.push(port);
        }
      }
    }

    return ports;
  }

  /**
   * Analyze a specific port and generate recommendation
   */
  private analyzePort(port: number): Recommendation | null {
    const securityRisks: Record<number, Recommendation> = {
      21: {
        category: 'security',
        priority: 'medium',
        title: 'FTP Port (21) is Open',
        description: 'FTP transmits data in plain text and can be a security risk',
        action: 'Consider using SFTP (port 22) or FTPS instead',
        autoApplicable: false,
        risk: 'Credentials and data can be intercepted'
      },
      23: {
        category: 'security',
        priority: 'high',
        title: 'Telnet Port (23) is Open',
        description: 'Telnet is insecure and transmits data in plain text',
        action: 'Disable Telnet and use SSH instead',
        autoApplicable: false,
