        risk: 'Credentials can be easily intercepted'
      },
      3306: {
        category: 'security',
        priority: 'high',
        title: 'MySQL Port (3306) Exposed',
        description: 'MySQL database port is accessible from external network',
        action: 'Bind MySQL to localhost (127.0.0.1) or configure firewall',
        autoApplicable: false,
        risk: 'Database can be attacked from external network'
      },
      5432: {
        category: 'security',
        priority: 'high',
        title: 'PostgreSQL Port (5432) Exposed',
        description: 'PostgreSQL database port is accessible from external network',
        action: 'Bind PostgreSQL to localhost or configure firewall',
        autoApplicable: false,
        risk: 'Database can be attacked from external network'
      },
      6379: {
        category: 'security',
        priority: 'critical',
        title: 'Redis Port (6379) Exposed',
        description: 'Redis has no authentication by default and is exposed',
        action: 'Bind Redis to localhost, enable authentication, and configure firewall',
        autoApplicable: false,
        risk: 'Anyone can access and modify your data'
      },
      27017: {
        category: 'security',
        priority: 'high',
        title: 'MongoDB Port (27017) Exposed',
        description: 'MongoDB port is accessible from external network',
        action: 'Bind MongoDB to localhost or enable authentication',
        autoApplicable: false,
        risk: 'Database can be accessed without proper authentication'
      }
    };

    return securityRisks[port] || null;
  }

  /**
   * Scan for specific port
   */
  async isPortOpen(port: number): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const { stdout } = await execAsync(`netstat -tulpn 2>/dev/null | grep :${port} || ss -tulpn 2>/dev/null | grep :${port}`);
      return stdout.trim().length > 0;
    } catch (error) {
      return false;
    }
  }
}
-- File: scan-scheduler.ts
/**
 * 鎵弿浠诲姟璋冨害鍣?
 * 
 * 绠＄悊鎵弿浠诲姟鐨勮皟搴︺€佹墽琛屽拰缁撴灉瀛樺偍
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { PortScanner } from './port-scanner';
import { WebScanner } from './web-scanner';
import { SystemScanner } from './system-scanner';
import { NucleiScanner, VulnerabilityResult, ScanProgress } from './nuclei-scanner';
import { getDatabase } from '../config/database';

export interface ScanTask {
    id: string;
    agentId?: string;
    target: string;
    type: 'port' | 'web' | 'system' | 'nuclei' | 'full';
    status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'completed' | 'failed';
    progress: number;
    createdAt: Date;
    startedAt?: Date;
    completedAt?: Date;
    results?: any;
    error?: string;
}

export class ScanScheduler extends EventEmitter {
    private tasks: Map<string, ScanTask> = new Map();
    private queue: string[] = [];
    private isProcessing: boolean = false;
    private maxConcurrent: number = 3;
    private runningCount: number = 0;

    // 鎵弿鍣ㄥ疄渚?
    private portScanner = new PortScanner();
    private webScanner = new WebScanner();
    private systemScanner = new SystemScanner();
    private nucleiScanner = new NucleiScanner();

    constructor() {
        super();
        this.initializeDatabase();
    }

    /**
     * 鍒濆鍖栨暟鎹簱琛?
     */
    private initializeDatabase(): void {
        try {
            const db = getDatabase().getDb();

            db.exec(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scan_results (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          agent_id TEXT,
          target TEXT NOT NULL,
          scan_type TEXT NOT NULL,
          status TEXT NOT NULL,
          progress INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
          results TEXT,
          error TEXT,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          started_at TEXT,
          completed_at TEXT
        )
      `);

            db.exec(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vulnerabilities (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          scan_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          template_id TEXT,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          severity TEXT NOT NULL,
          host TEXT,
          matched TEXT,
          description TEXT,
          timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
          FOREIGN KEY (scan_id) REFERENCES scan_results(id)
        )
      `);

            logger.info('Scan database tables initialized');
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error('Failed to initialize scan database', { error: error.message });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓鎵弿浠诲姟
     */
    createTask(
        target: string,
        type: ScanTask['type'],
        agentId?: string
    ): ScanTask {
        const task: ScanTask = {
            id: `scan-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`,
            agentId,
            target,
            type,
            status: 'pending',
            progress: 0,
            createdAt: new Date()
        };

        this.tasks.set(task.id, task);
        this.queue.push(task.id);

        // 淇濆瓨鍒版暟鎹簱
        this.saveTask(task);

        logger.info(`Scan task created: ${task.id}`, { target, type });
        this.emit('task:created', task);

        // 灏濊瘯澶勭悊闃熷垪
        this.processQueue();

        return task;
    }

    /**
     * 澶勭悊浠诲姟闃熷垪
     */
    private async processQueue(): Promise<void> {
        if (this.isProcessing || this.runningCount >= this.maxConcurrent) {
            return;
        }

        this.isProcessing = true;

        while (this.queue.length > 0 && this.runningCount < this.maxConcurrent) {
            const taskId = this.queue.shift();
            if (!taskId) continue;

            const task = this.tasks.get(taskId);
            if (!task || task.status !== 'pending') continue;

            this.runningCount++;
            this.executeTask(task).finally(() => {
                this.runningCount--;
                this.processQueue();
            });
        }

        this.isProcessing = false;
    }

    /**
     * 鎵ц鎵弿浠诲姟
     */
    private async executeTask(task: ScanTask): Promise<void> {
        task.status = 'running';
        task.startedAt = new Date();
        this.updateTask(task);
        this.emit('task:started', task);

        try {
            let results: any;

            switch (task.type) {
                case 'port':
                    results = await this.runPortScan(task);
                    break;
                case 'web':
                    results = await this.runWebScan(task);
                    break;
                case 'system':
                    results = await this.runSystemScan(task);
                    break;
                case 'nuclei':
                    results = await this.runNucleiScan(task);
                    break;
                case 'full':
                    results = await this.runFullScan(task);
                    break;
            }

            task.status = 'completed';
            task.progress = 100;
            task.results = results;
            task.completedAt = new Date();

            logger.info(`Scan task completed: ${task.id}`);
            this.emit('task:completed', task);

        } catch (error: any) {
            task.status = 'failed';
            task.error = error.message;
            task.completedAt = new Date();

            logger.error(`Scan task failed: ${task.id}`, { error: error.message });
            this.emit('task:failed', task);
        }

        this.updateTask(task);
    }

    /**
     * 杩愯绔彛鎵弿
     */
    private async runPortScan(task: ScanTask): Promise<any> {
        task.progress = 10;
        this.updateTask(task);

        const results = await this.portScanner.scanCommonPorts();

        task.progress = 100;
        return { recommendations: results };
    }

    /**
     * 杩愯 Web 鎵弿
     */
    private async runWebScan(task: ScanTask): Promise<any> {
        task.progress = 10;
        this.updateTask(task);

        const results = await this.webScanner.scanWebServer();

        task.progress = 100;
        return results;
    }

    /**
     * 杩愯绯荤粺鎵弿
     */
    private async runSystemScan(task: ScanTask): Promise<any> {
        task.progress = 10;
        this.updateTask(task);

        const results = await this.systemScanner.scanSystem();

        task.progress = 100;
        return results;
    }

    /**
     * 杩愯 Nuclei 鎵弿
     */
    private async runNucleiScan(task: ScanTask): Promise<any> {
        const onProgress = (progress: ScanProgress) => {
            task.progress = progress.progress;
            this.updateTask(task);
            this.emit('task:progress', task, progress);
        };

        const vulnerabilities = await this.nucleiScanner.quickScan(task.target, onProgress);

        // 淇濆瓨婕忔礊缁撴灉
        this.saveVulnerabilities(task.id, vulnerabilities);

        return {
            vulnerabilities,
            summary: this.nucleiScanner.getSummary(vulnerabilities)
        };
    }

    /**
     * 杩愯瀹屾暣鎵弿
     */
    private async runFullScan(task: ScanTask): Promise<any> {
        const results: any = {
            port: null,
            web: null,
            system: null,
            nuclei: null
        };

        // 绔彛鎵弿 (0-25%)
        task.progress = 5;
        this.updateTask(task);
        results.port = await this.portScanner.scanCommonPorts();

        // Web 鎵弿 (25-50%)
        task.progress = 25;
        this.updateTask(task);
        results.web = await this.webScanner.scanWebServer();

        // 绯荤粺鎵弿 (50-75%)
        task.progress = 50;
        this.updateTask(task);
        results.system = await this.systemScanner.scanSystem();

        // Nuclei 鎵弿 (75-100%)
        task.progress = 75;
        this.updateTask(task);

        const onProgress = (progress: ScanProgress) => {
            task.progress = 75 + Math.round(progress.progress * 0.25);
            this.updateTask(task);
        };

        try {
            const vulnerabilities = await this.nucleiScanner.quickScan(task.target, onProgress);
            results.nuclei = {
                vulnerabilities,
                summary: this.nucleiScanner.getSummary(vulnerabilities)
            };
            this.saveVulnerabilities(task.id, vulnerabilities);
        } catch (error: any) {
            results.nuclei = { error: error.message };
        }

        return results;
    }

    /**
     * 淇濆瓨浠诲姟鍒版暟鎹簱
     */
    private saveTask(task: ScanTask): void {
        try {
            const db = getDatabase().getDb();

            db.prepare(`
        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO scan_results 
        (id, agent_id, target, scan_type, status, progress, results, error, created_at, started_at, completed_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `).run(
                task.id,
                task.agentId || null,
                task.target,
                task.type,
                task.status,
                task.progress,
                task.results ? JSON.stringify(task.results) : null,
                task.error || null,
                task.createdAt.toISOString(),
                task.startedAt?.toISOString() || null,
                task.completedAt?.toISOString() || null
            );
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error('Failed to save scan task', { error: error.message });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鏇存柊浠诲姟
     */
    private updateTask(task: ScanTask): void {
        this.tasks.set(task.id, task);
        this.saveTask(task);
        this.emit('task:updated', task);
    }

    /**
     * 淇濆瓨婕忔礊缁撴灉
     */
    private saveVulnerabilities(scanId: string, vulnerabilities: VulnerabilityResult[]): void {
        try {
            const db = getDatabase().getDb();
            const stmt = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO vulnerabilities 
        (scan_id, template_id, name, severity, host, matched, description, timestamp)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);

            for (const vuln of vulnerabilities) {
                stmt.run(
                    scanId,
                    vuln.templateId,
                    vuln.name,
                    vuln.severity,
                    vuln.host,
                    vuln.matched,
                    vuln.description || null,
                    vuln.timestamp.toISOString()
                );
            }
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error('Failed to save vulnerabilities', { error: error.message });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇浠诲姟
     */
    getTask(taskId: string): ScanTask | undefined {
        return this.tasks.get(taskId);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈変换鍔?
     */
    getAllTasks(): ScanTask[] {
        return Array.from(this.tasks.values());
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍘嗗彶鎵弿缁撴灉
     */
    getHistory(limit: number = 50): ScanTask[] {
        try {
            const db = getDatabase().getDb();
            const rows = db.prepare(`
        SELECT * FROM scan_results 
        ORDER BY created_at DESC 
        LIMIT ?
      `).all(limit);

            return rows.map((row: any) => ({
                id: row.id,
                agentId: row.agent_id,
                target: row.target,
                type: row.scan_type,
                status: row.status,
                progress: row.progress,
                results: row.results ? JSON.parse(row.results) : null,
                error: row.error,
                createdAt: new Date(row.created_at),
                startedAt: row.started_at ? new Date(row.started_at) : undefined,
                completedAt: row.completed_at ? new Date(row.completed_at) : undefined
            }));
        } catch (error: any) {
            logger.error('Failed to get scan history', { error: error.message });
            return [];
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鍙栨秷浠诲姟
     */
    cancelTask(taskId: string): boolean {
        const task = this.tasks.get(taskId);
        if (!task || task.status !== 'pending') {
            return false;
        }

        task.status = 'failed';
        task.error = 'Cancelled by user';
        this.updateTask(task);

        // 浠庨槦鍒椾腑绉婚櫎
        const index = this.queue.indexOf(taskId);
        if (index > -1) {
            this.queue.splice(index, 1);
        }

        return true;
    }
}

// 瀵煎嚭鍗曚緥
export const scanScheduler = new ScanScheduler();
-- File: smart-fixer.ts
import { AIEngine, AIAnalysisRequest, AIAnalysisResponse } from './ai-engine';
import { ExecutionMonitor, ExecutionOptions, ExecutionResult } from './execution-monitor';
import { Recommendation } from '../database/models/scan-result';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

/**
 * Smart Fixer
 * AI-powered intelligent problem fixing with automatic retry and adaptation
 */

export interface FixAttempt {
  attemptNumber: number;
  solution: any;
  executionResult: ExecutionResult;
  aiDiagnosis?: any;
  timestamp: number;
}

export interface FixResult {
  success: boolean;
  recommendation: Recommendation;
  attempts: FixAttempt[];
  finalSolution?: string;
  totalTime: number;
  aiInsights?: {
    originalDiagnosis: any;
    adaptations: string[];
    lessonsLearned: string[];
  };
}

export interface SmartFixerOptions {
  maxAttempts?: number;
  enableAI?: boolean;
  aiProvider?: {
    name: 'openai' | 'anthropic' | 'ollama' | 'zhipu' | 'custom';
    apiKey?: string;
    baseURL?: string;
    model: string;
  };
  executionTimeout?: number;
  enableRealTimeMonitoring?: boolean;
}

export class SmartFixer {
  private aiEngine: AIEngine | null = null;
  private options: Required<SmartFixerOptions>;

  constructor(options: SmartFixerOptions = {}) {
    this.options = {
      maxAttempts: options.maxAttempts || 3,
      enableAI: options.enableAI !== false,
      aiProvider: options.aiProvider || {
        name: 'ollama',
        model: 'llama2'
      },
      executionTimeout: options.executionTimeout || 60000,
      enableRealTimeMonitoring: options.enableRealTimeMonitoring !== false
    };

    if (this.options.enableAI) {
      this.aiEngine = new AIEngine(this.options.aiProvider);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Fix a problem intelligently with AI guidance
   */
  async fixProblem(
    recommendation: Recommendation,
    context: any = {}
  ): Promise<FixResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const attempts: FixAttempt[] = [];
    const aiInsights: any = {
      originalDiagnosis: null,
      adaptations: [],
      lessonsLearned: []
    };

    logger.info(`Starting smart fix for: ${recommendation.title}`);

    try {
      // Step 1: AI Analysis (if enabled)
      let aiAnalysis: AIAnalysisResponse | null = null;

      if (this.aiEngine) {
        logger.info('Running AI analysis...');

        const analysisRequest: AIAnalysisRequest = {
          problem: {
            title: recommendation.title,
            description: recommendation.description,
            category: recommendation.category,
            priority: recommendation.priority
          },
          context: {
            systemInfo: context.systemInfo,
            logs: context.logs || [],
            configFiles: context.configFiles || {}
          }
        };

        aiAnalysis = await this.aiEngine.analyzeProblem(analysisRequest);
        aiInsights.originalDiagnosis = aiAnalysis.diagnosis;

        logger.info('AI Analysis complete', {
          rootCause: aiAnalysis.diagnosis.rootCause,
          severity: aiAnalysis.diagnosis.severity,
          confidence: aiAnalysis.diagnosis.confidence
        });
      }

      // Step 2: Try solutions with AI guidance
      let success = false;
      let attemptNumber = 0;

      while (!success && attemptNumber < this.options.maxAttempts) {
        attemptNumber++;

        // Get solution to try
        const solution = this.selectSolution(attemptNumber, recommendation, aiAnalysis, attempts);
        logger.info(`Attempt ${attemptNumber}: ${solution.explanation}`);

        // Execute with monitoring
        const executionResult = await this.executeSolution(solution, attempts);

        const fixAttempt: FixAttempt = {
          attemptNumber,
          solution,
          executionResult,
          timestamp: Date.now()
        };

        attempts.push(fixAttempt);

        // Check if successful
        if (executionResult.success) {
          success = true;
          logger.info(`鉁?Fix successful on attempt ${attemptNumber}`);

          aiInsights.lessonsLearned.push(
            `Solution "${solution.explanation}" successfully fixed the problem`
          );

          break;
        }

        // If failed and AI is enabled, get diagnosis
        if (!success && this.aiEngine && attemptNumber < this.options.maxAttempts) {
          logger.info('Attempt failed, requesting AI diagnosis...');

          try {
            const diagnosis = await this.aiEngine.diagnoseFailure(
              recommendation,
              solution.command,
              executionResult.stdout,
              executionResult.stderr
            );

            fixAttempt.aiDiagnosis = diagnosis;

            logger.info('AI Diagnosis:', {
              diagnosis: diagnosis.diagnosis,
              alternative: diagnosis.alternativeSolution
            });

            aiInsights.adaptations.push(
              `Attempt ${attemptNumber} failed: ${diagnosis.diagnosis}. ` +
              `Trying alternative: ${diagnosis.alternativeSolution}`
            );

            // Add adaptive solution for next attempt
            if (attemptNumber < this.options.maxAttempts) {
              // Store the alternative for next attempt
              aiAnalysis = {
                ...aiAnalysis!,
                solutions: [
                  {
                    command: diagnosis.alternativeSolution,
                    explanation: diagnosis.explanation,
                    riskLevel: 'medium',
                    estimatedSuccess: 70,
                    prerequisites: []
                  }
                ]
              };
            }
          } catch (error) {
            logger.error('AI diagnosis failed:', { error });
          }
        }

        // Wait before retry (exponential backoff)
        if (attemptNumber < this.options.maxAttempts) {
          const waitTime = Math.pow(2, attemptNumber) * 1000;
          logger.info(`Waiting ${waitTime}ms before retry...`);
          await this.sleep(waitTime);
        }
      }

      // Prepare result
      const result: FixResult = {
        success,
        recommendation,
        attempts,
        finalSolution: success ? attempts[attempts.length - 1].solution.command : undefined,
        totalTime: Date.now() - startTime
      };

      if (this.options.enableAI) {
        result.aiInsights = aiInsights;
      }

      return result;

    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Smart fix failed with error:', error);

      return {
        success: false,
        recommendation,
        attempts,
        totalTime: Date.now() - startTime,
        aiInsights
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Select solution for current attempt
   */
  private selectSolution(
    attemptNumber: number,
    recommendation: Recommendation,
    aiAnalysis: AIAnalysisResponse | null,
    previousAttempts: FixAttempt[]
  ): any {
    // If AI analysis available and has solutions, use AI's solutions
    if (aiAnalysis && aiAnalysis.solutions && aiAnalysis.solutions.length > 0) {
      const solutionIndex = Math.min(attemptNumber - 1, aiAnalysis.solutions.length - 1);
      return aiAnalysis.solutions[solutionIndex];
    }

    // Fallback to original recommendation
    return {
      command: recommendation.action,
      explanation: recommendation.description,
      riskLevel: recommendation.priority === 'critical' ? 'high' : 'medium',
      estimatedSuccess: 70
    };
  }

  /**
   * Execute a solution with monitoring
   */
  private async executeSolution(
    solution: any,
    previousAttempts: FixAttempt[]
  ): Promise<ExecutionResult> {
    const monitor = new ExecutionMonitor();

    // Setup event listeners for real-time monitoring
    if (this.options.enableRealTimeMonitoring) {
      monitor.on('execution-event', (event) => {
        logger.debug('Execution event:', event);

        // Send to WebSocket if available
        this.emitToWebSocket('execution-event', event);
      });
    }

    const options: ExecutionOptions = {
      timeout: this.options.executionTimeout,
      aiEngine: this.aiEngine || undefined,
      enableAIMonitoring: this.options.enableRealTimeMonitoring && !!this.aiEngine
    };

    try {
      // Execute prerequisites first
      if (solution.prerequisites && solution.prerequisites.length > 0) {
        logger.info('Running prerequisites...');

        for (const prereq of solution.prerequisites) {
          await monitor.execute(prereq, options);
        }
      }

      // Execute main command
      const result = await monitor.execute(solution.command, options);

      return result;
    } catch (error: any) {
      // Execution failed (error is the ExecutionResult)
      return error as ExecutionResult;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Emit event to WebSocket clients
   */
  private emitToWebSocket(event: string, data: any): void {
    // This would be connected to the WebSocket server in index.ts
    // For now, just log
    logger.debug(`WebSocket event [${event}]:`, data);
  }

  /**
   * Sleep utility
   */
  private sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }

  /**
   * Batch fix multiple problems
   */
  async batchFix(
    recommendations: Recommendation[],
    context: any = {}
  ): Promise<FixResult[]> {
    logger.info(`Batch fixing ${recommendations.length} problems...`);

    const results: FixResult[] = [];

    // Sort by priority
    const sorted = [...recommendations].sort((a, b) => {
      const priorityOrder = { critical: 0, high: 1, medium: 2, low: 3 };
      return priorityOrder[a.priority] - priorityOrder[b.priority];
    });

    for (const rec of sorted) {
      logger.info(`Fixing: ${rec.title}`);

      const result = await this.fixProblem(rec, context);
      results.push(result);

      // Wait between fixes to avoid overwhelming the system
      await this.sleep(2000);
    }

    return results;
  }

  /**
   * Dry run - simulate fixing without executing
   */
  async dryFix(
    recommendation: Recommendation,
    context: any = {}
  ): Promise<{
    aiAnalysis: AIAnalysisResponse | null;
    plannedAttempts: any[];
    estimatedTime: number;
    risks: string[];
  }> {
    logger.info(`Dry run for: ${recommendation.title}`);

    const aiAnalysis = this.aiEngine ?
      await this.aiEngine.analyzeProblem({
        problem: {
          title: recommendation.title,
          description: recommendation.description,
          category: recommendation.category,
          priority: recommendation.priority
        },
        context: {
          systemInfo: context.systemInfo,
          logs: context.logs || [],
          configFiles: context.configFiles || {}
        }
      }) :
      null;

    const plannedAttempts = aiAnalysis ?
      aiAnalysis.solutions.map((sol, idx) => ({
        attemptNumber: idx + 1,
        solution: sol,
        estimatedSuccess: sol.estimatedSuccess
      })) :
      [{
        attemptNumber: 1,
        solution: {
          command: recommendation.action,
          explanation: recommendation.description
        },
        estimatedSuccess: 70
      }];

    const risks: string[] = [];

    if (aiAnalysis && aiAnalysis.warnings) {
      risks.push(...aiAnalysis.warnings);
    }

    const estimatedTime = plannedAttempts.length * 30000; // 30s per attempt

    return {
      aiAnalysis,
      plannedAttempts,
      estimatedTime,
      risks
    };
  }
}
-- File: system-scanner.ts
import { exec } from 'child_process';
import { promisify } from 'util';
import os from 'os';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { Recommendation } from '../database/models/scan-result';

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

/**
 * System Scanner
 * Scans system configuration and vulnerabilities
 */
export class SystemScanner {
  /**
   * Scan system information
   */
  async scanSystem(): Promise<{ info: any; recommendations: Recommendation[] }> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];
    const info: any = {};

    try {
      // Get OS information
      info.platform = os.platform();
      info.arch = os.arch();
      info.release = os.release();
      info.cpus = os.cpus().length;
      info.totalMemory = `${Math.round(os.totalmem() / 1024 / 1024 / 1024)}GB`;
      info.uptime = `${Math.round(os.uptime() / 3600)}h`;

      // Check system updates
      const updateRecommendations = await this.checkSystemUpdates();
      recommendations.push(...updateRecommendations);

      // Check system limits
      const limitRecommendations = await this.checkSystemLimits();
      recommendations.push(...limitRecommendations);

      // Check disk space
      const diskRecommendations = await this.checkDiskSpace();
      recommendations.push(...diskRecommendations);

      // Check firewall status
      const firewallRecommendations = await this.checkFirewall();
      recommendations.push(...firewallRecommendations);

      logger.info('System scan complete');
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('System scan failed', { error });
    }

    return { info, recommendations };
  }

  /**
   * Check for system updates
   */
  private async checkSystemUpdates(): Promise<Recommendation[]> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];

    try {
      if (os.platform() === 'linux') {
        // Check for Debian/Ubuntu updates
        try {
          const { stdout } = await execAsync('apt list --upgradable 2>/dev/null | grep -c "upgradable" || echo "0"');
          const updateCount = parseInt(stdout.trim());

          if (updateCount > 0) {
            recommendations.push({
              category: 'security',
              priority: 'high',
              title: `${updateCount} System Updates Available`,
              description: 'Your system has pending security updates',
              action: 'sudo apt update && sudo apt upgrade -y',
              autoApplicable: true,
              risk: 'Security vulnerabilities may be exposed'
            });
          }
        } catch (error) {
          // apt not available, might be different distro
        }

        // Check for RHEL/CentOS updates
        try {
          const { stdout } = await execAsync('yum check-update 2>/dev/null | grep -c "^" || echo "0"');
          const updateCount = parseInt(stdout.trim());

          if (updateCount > 10) {
            recommendations.push({
              category: 'security',
              priority: 'high',
              title: 'System Updates Available',
              description: 'Your system has pending updates',
              action: 'sudo yum update -y',
              autoApplicable: true,
              risk: 'Security vulnerabilities may be exposed'
            });
          }
        } catch (error) {
          // yum not available
        }
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to check system updates', { error });
    }

    return recommendations;
  }

  /**
   * Check system limits
   */
  private async checkSystemLimits(): Promise<Recommendation[]> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];

    try {
      // Check file descriptor limit
      const { stdout: ulimitOutput } = await execAsync('ulimit -n');
      const fileDescriptorLimit = parseInt(ulimitOutput.trim());

      if (fileDescriptorLimit < 65535) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'performance',
          priority: 'medium',
          title: 'File Descriptor Limit Too Low',
          description: `Current limit: ${fileDescriptorLimit}, Recommended: 65535`,
          action: 'ulimit -n 65535 (add to /etc/security/limits.conf for permanent)',
          autoApplicable: false,
          risk: 'May hit "too many open files" error under high load'
        });
      }

      // Check max user processes
      const { stdout: uLimitOutput } = await execAsync('ulimit -u');
      const maxProcesses = parseInt(uLimitOutput.trim());

      if (maxProcesses < 4096) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'performance',
          priority: 'low',
          title: 'Max User Processes Limit',
          description: `Current limit: ${maxProcesses}, Recommended: 4096+`,
          action: 'ulimit -u 4096',
          autoApplicable: false,
          risk: 'May prevent spawning new processes under load'
        });
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to check system limits', { error });
    }

    return recommendations;
  }

  /**
   * Check disk space
   */
  private async checkDiskSpace(): Promise<Recommendation[]> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];

    try {
      const { stdout } = await execAsync('df -h / | tail -1');

      const parts = stdout.split(/\s+/);
      const usedPercent = parseInt(parts[4].replace('%', ''));

      if (usedPercent > 80) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'reliability',
          priority: 'critical',
          title: 'Disk Space Almost Full',
          description: `Root partition is ${usedPercent}% full`,
          action: 'Clean up logs, remove old backups, or expand disk',
          autoApplicable: false,
          risk: 'System may crash when disk is full'
        });
      } else if (usedPercent > 60) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'reliability',
          priority: 'medium',
          title: 'Disk Space Warning',
          description: `Root partition is ${usedPercent}% full`,
          action: 'Monitor disk usage and plan cleanup',
          autoApplicable: false,
          risk: 'System may run out of disk space soon'
        });
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to check disk space', { error });
    }

    return recommendations;
  }

  /**
   * Check firewall status
   */
  private async checkFirewall(): Promise<Recommendation[]> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];

    try {
      // Check if UFW is installed (Ubuntu/Debian)
      try {
        const { stdout } = await execAsync('ufw status');
        if (stdout.includes('inactive')) {
          recommendations.push({
            category: 'security',
            priority: 'high',
            title: 'Firewall is Inactive',
            description: 'UFW firewall is not enabled',
            action: 'sudo ufw enable',
            autoApplicable: true,
            risk: 'Server is exposed to all incoming connections'
          });
        }
      } catch (error) {
        // UFW not installed
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'security',
          priority: 'high',
          title: 'No Firewall Detected',
          description: 'Consider installing and configuring a firewall',
          action: 'sudo apt install ufw && sudo ufw enable',
          autoApplicable: true,
          risk: 'Server is exposed to all incoming connections'
        });
      }

      // Check if firewalld is running (RHEL/CentOS)
      try {
        await execAsync('firewall-cmd --state 2>/dev/null');
        // firewalld is active, good
      } catch (error) {
        // firewalld not active
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to check firewall', { error });
    }

    return recommendations;
  }

  /**
   * Get installed software packages
   */
  async getInstalledPackages(): Promise<string[]> {
    const packages: string[] = [];

    try {
      if (os.platform() === 'linux') {
        // Try dpkg (Debian/Ubuntu)
        try {
          const { stdout } = await execAsync("dpkg -l | grep '^ii' | awk '{print $2}'");
          packages.push(...stdout.split('\n').filter(p => p.trim()));
        } catch (error) {
          // Try rpm (RHEL/CentOS)
          try {
            const { stdout } = await execAsync('rpm -qa');
            packages.push(...stdout.split('\n').filter(p => p.trim()));
          } catch (error) {
            // Neither dpkg nor rpm available
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Failed to get installed packages', { error });
    }

    return packages;
  }
}
-- File: web-scanner.ts
import { exec } from 'child_process';
import { promisify } from 'util';
import fs from 'fs/promises';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { Recommendation } from '../database/models/scan-result';

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

/**
 * Web Server Scanner
 * Scans Nginx/Apache configuration
 */
export class WebScanner {
  /**
   * Scan web server configuration
   */
  async scanWebServer(): Promise<{ server: string | null; recommendations: Recommendation[] }> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];
    let server: string | null = null;

    // Check for Nginx
    const nginxRecs = await this.checkNginx();
    if (nginxRecs) {
      server = 'nginx';
      recommendations.push(...nginxRecs);
    }

    // Check for Apache
    const apacheRecs = await this.checkApache();
    if (apacheRecs) {
      server = server === null ? 'apache' : 'both';
      recommendations.push(...apacheRecs);
    }

    logger.info(`Web server scan complete. Detected: ${server || 'none'}`);

    return { server, recommendations };
  }

  /**
   * Check Nginx configuration
   */
  private async checkNginx(): Promise<Recommendation[] | null> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];

    try {
      // Check if Nginx is installed
      await execAsync('which nginx');
    } catch (error) {
      return null; // Nginx not installed
    }

    try {
      // Check Nginx version
      const { stdout: versionOutput } = await execAsync('nginx -v 2>&1');
      const version = versionOutput.match(/nginx\/(\d+\.\d+\.\d+)/)?.[1];

      // Check if running
      try {
        await execAsync('pgrep nginx');
      } catch (error) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'reliability',
          priority: 'high',
          title: 'Nginx is Not Running',
          description: 'Nginx is installed but not running',
          action: 'sudo systemctl start nginx',
          autoApplicable: true,
          risk: 'Web server is not serving content'
        });
        return recommendations;
      }

      // Test configuration
      try {
        await execAsync('nginx -t');
      } catch (error: any) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'reliability',
          priority: 'critical',
          title: 'Nginx Configuration Error',
          description: 'Nginx configuration test failed',
          action: 'Check nginx -t output and fix configuration',
          autoApplicable: false,
          risk: 'Nginx may fail to reload'
        });
      }

      // Check if gzip is enabled
      const { stdout: config } = await execAsync('cat /etc/nginx/nginx.conf');
      if (!config.includes('gzip on')) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'performance',
          priority: 'medium',
          title: 'Gzip Compression Not Enabled',
          description: 'Enabling gzip can significantly reduce bandwidth',
          action: 'Add "gzip on;" to nginx.conf',
          autoApplicable: true,
          risk: 'Increased bandwidth usage'
        });
      }

      // Check if HTTP/2 is enabled
      if (!config.includes('http2')) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'performance',
          priority: 'low',
          title: 'HTTP/2 Not Enabled',
          description: 'HTTP/2 improves performance for modern browsers',
          action: 'Add "http2" to listen directive',
          autoApplicable: false,
          risk: 'Suboptimal performance'
        });
      }

      // Check worker processes
      const workerMatch = config.match(/worker_processes\s+(\d+|auto);/);
      if (workerMatch) {
        const workers = workerMatch[1];
        if (workers !== 'auto' && parseInt(workers) < 4) {
          recommendations.push({
            category: 'performance',
            priority: 'low',
            title: 'Nginx Worker Processes',
            description: `Current: ${workers}, Recommended: auto`,
            action: 'Set "worker_processes auto;" in nginx.conf',
            autoApplicable: true,
            risk: 'Suboptimal performance'
          });
        }
      }

      // Check client body size limit
      if (!config.includes('client_max_body_size')) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'performance',
          priority: 'low',
          title: 'Missing Client Body Size Limit',
          description: 'No limit on upload file size',
          action: 'Add "client_max_body_size 100M;" to http block',
          autoApplicable: true,
          risk: 'Potential disk space exhaustion from large uploads'
        });
      }

      // Check keepalive timeout
      const keepaliveMatch = config.match(/keepalive_timeout\s+(\d+);/);
      if (keepaliveMatch) {
        const timeout = parseInt(keepaliveMatch[1]);
        if (timeout > 75) {
          recommendations.push({
            category: 'performance',
            priority: 'low',
            title: 'Keepalive Timeout Too High',
            description: `Current: ${timeout}s, Recommended: 65s`,
            action: 'Set "keepalive_timeout 65;"',
            autoApplicable: true,
            risk: 'Connections may stay open too long'
          });
        }
      }

    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Error checking Nginx', { error });
    }

    return recommendations;
  }

  /**
   * Check Apache configuration
   */
  private async checkApache(): Promise<Recommendation[] | null> {
    const recommendations: Recommendation[] = [];

    try {
      // Check if Apache is installed
      await execAsync('which apachectl || which apache2');
    } catch (error) {
      return null; // Apache not installed
    }

    try {
      // Check Apache version
      const { stdout: versionOutput } = await execAsync('apachectl -v 2>/dev/null || apache2 -v 2>/dev/null');
      const version = versionOutput.match(/Apache\/(\d+\.\d+\.\d+)/)?.[1];

      // Check if running
      try {
        await execAsync('pgrep apache');
      } catch (error) {
        try {
          await execAsync('pgrep httpd');
        } catch (error2) {
          recommendations.push({
            category: 'reliability',
            priority: 'high',
            title: 'Apache is Not Running',
            description: 'Apache is installed but not running',
            action: 'sudo systemctl start apache2 || sudo systemctl start httpd',
            autoApplicable: true,
            risk: 'Web server is not serving content'
          });
          return recommendations;
        }
      }

      // Test configuration
      try {
        await execAsync('apachectl configtest 2>/dev/null || apache2ctl configtest 2>/dev/null');
      } catch (error: any) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'reliability',
          priority: 'critical',
          title: 'Apache Configuration Error',
          description: 'Apache configuration test failed',
          action: 'Check configtest output and fix configuration',
          autoApplicable: false,
          risk: 'Apache may fail to reload'
        });
      }

      // Check for mod_deflate (compression)
      try {
        await execAsync('apachectl -M 2>/dev/null | grep deflate || apache2ctl -M 2>/dev/null | grep deflate');
      } catch (error) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'performance',
          priority: 'medium',
          title: 'Mod Deflate Not Enabled',
          description: 'Compression module not enabled',
          action: 'sudo a2enmod deflate && sudo systemctl restart apache',
          autoApplicable: true,
          risk: 'Increased bandwidth usage'
        });
      }

      // Check for mod_http2
      try {
        await execAsync('apachectl -M 2>/dev/null | grep http2 || apache2ctl -M 2>/dev/null | grep http2');
      } catch (error) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'performance',
          priority: 'low',
          title: 'HTTP/2 Not Enabled',
          description: 'HTTP/2 module not enabled',
          action: 'sudo a2enmod http2 && sudo systemctl restart apache',
          autoApplicable: true,
          risk: 'Suboptimal performance for modern browsers'
        });
      }

      // Check for mod_security
      try {
        await execAsync('apachectl -M 2>/dev/null | grep security || apache2ctl -M 2>/dev/null | grep security');
      } catch (error) {
        recommendations.push({
          category: 'security',
          priority: 'medium',
          title: 'Mod Security Not Installed',
          description: 'Web Application Firewall (WAF) not enabled',
          action: 'sudo a2enmod security2 && sudo systemctl restart apache',
          autoApplicable: true,
          risk: 'Increased vulnerability to web attacks'
        });
      }

    } catch (error: any) {
      logger.error('Error checking Apache', { error });
    }

    return recommendations;
  }

  /**
   * Get Nginx configuration
   */
  async getNginxConfig(): Promise<string | null> {
    try {
      const { stdout } = await execAsync('cat /etc/nginx/nginx.conf');
      return stdout;
    } catch (error) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get Apache configuration
   */
  async getApacheConfig(): Promise<string | null> {
    try {
      const { stdout } = await execAsync('cat /etc/apache2/apache2.conf 2>/dev/null || cat /etc/httpd/conf/httpd.conf 2>/dev/null');
      return stdout;
    } catch (error) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}
-- File: zhipu-client.ts
import axios from 'axios';

/**
 * Zhipu AI (BigModel) Client
 * GLM-4 series models
 */

export class ZhipuClient {
  private apiKey: string;
  private baseURL: string;

  constructor(apiKey: string, baseURL: string = 'https://open.bigmodel.cn/api/anthropic') {
    this.apiKey = apiKey;
    this.baseURL = baseURL;
  }

  /**
   * Generate completion using GLM model
   */
  async generateCompletion(prompt: string, model: string = 'glm-4'): Promise<string> {
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(
        `${this.baseURL}/v1/messages`,
        {
          model: model,
          max_tokens: 2000,
          messages: [
            {
              role: 'user',
              content: prompt
            }
          ]
        },
        {
          headers: {
            'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
          },
          timeout: 60000 // 60 seconds timeout
        }
      );

      // Extract response text
      if (response.data && response.data.content && response.data.content[0]) {
        return response.data.content[0].text;
      }

      throw new Error('Invalid response format from Zhipu AI');
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Zhipu AI API error:', error.response?.data || error.message);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Chat completion with conversation history
   */
  async chatCompletion(
    messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>,
    model: string = 'glm-4'
  ): Promise<string> {
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(
        `${this.baseURL}/v1/messages`,
        {
          model: model,
          max_tokens: 2000,
          messages: messages
        },
        {
          headers: {
            'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
          },
          timeout: 60000
        }
      );

      return response.data.content[0].text;
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Zhipu AI chat error:', error.response?.data || error.message);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Test API connection
   */
  async testConnection(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const response = await this.generateCompletion('Hello', 'glm-4');
      return !!response;
    } catch (error) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get available models
   */
  getAvailableModels(): string[] {
    return [
      'glm-4',           // Latest GLM-4 model
      'glm-4-flash',     // Faster version
      'glm-4-air',       // Lightweight version
      'glm-4-plus',      // Enhanced version
      'glm-3-turbo'      // Previous generation
    ];
  }
}

/**
 * Quick test function
 */
export async function testZhipuAI(apiKey: string): Promise<{
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  latency?: number;
}> {
  const startTime = Date.now();

  try {
    const client = new ZhipuClient(apiKey);
    const response = await client.generateCompletion('浣犲ソ锛岃绠€鍗曚粙缁嶄竴涓嬭嚜宸便€?);

    const latency = Date.now() - startTime;

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Zhipu AI connection successful',
      latency
    };
  } catch (error: any) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: error.message || 'Connection failed'
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Smart Fixer with Zhipu AI (BigModel) Integration
 */

export interface SmartFixerOptionsZhipu {
  maxAttempts?: number;
  enableAI?: boolean;
  aiProvider?: {
    name: 'openai' | 'anthropic' | 'ollama' | 'zhipu';
    apiKey?: string;
    baseURL?: string;
    model: string;
  };
  executionTimeout?: number;
  enableRealTimeMonitoring?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Create Smart Fixer optimized for Zhipu AI
 */
export function createZhipuSmartFixer(apiKey: string, model: string = 'glm-4'): any {


  return new SmartFixer({
    enableAI: true,
    aiProvider: {
      name: 'zhipu',
      apiKey: apiKey,
      baseURL: 'https://open.bigmodel.cn/api/anthropic',
      model: model
    },
    maxAttempts: 3,
    executionTimeout: 60000,
    enableRealTimeMonitoring: true
  });
}

    description: string;
    category: string;
    priority: string;
    action: string;
  },
  context?: any
) {

  );

-- File: anti-bot.middleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import redisService from './redis.service';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { defaultConfig as config } from '../config';

// Known bot user agents
const KNOWN_BOTS = [
  'googlebot',
  'bingbot',
  'slurp',
  'duckduckbot',
  'baiduspider',
  'yandexbot',
  'sogou',
  'exabot',
  'facebot',
  'facebookexternalhit',
  'ia_archiver',
  'twitterbot',
  'linkedinbot',
  'embedly',
  'quora link preview',
  'showyoubot',
  'outbrain',
  'pinterest',
  'applebot',
  'semrushbot',
];

// Suspicious patterns (scrapers/crawlers)
const SUSPICIOUS_PATTERNS = [
  /bot/i,
  /spider/i,
  /crawler/i,
  /scraper/i,
  /curl/i,
  /wget/i,
  /python/i,
  /java/i,
  /go-http-client/i,
  /node/i,
  /axios/i,
  /http.rb/i,
  /okhttp/i,
  /allover/i,
  /scan/i,
  /harvest/i,
];

// Honey pot field names (hidden fields that bots might fill)
const HONEYPOT_FIELD_NAME = 'website_confirm';

interface RequestFingerprint {
  ip: string;
  userAgent: string;
  acceptLanguage: string;
  acceptEncoding: string;
}

export class AntiBotMiddleware {
  private readonly REQUEST_THRESHOLD = 100; // requests per minute
  private readonly FAST_REQUEST_THRESHOLD = 10; // requests in 10 seconds
  private readonly BLOCK_DURATION = 3600; // 1 hour in seconds

  // Generate request fingerprint
  private generateFingerprint(req: Request): RequestFingerprint {
    return {
      ip: req.ip || req.connection.remoteAddress || 'unknown',
      userAgent: req.headers['user-agent'] || 'unknown',
      acceptLanguage: req.headers['accept-language'] || 'unknown',
      acceptEncoding: req.headers['accept-encoding'] || 'unknown',
    };
  }

  // Check if user agent is a known bot
  private isKnownBot(userAgent: string): boolean {
    return KNOWN_BOTS.some(bot => userAgent.toLowerCase().includes(bot));
  }

  // Check if user agent looks suspicious
  private isSuspiciousUserAgent(userAgent: string): boolean {
    // Empty user agent is suspicious
    if (!userAgent || userAgent.length < 10) {
      return true;
    }

    // Check against suspicious patterns
    return SUSPICIOUS_PATTERNS.some(pattern => pattern.test(userAgent));
  }

  // Check request rate using sliding window
  private async checkRequestRate(ip: string): Promise<{ allowed: boolean; rate: number }> {
    const key = `antibot:requests:${ip}`;
    const now = Date.now();
    const windowStart = now - 60000; // 1 minute window

    try {
      // Add current request timestamp
      await redisService.getClient().zadd(key, now, uuidv4());

      // Remove old entries outside the window
      await redisService.getClient().zremrangebyscore(key, 0, windowStart);

      // Count requests in the window
      const count = await redisService.getClient().zcard(key);

      // Set expiry
      await redisService.getClient().expire(key, 60);

      // Check fast request rate (10 requests in 10 seconds)
      const fastWindowStart = now - 10000;
      const fastCount = await redisService.getClient().zcount(key, fastWindowStart, now);

      // Determine if allowed
      const allowed = count < this.REQUEST_THRESHOLD && fastCount < this.FAST_REQUEST_THRESHOLD;

      return { allowed, rate: count };
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error checking request rate', { error, ip });
      return { allowed: true, rate: 0 };
    }
  }

  // Check for fast sequential requests (possible automation)
  private async checkFastSequentialRequests(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `antibot:fast:${ip}`;
    const now = Date.now();

    try {
      const lastRequest = await redisService.get(key);
      if (lastRequest) {
        const timeSinceLastRequest = now - parseInt(lastRequest);
        // If requests are less than 100ms apart, it's likely automated
        if (timeSinceLastRequest < 100) {
          return true; // Suspicious
        }
      }

      // Update last request time
      await redisService.set(key, now.toString(), 10);
      return false;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error checking fast requests', { error, ip });
      return false;
    }
  }

  // Check honeypot field (if present in request body)
  private checkHoneypot(req: Request): boolean {
    if (req.body && typeof req.body === 'object') {
      const honeypotValue = req.body[HONEYPOT_FIELD_NAME];
      // If honeypot field is filled, it's likely a bot
      if (honeypotValue && honeypotValue.toString().trim().length > 0) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  // Block IP address
  private async blockIP(ip: string, reason: string): Promise<void> {
    const key = `antibot:blocked:${ip}`;
    try {
      await redisService.set(
        key,
        JSON.stringify({
          reason,
          blockedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        }),
        this.BLOCK_DURATION
      );

      logger.warn('IP blocked for suspicious activity', { ip, reason });
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error blocking IP', { error, ip });
    }
  }

  // Check if IP is blocked
  private async isIPBlocked(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `antibot:blocked:${ip}`;
    try {
      const blocked = await redisService.exists(key);
      return blocked;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error checking blocked IP', { error, ip });
      return false;
    }
  }

  // Main middleware function
  public protect = async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): Promise<void> => {
    const fingerprint = this.generateFingerprint(req);
    const userAgent = fingerprint.userAgent.toLowerCase();

    try {
      // Skip all checks in development/test environment for testing purposes
      if (config.env !== 'production') {
        logger.debug('Skipping anti-bot checks in development environment', { ip: fingerprint.ip });
        next();
        return;
      }

      // Check if IP is already blocked
      const isBlocked = await this.isIPBlocked(fingerprint.ip);
      if (isBlocked) {
        logger.warn('Blocked IP attempted access', { ip: fingerprint.ip });
        res.status(429).json({
          error: 'Too many requests',
          message: 'Your access has been temporarily restricted due to suspicious activity',
        });
        return;
      }

      // Allow known good bots (Google, Bing, etc.)
      if (this.isKnownBot(userAgent)) {
        logger.debug('Known bot allowed', { ip: fingerprint.ip, userAgent: fingerprint.userAgent });
        next();
        return;
      }

      // Allow localhost/127.0.0.1/::1 for development
      if (fingerprint.ip === '127.0.0.1' || fingerprint.ip === '::1' || fingerprint.ip === 'localhost') {
        logger.debug('Localhost allowed', { ip: fingerprint.ip });
        next();
        return;
      }

      // Check for suspicious user agent
      if (this.isSuspiciousUserAgent(userAgent)) {
        await this.blockIP(fingerprint.ip, 'Suspicious user agent');
        logger.warn('Suspicious user agent blocked', { ip: fingerprint.ip, userAgent: fingerprint.userAgent });
        res.status(403).json({
          error: 'Access denied',
          message: 'Your request appears to be automated',
        });
        return;
      }

      // Check honeypot
      if (this.checkHoneypot(req)) {
        await this.blockIP(fingerprint.ip, 'Honeypot triggered');
        logger.warn('Honeypot triggered', { ip: fingerprint.ip });
        res.status(403).json({
          error: 'Access denied',
          message: 'Invalid request',
        });
        return;
      }

      // Check request rate
      const rateCheck = await this.checkRequestRate(fingerprint.ip);
      if (!rateCheck.allowed) {
        await this.blockIP(fingerprint.ip, `Rate limit exceeded: ${rateCheck.rate} req/min`);
        logger.warn('Rate limit exceeded', { ip: fingerprint.ip, rate: rateCheck.rate });
        res.status(429).json({
          error: 'Too many requests',
          message: 'Rate limit exceeded. Please slow down.',
          retryAfter: this.BLOCK_DURATION,
        });
        return;
      }

      // Check for fast sequential requests
      const isFastSequential = await this.checkFastSequentialRequests(fingerprint.ip);
      if (isFastSequential) {
        logger.warn('Fast sequential requests detected', { ip: fingerprint.ip });
        // Don't block immediately, but log the suspicious activity
      }

      // Check for missing required headers
      if (!req.headers['user-agent'] || !req.headers['accept']) {
        logger.warn('Missing required headers', { ip: fingerprint.ip, headers: req.headers });
        res.status(400).json({
          error: 'Bad request',
          message: 'Invalid request headers',
        });
        return;
      }

      // All checks passed
      next();
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error in anti-bot middleware', { error, ip: fingerprint.ip });
      // Fail open to avoid blocking legitimate traffic on error
      next();
    }
  };

  // Get honeypot field name (for use in forms)
  public static getHoneypotFieldName(): string {
    return HONEYPOT_FIELD_NAME;
  }
}

// Export singleton instance
export default new AntiBotMiddleware();
-- File: auth-security.service.ts
import redisService from './redis.service';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { Request } from 'express';

export interface PasswordPolicy {
  minLength: number;
  requireUppercase: boolean;
  requireLowercase: boolean;
  requireNumbers: boolean;
  requireSpecialChars: boolean;
  maxLength?: number;
}

export interface LoginAttempt {
  email: string;
  ip: string;
  success: boolean;
  timestamp: number;
}

export class AuthSecurityService {
  private readonly MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS = 5;
  private readonly LOCKOUT_DURATION = 900; // 15 minutes in seconds
  private readonly ATTEMPT_WINDOW = 300; // 5 minutes in seconds

  private readonly PASSWORD_POLICY: PasswordPolicy = {
    minLength: 8,
    requireUppercase: true,
    requireLowercase: true,
    requireNumbers: true,
    requireSpecialChars: true,
    maxLength: 128,
  };

  private readonly COMMON_PASSWORDS = [
    'password', '123456', '12345678', 'qwerty', 'abc123',
    'monkey', 'master', 'dragon', '111111', 'baseball',
    'iloveyou', 'trustno1', 'sunshine', 'princess', 'admin',
    'welcome', 'shadow', 'ashley', 'football', 'jesus',
    'michael', 'ninja', 'mustang', 'password1', 'password123',
  ];

  private readonly SUSPICIOUS_PATTERNS = [
    /1234.*56/, // Sequential numbers
    /abcd.*ef/, // Sequential letters
    /qwerty.*uiop/, // Keyboard patterns
    /(.+)\1{2,}/, // Repeated characters (aaa111)
  ];

  // Check password strength
  public validatePassword(password: string): { valid: boolean; errors: string[]; strength: string } {
    const errors: string[] = [];
    let strength = 'weak';

    // Check length
    if (password.length < this.PASSWORD_POLICY.minLength) {
      errors.push(`Password must be at least ${this.PASSWORD_POLICY.minLength} characters long`);
    } else if (password.length >= 12) {
      strength = 'strong';
    } else if (password.length >= 8) {
      strength = 'medium';
    }

    // Check max length
    if (this.PASSWORD_POLICY.maxLength && password.length > this.PASSWORD_POLICY.maxLength) {
      errors.push(`Password must not exceed ${this.PASSWORD_POLICY.maxLength} characters`);
    }

    // Check uppercase
    if (this.PASSWORD_POLICY.requireUppercase && !/[A-Z]/.test(password)) {
      errors.push('Password must contain at least one uppercase letter');
    }

    // Check lowercase
    if (this.PASSWORD_POLICY.requireLowercase && !/[a-z]/.test(password)) {
      errors.push('Password must contain at least one lowercase letter');
    }

    // Check numbers
    if (this.PASSWORD_POLICY.requireNumbers && !/\d/.test(password)) {
      errors.push('Password must contain at least one number');
    }

    // Check special characters
    if (this.PASSWORD_POLICY.requireSpecialChars && !/[!@#$%^&*()_+\-=\[\]{};':"\\|,.<>\/?]/.test(password)) {
      errors.push('Password must contain at least one special character');
    }

    // Check against common passwords
    if (this.COMMON_PASSWORDS.includes(password.toLowerCase())) {
      errors.push('Password is too common');
      strength = 'weak';
    }

    // Check for suspicious patterns
    for (const pattern of this.SUSPICIOUS_PATTERNS) {
      if (pattern.test(password)) {
        errors.push('Password contains predictable patterns');
        strength = 'weak';
        break;
      }
    }

    return {
      valid: errors.length === 0,
      errors,
      strength,
    };
  }

  // Record login attempt
  public async recordLoginAttempt(email: string, ip: string, success: boolean): Promise<void> {
    const key = `auth:attempts:${email.toLowerCase()}`;

    try {
      const attempt: LoginAttempt = {
        email: email.toLowerCase(),
        ip,
        success,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
      };

      // Add attempt to Redis list
      await redisService.getClient().lpush(key, JSON.stringify(attempt));

      // Remove old attempts outside the window
      const cutoffTime = Date.now() - (this.ATTEMPT_WINDOW * 1000);
      const attempts = await redisService.getClient().lrange(key, 0, -1);

      // Filter and keep only recent attempts
      const recentAttempts = attempts
        .map(a => {
          try {
            return JSON.parse(a);
          } catch {
            return null;
          }
        })
        .filter((a: LoginAttempt | null) => a !== null && a.timestamp > cutoffTime) as LoginAttempt[];

      // Clear and repopulate with recent attempts
      await redisService.del(key);
      for (const a of recentAttempts.reverse()) {
        await redisService.getClient().rpush(key, JSON.stringify(a));
      }

      // Set expiry
      await redisService.getClient().expire(key, this.ATTEMPT_WINDOW);

      // If failed attempt, check if we need to lock the account
      if (!success) {
        await this.checkAndLockAccount(email, ip);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error recording login attempt', { error, email });
    }
  }

  // Check and lock account if too many failed attempts
  private async checkAndLockAccount(email: string, ip: string): Promise<void> {
    const key = `auth:attempts:${email.toLowerCase()}`;

    try {
      const attempts = await redisService.getClient().lrange(key, 0, -1);
      const failedAttempts = attempts
        .map(a => {
          try {
            return JSON.parse(a);
          } catch {
            return null;
          }
        })
        .filter((a: LoginAttempt | null): a is LoginAttempt => a !== null && !a.success);

      if (failedAttempts.length >= this.MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS) {
        // Lock the account
        const lockKey = `auth:locked:${email.toLowerCase()}`;
        await redisService.set(
          lockKey,
          JSON.stringify({
            reason: `Too many failed login attempts (${this.MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS})`,
            lockedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            attempts: failedAttempts.slice(0, this.MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS),
          }),
          this.LOCKOUT_DURATION
        );

        logger.error('Account locked due to too many failed attempts', {
          email,
          ip,
          attempts: failedAttempts.length,
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error checking account lock', { error, email });
    }
  }

  // Check if account is locked
  public async isAccountLocked(email: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `auth:locked:${email.toLowerCase()}`;

    try {
      return await redisService.exists(key);
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error checking account lock', { error, email });
      return false;
    }
  }

  // Get lock information
  public async getLockInfo(email: string): Promise<{ locked: boolean; reason?: string; remainingTime?: number }> {
    const key = `auth:locked:${email.toLowerCase()}`;

    try {
      const lockData = await redisService.get(key);
      if (!lockData) {
        return { locked: false };
      }

      const ttl = await redisService.getClient().ttl(key);
      const lock = JSON.parse(lockData);

      return {
        locked: true,
        reason: lock.reason,
        remainingTime: ttl > 0 ? ttl : 0,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error getting lock info', { error, email });
      return { locked: false };
    }
  }

  // Unlock account (admin only or after lockout period)
  public async unlockAccount(email: string): Promise<void> {
    const key = `auth:locked:${email.toLowerCase()}`;

    try {
      await redisService.del(key);
      logger.info('Account unlocked', { email });
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error unlocking account', { error, email });
    }
  }

  // Check IP for multiple failed login attempts (possible brute force)
  public async checkIPBruteForce(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `auth:ip:attempts:${ip}`;

    try {
      const attempts = await redisService.getClient().lrange(key, 0, -1);
      const recentAttempts = attempts
        .map(a => {
          try {
            return JSON.parse(a);
          } catch {
            return null;
          }
        })
        .filter((a: LoginAttempt | null): a is LoginAttempt => {
          if (!a) return false;
          const timeSinceAttempt = Date.now() - a.timestamp;
          return timeSinceAttempt < (this.ATTEMPT_WINDOW * 1000);
        });

      // If more than 10 failed attempts from same IP, it's suspicious
      const failedAttempts = recentAttempts.filter((a: LoginAttempt) => !a.success);
      if (failedAttempts.length > 10) {
        logger.error('Possible brute force attack detected', {
          ip,
          attempts: failedAttempts.length,
        });
        return true;
      }

      return false;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error checking IP brute force', { error, ip });
      return false;
    }
  }

  // Get failed login attempts for monitoring
  public async getFailedAttempts(email: string): Promise<LoginAttempt[]> {
    const key = `auth:attempts:${email.toLowerCase()}`;

    try {
      const attempts = await redisService.getClient().lrange(key, 0, -1);
      return attempts
        .map(a => {
          try {
            return JSON.parse(a);
          } catch {
            return null;
          }
        })
        .filter((a: LoginAttempt | null): a is LoginAttempt => a !== null && !a.success);
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error getting failed attempts', { error, email });
      return [];
    }
  }

  // Detect suspicious login behavior
  public async detectSuspiciousLogin(req: Request, email: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const warnings: string[] = [];
    const ip = req.ip || req.connection.remoteAddress || 'unknown';

    // Check if account is locked
    const isLocked = await this.isAccountLocked(email);
    if (isLocked) {
      const lockInfo = await this.getLockInfo(email);
      warnings.push(`Account is locked: ${lockInfo.reason}`);
    }

    // Check IP for brute force
    const isBruteForce = await this.checkIPBruteForce(ip);
    if (isBruteForce) {
      warnings.push('Multiple failed login attempts detected from your IP');
    }

    // Check for unusual login time (optional)
    const hour = new Date().getHours();
    if (hour >= 2 && hour <= 5) {
      // Logins between 2-5 AM are suspicious
      warnings.push('Unusual login time detected');
    }

    // Check User-Agent consistency (optional enhancement)
    const userAgent = req.headers['user-agent'];
    if (!userAgent || userAgent.length < 10) {
      warnings.push('Invalid User-Agent');
    }

    return warnings;
  }

  // Generate session ID for additional security
  public generateSessionId(): string {
    return `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15)}`;
  }

  // Validate session
  public async validateSession(userId: string, sessionId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `auth:session:${userId}`;

    try {
      const storedSessionId = await redisService.get(key);
      return storedSessionId === sessionId;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error validating session', { error, userId });
      return false;
    }
  }

  // Invalidate session (logout)
  public async invalidateSession(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    const key = `auth:session:${userId}`;

    try {
      await redisService.del(key);
      logger.info('Session invalidated', { userId });
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error invalidating session', { error, userId });
    }
  }

  // Check for concurrent sessions
  public async checkConcurrentSessions(userId: string, maxSessions: number = 3): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `auth:concurrent:${userId}`;

    try {
      const sessionCount = await redisService.getClient().scard(key);
      return sessionCount < maxSessions;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error checking concurrent sessions', { error, userId });
      return true;
    }
  }

  // Add session
  public async addSession(userId: string, sessionId: string, deviceInfo: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    const key = `auth:concurrent:${userId}`;

    try {
      await redisService.getClient().sadd(key, sessionId);
      await redisService.getClient().expire(key, 86400); // 24 hours

      // Store session details
      const sessionKey = `auth:session:${sessionId}`;
      await redisService.set(
        sessionKey,
        JSON.stringify({
          userId,
          deviceInfo,
          createdAt: Date.now(),
        }),
        86400
      );
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error adding session', { error, userId });
    }
  }

  // Remove session
  public async removeSession(userId: string, sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
    const key = `auth:concurrent:${userId}`;
    const sessionKey = `auth:session:${sessionId}`;

    try {
      await redisService.getClient().srem(key, sessionId);
      await redisService.del(sessionKey);
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Error removing session', { error, userId });
    }
  }
}

export default new AuthSecurityService();
-- File: concurrency.middleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import { CONCURRENCY, MONITORING } from '../config';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import redisService from './redis.service';

/**
 * Concurrency control middleware for high-traffic scenarios
 * Prevents server overload and ensures fair resource allocation
 */

interface QueueItem {
  resolve: () => void;
  reject: (error: any) => void;
  timestamp: number;
}

class ConcurrencyController {
  private userQueues = new Map<string, QueueItem[]>();
  private activeRequests = new Map<string, number>();
  // Use locks to prevent race conditions
  private locks = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();

  /**
   * Acquire request slot for user (thread-safe)
   */
  async acquireSlot(userId: string): Promise<void> {
    // Wait for any existing operation on this user to complete
    let existingLock = this.locks.get(userId);
    while (existingLock) {
      await existingLock;
      existingLock = this.locks.get(userId);
    }

    // Create a new lock for this operation
    let resolveLock: (() => void) | undefined;
    const lockPromise = new Promise<void>(resolve => { resolveLock = resolve; });
    this.locks.set(userId, lockPromise);

    try {
      const active = this.activeRequests.get(userId) || 0;

      if (active < CONCURRENCY.MAX_CONCURRENT_REQUESTS_PER_USER) {
        // Slot available - atomic increment
        this.activeRequests.set(userId, active + 1);
        return;
      }

      // Wait in queue
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        let queue = this.userQueues.get(userId);

        if (!queue) {
          queue = [];
          this.userQueues.set(userId, queue);
        }

        // Check queue size limit
        if (queue.length >= CONCURRENCY.MAX_QUEUE_SIZE) {
          reject(new Error('Request queue full'));
          return;
        }

        queue.push({
          resolve: resolve as () => void,
          reject,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
        });
      });
    } finally {
      // Release the lock
      if (resolveLock) {
        resolveLock();
      }
      this.locks.delete(userId);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Release request slot for user
   */
  releaseSlot(userId: string): void {
    const active = this.activeRequests.get(userId) || 0;

    if (active > 0) {
      this.activeRequests.set(userId, active - 1);
    }

    // Process next request in queue
    const queue = this.userQueues.get(userId);

    if (queue && queue.length > 0) {
      const next = queue.shift();
      this.activeRequests.set(userId, (this.activeRequests.get(userId) || 0) + 1);
      next?.resolve();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get current statistics
   */
  getStats(userId: string) {
    return {
      active: this.activeRequests.get(userId) || 0,
      queued: (this.userQueues.get(userId) || []).length,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Cleanup inactive users to prevent memory leaks
   */
  cleanup(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    const staleThreshold = now - (60 * 60 * 1000); // 1 hour

    for (const [userId, queue] of this.userQueues.entries()) {
      const active = this.activeRequests.get(userId) || 0;
      const hasStaleRequests = queue.some(item => item.timestamp < staleThreshold);

      // Clean up if no active requests and only stale queued requests
      if (active === 0 && hasStaleRequests) {
        this.userQueues.delete(userId);
        this.activeRequests.delete(userId);
      }
    }
  }
}

const controller = new ConcurrencyController();

/**
 * Concurrency limiting middleware
 */
export const concurrencyLimit = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction
): Promise<void> => {
  const userId = (req as any).user?.userId || 'anonymous';

  try {
    // Acquire slot
    await controller.acquireSlot(userId);

    logger.debug('Request slot acquired', { userId });

    // Release slot after response
    res.on('finish', () => {
      controller.releaseSlot(userId);
      logger.debug('Request slot released', { userId });
    });

    next();
  } catch (error) {
    logger.warn('Concurrency limit exceeded', { userId, error });

    res.status(429).json({
      success: false,
      error: {
        message: 'Too many concurrent requests. Please retry later.',
        type: 'concurrency_limit_exceeded',
      },
    });
  }
};

/**
 * Performance monitoring middleware
 */
export const performanceMonitor = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction
): Promise<void> => {
  if (!MONITORING.ENABLE_PERFORMANCE_TRACKING) {
    next();
    return;
  }

  const startTime = Date.now();
  const startMemory = process.memoryUsage();

  // Track response
  const originalJson = res.json.bind(res);

  res.json = function (body: any) {
    const endTime = Date.now();
    const duration = endTime - startTime;
    const endMemory = process.memoryUsage();
    const memoryUsed = (endMemory.heapUsed - startMemory.heapUsed) / 1024 / 1024; // MB

    // Log slow requests
    if (duration > MONITORING.SLOW_QUERY_THRESHOLD) {
      logger.warn('Slow request detected', {
        url: req.url,
        method: req.method,
        duration,
        memoryUsed: `${memoryUsed.toFixed(2)}MB`,
      });
    } else {
      logger.debug('Request completed', {
        url: req.url,
        method: req.method,
        duration,
        memoryUsed: `${memoryUsed.toFixed(2)}MB`,
      });
    }

    // Add performance headers
    res.setHeader('X-Response-Time', `${duration}ms`);

    return originalJson(body);
  };

  next();
};

// Store timer IDs for cleanup
let memoryMonitorInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
let cpuMonitorInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

/**
 * Memory usage monitoring
 */
export function startMemoryMonitoring(): void {
  // Clear existing interval if any
  if (memoryMonitorInterval) {
    clearInterval(memoryMonitorInterval);
  }

  memoryMonitorInterval = setInterval(() => {
    const memory = process.memoryUsage();
    const heapUsedMB = memory.heapUsed / 1024 / 1024;

    if (heapUsedMB > MONITORING.MEMORY_WARNING_THRESHOLD) {
      logger.warn('High memory usage detected', {
        heapUsed: `${heapUsedMB.toFixed(2)}MB`,
        heapTotal: `${(memory.heapTotal / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)}MB`,
        external: `${(memory.external / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)}MB`,
      });
    }
  }, 30000); // Check every 30 seconds
}

/**
 * CPU usage monitoring
 */
export function startCPUMonitoring(): void {
  // Clear existing interval if any
  if (cpuMonitorInterval) {
    clearInterval(cpuMonitorInterval);
  }

  // Reset baseline each time monitoring starts
  let startUsage = process.cpuUsage();

  cpuMonitorInterval = setInterval(() => {
    const usage = process.cpuUsage(startUsage);
    const userUsage = usage.user / 1000000; // Convert to seconds
    const systemUsage = usage.system / 1000000;

    logger.debug('CPU usage', {
      user: `${userUsage.toFixed(2)}s`,
      system: `${systemUsage.toFixed(2)}s`,
    });

    // Reset baseline for next interval
    startUsage = process.cpuUsage();
  }, 30000); // Check every 30 seconds
}

/**
 * Stop all monitoring intervals (for cleanup)
 */
export function stopMonitoring(): void {
  if (memoryMonitorInterval) {
    clearInterval(memoryMonitorInterval);
    memoryMonitorInterval = null;
  }
  if (cpuMonitorInterval) {
    clearInterval(cpuMonitorInterval);
    cpuMonitorInterval = null;
  }
}
