-- File: ratelimit.middleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import redisService from './redis.service';
import { RATE_LIMIT, HTTP_STATUS, REDIS_KEYS } from '../config';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

// Helper function to set rate limit headers
function setRateLimitHeaders(
  res: Response,
  limit: number,
  remaining: number,
  resetTime: number
): void {
  res.set('X-RateLimit-Limit', limit.toString());
  res.set('X-RateLimit-Remaining', remaining.toString());
  res.set('X-RateLimit-Reset', resetTime.toString());
}

// Rate limit middleware using Redis
export const rateLimitMiddleware = async (
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    if (!(req as any).apiKey) {
      next();
      return;
    }

    const { userId, rateLimit } = (req as any).apiKey;
    const limit = rateLimit.requestsPerMinute;

    // Create rate limit keys
    const requestCountKey = `${REDIS_KEYS.RATE_LIMIT_PREFIX}:${userId}:${REDIS_KEYS.REQUEST_COUNT_SUFFIX}`;
    const requestLimitKey = `${REDIS_KEYS.RATE_LIMIT_PREFIX}:${userId}:${REDIS_KEYS.LIMIT_SUFFIX}`;
    const windowKey = `${REDIS_KEYS.RATE_LIMIT_PREFIX}:${userId}:${REDIS_KEYS.WINDOW_SUFFIX}`;

    const now = Date.now();

    // Use atomic increment with expiry to prevent race conditions
    const client = redisService.getClient();

    // Get current window info
    const currentWindow = await redisService.get(windowKey);

    if (!currentWindow) {
      // Start new window atomically
      const multi = client.multi();
      multi.set(windowKey, now.toString(), 'EX', RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS);
      multi.set(requestCountKey, '1', 'EX', RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS);
      multi.set(requestLimitKey, limit.toString(), 'EX', RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS);
      await multi.exec();

      setRateLimitHeaders(
        res,
        limit,
        limit - 1,
        Math.ceil((now + RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000) / 1000)
      );

      next();
      return;
    }

    const windowStart = parseInt(currentWindow, 10);
    const elapsed = now - windowStart;

    if (elapsed >= RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000) {
      // New window - reset atomically
      const multi = client.multi();
      multi.set(windowKey, now.toString(), 'EX', RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS);
      multi.set(requestCountKey, '1', 'EX', RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS);
      multi.set(requestLimitKey, limit.toString(), 'EX', RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS);
      await multi.exec();

      setRateLimitHeaders(
        res,
        limit,
        limit - 1,
        Math.ceil((now + RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000) / 1000)
      );

      next();
      return;
    }

    // Atomic increment and check in one operation
    const newCount = await client.incr(requestCountKey);

    if (newCount > limit) {
      const resetTime = Math.ceil((windowStart + RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000) / 1000);
      const retryAfter = Math.max(0, resetTime - Math.ceil(Date.now() / 1000));

      setRateLimitHeaders(res, limit, 0, resetTime);
      res.set('Retry-After', retryAfter.toString());

      res.status(HTTP_STATUS.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS).json({
        success: false,
        error: {
          message: 'Rate limit exceeded. Please retry later.',
          type: 'rate_limit_exceeded',
          param: null,
        },
      });
      return;
    }

    const remaining = Math.max(0, limit - newCount);
    const resetTime = Math.ceil((windowStart + RATE_LIMIT.WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000) / 1000);

    setRateLimitHeaders(res, limit, remaining, resetTime);

    next();
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('Rate limit error', { error, userId: (req as any).apiKey?.userId });
    // On Redis error, allow request but log warning
    next();
  }
};
-- File: redis.service.ts
// Redis Service

import Redis from 'ioredis';
import logger from '../utils/logger';

class RedisService {
  private client: Redis | null = null;

  constructor() {
    this.connect();
  }

  private connect(): void {
    try {
      const redisUrl = process.env.REDIS_URL || 'redis://localhost:6379';

      this.client = new Redis(redisUrl, {
        retryStrategy: (times) => {
          const delay = Math.min(times * 50, 2000);
          return delay;
        },
        maxRetriesPerRequest: 3,
      });

      this.client.on('connect', () => {
        logger.info('Redis connected successfully');
      });

      this.client.on('error', (error) => {
        logger.error('Redis connection error', { error });
      });

      this.client.on('close', () => {
        logger.warn('Redis connection closed');
      });
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Failed to initialize Redis', { error });
    }
  }

  getClient(): Redis {
    if (!this.client) {
      throw new Error('Redis client not initialized');
    }
    return this.client;
  }

  async get(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
    try {
      return await this.getClient().get(key);
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Redis GET error', { error, key });
      return null;
    }
  }

  async set(key: string, value: string, seconds?: number): Promise<void> {
    try {
      if (seconds) {
        await this.getClient().setex(key, seconds, value);
      } else {
        await this.getClient().set(key, value);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Redis SET error', { error, key });
    }
  }

  async del(key: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.getClient().del(key);
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Redis DEL error', { error, key });
    }
  }

  async exists(key: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const result = await this.getClient().exists(key);
      return result === 1;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error('Redis EXISTS error', { error, key });
      return false;
    }
  }

  async disconnect(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.client) {
      await this.client.quit();
      this.client = null;
    }
  }
}

export default new RedisService();

-- File: simulation.service.ts
import { defaultConfig as config } from '../config';

interface TrafficStats {
    totalRequests: number;
    blockedRequests: number;
    activeConnections: number;
    successRate: number;
    currentRPS: number;
    history: { time: string; requests: number; blocked: number }[];
    threats: { name: string; value: number; color: string }[];
}

class SimulationService {
    private stats: TrafficStats = {
        totalRequests: 1250000,
        blockedRequests: 4200,
        activeConnections: 25,
        successRate: 99.5,
        currentRPS: 50,
        history: [],
        threats: [
            { name: 'Bot Attacks', value: 65, color: '#ef4444' }, // red-500
            { name: 'SQL Injection', value: 15, color: '#f97316' }, // orange-500
            { name: 'DDoS Attempts', value: 10, color: '#eab308' }, // yellow-500
            { name: 'Rate Limit', value: 10, color: '#3b82f6' }, // blue-500
        ]
    };

    private interval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

    constructor() {
        // Initialize history with 20 points
        const now = new Date();
        for (let i = 19; i >= 0; i--) {
            const time = new Date(now.getTime() - i * 1000 * 5); // every 5 seconds
            this.stats.history.push({
                time: time.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' }),
                requests: 50 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20),
                blocked: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5)
            });
        }
        this.start();
    }

    public start() {
        if (this.interval) return;

        this.interval = setInterval(() => {
            this.updateStats();
        }, 2000); // Update every 2 seconds
    }

    private updateStats() {
        // Simulate RPS fluctuation (sine wave + noise)
        const time = Date.now() / 10000;
        const baseRPS = 80;
        const fluctuation = Math.sin(time) * 30; // +/- 30
        const noise = Math.random() * 10 - 5;

        this.stats.currentRPS = Math.max(10, Math.floor(baseRPS + fluctuation + noise));

        // Update totals
        const newRequests = this.stats.currentRPS * 2; // for 2 seconds
        this.stats.totalRequests += newRequests;

        // Simulate blocks (approx 2% of traffic)
        const newBlocks = Math.floor(newRequests * (0.01 + Math.random() * 0.02));
        this.stats.blockedRequests += newBlocks;

        // Update Success Rate
        const total = this.stats.totalRequests;
        const blocked = this.stats.blockedRequests;
        this.stats.successRate = Number(((1 - blocked / total) * 100).toFixed(2));

        // Update Active Connections
        this.stats.activeConnections = Math.floor(this.stats.currentRPS * 0.5) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);

        // Update History
        const now = new Date();
        const newPoint = {
            time: now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' }),
            requests: this.stats.currentRPS,
            blocked: Math.max(0, Math.floor(this.stats.currentRPS * (Math.random() * 0.1))) // 0-10% blocked in chart
        };

        this.stats.history.push(newPoint);
        if (this.stats.history.length > 20) {
            this.stats.history.shift();
        }
    }

    public getStats() {
        return this.stats;
    }
}

export default new SimulationService();
-- File: logger.ts
// Logger Utility

type LogLevel = 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';

interface LogContext {
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

class Logger {
  private formatMessage(level: LogLevel, message: string, context?: LogContext): string {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    const contextStr = context ? ` ${JSON.stringify(context)}` : '';
    return `[${timestamp}] [${level.toUpperCase()}] ${message}${contextStr}`;
  }

  debug(message: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
      console.debug(this.formatMessage('debug', message, context));
    }
  }

  info(message: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    console.info(this.formatMessage('info', message, context));
  }

  warn(message: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    console.warn(this.formatMessage('warn', message, context));
  }

  error(message: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    console.error(this.formatMessage('error', message, context));
  }
}

export default new Logger();
-- File: agent-gateway.ts
/**
 * WebSocket 缃戝叧 - Agent 閫氫俊涓績
 * 
 * 鍔熻兘:
 * - 鎺ユ敹 Agent 娉ㄥ唽鍜屽績璺?
 * - 鎺ㄩ€佸憡璀﹀埌鍓嶇
 * - 涓嬪彂鎸囦护鍒?Agent
 */

import { Server as SocketIOServer, Socket } from 'socket.io';
import { Server as HttpServer } from 'http';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import { AgentStatus, ThreatAlert } from '../agent';

interface ConnectedAgent {
    socketId: string;
    agentId: string;
    registration: any;
    lastHeartbeat: Date;
    status: AgentStatus | null;
}

export class AgentGateway {
    private io: SocketIOServer;
    private agents: Map<string, ConnectedAgent> = new Map();
    private dashboardClients: Set<string> = new Set();

    constructor(httpServer: HttpServer) {
        this.io = new SocketIOServer(httpServer, {
            cors: {
                origin: process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS?.split(',') || ['http://localhost:5173'],
                methods: ['GET', 'POST']
            }
        });

        this.setupEventHandlers();
        logger.info('Agent Gateway initialized');
    }

    /**
     * 璁剧疆浜嬩欢澶勭悊鍣?
     */
    private setupEventHandlers(): void {
        this.io.on('connection', (socket: Socket) => {
            logger.info(`New connection: ${socket.id}`);

            // 鍒ゆ柇鏄?Agent 杩樻槸 Dashboard 瀹㈡埛绔?
            const clientType = socket.handshake.auth.type || 'dashboard';

            if (clientType === 'agent') {
                this.handleAgentConnection(socket);
            } else {
                this.handleDashboardConnection(socket);
            }

            socket.on('disconnect', () => {
                this.handleDisconnect(socket);
            });
        });
    }

    /**
     * 澶勭悊 Agent 杩炴帴
     */
    private handleAgentConnection(socket: Socket): void {
        const agentId = socket.handshake.auth.agentId;

        if (!agentId) {
            logger.warn('Agent connection without ID, rejecting');
            socket.disconnect();
            return;
        }

        logger.info(`Agent connected: ${agentId}`);

        // Agent 娉ㄥ唽
        socket.on('agent:register', (registration: any) => {
            const agent: ConnectedAgent = {
                socketId: socket.id,
                agentId: registration.agentId,
                registration,
                lastHeartbeat: new Date(),
                status: null
            };

            this.agents.set(agentId, agent);

            // 閫氱煡 Dashboard 瀹㈡埛绔?
            this.broadcastToDashboards('agent:online', {
                agentId,
                registration
            });

            logger.info(`Agent registered: ${agentId}`, { registration });
        });

        // Agent 蹇冭烦
        socket.on('agent:heartbeat', (status: AgentStatus) => {
            const agent = this.agents.get(agentId);
            if (agent) {
                agent.lastHeartbeat = new Date();
                agent.status = status;
            }

            // 杞彂鐘舵€佸埌 Dashboard
            this.broadcastToDashboards('agent:status', status);
        });

        // Agent 濞佽儊鍛婅
        socket.on('agent:threat', (data: { agentId: string; threat: ThreatAlert; timestamp: string }) => {
            logger.warn(`Threat from ${data.agentId}`, { threat: data.threat });

            // 杞彂鍒版墍鏈?Dashboard 瀹㈡埛绔?
            this.broadcastToDashboards('threat:detected', data);
        });

        // Agent 鎵弿缁撴灉
        socket.on('agent:scan_result', (data: any) => {
            logger.info(`Scan result from ${agentId}`);
            this.broadcastToDashboards('scan:result', data);
        });

        // Agent 鏃ュ織
        socket.on('agent:log', (log: any) => {
            this.broadcastToDashboards('agent:log', { agentId, log });
        });
    }

    /**
     * 澶勭悊 Dashboard 杩炴帴
     */
    private handleDashboardConnection(socket: Socket): void {
        logger.info(`Dashboard client connected: ${socket.id}`);
        this.dashboardClients.add(socket.id);

        // 鍙戦€佸綋鍓?Agent 鍒楄〃
        socket.emit('agents:list', this.getAgentList());

        // Dashboard 璇锋眰鎵弿
        socket.on('request:scan', (data: { agentId: string }) => {
            this.sendCommandToAgent(data.agentId, { type: 'scan' });
        });

        // Dashboard 璇锋眰灏佺 IP
        socket.on('request:block_ip', (data: { agentId: string; ip: string }) => {
            this.sendCommandToAgent(data.agentId, {
                type: 'block_ip',
                payload: { ip: data.ip }
            });
        });

        // Dashboard 璇锋眰瑙ｅ皝 IP
        socket.on('request:unblock_ip', (data: { agentId: string; ip: string }) => {
            this.sendCommandToAgent(data.agentId, {
                type: 'unblock_ip',
                payload: { ip: data.ip }
            });
        });
    }

    /**
     * 澶勭悊鏂紑杩炴帴
     */
    private handleDisconnect(socket: Socket): void {
        // 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚︽槸 Agent
        for (const [agentId, agent] of this.agents) {
            if (agent.socketId === socket.id) {
                this.agents.delete(agentId);
                this.broadcastToDashboards('agent:offline', { agentId });
                logger.info(`Agent disconnected: ${agentId}`);
                return;
            }
        }

        // 鍚﹀垯鏄?Dashboard 瀹㈡埛绔?
        this.dashboardClients.delete(socket.id);
        logger.info(`Dashboard client disconnected: ${socket.id}`);
    }

    /**
     * 骞挎挱娑堟伅鍒版墍鏈?Dashboard 瀹㈡埛绔?
     */
    private broadcastToDashboards(event: string, data: any): void {
        for (const clientId of this.dashboardClients) {
            this.io.to(clientId).emit(event, data);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鍙戦€佹寚浠ゅ埌鎸囧畾 Agent
     */
    sendCommandToAgent(agentId: string, command: { type: string; payload?: any }): boolean {
        const agent = this.agents.get(agentId);
        if (!agent) {
            logger.warn(`Agent not found: ${agentId}`);
            return false;
        }

        this.io.to(agent.socketId).emit('command', command);
        logger.info(`Command sent to ${agentId}: ${command.type}`);
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈?Agent 鍒楄〃
     */
    getAgentList(): any[] {
        return Array.from(this.agents.values()).map(agent => ({
            agentId: agent.agentId,
            registration: agent.registration,
            lastHeartbeat: agent.lastHeartbeat,
            status: agent.status,
            isOnline: true
        }));
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎸囧畾 Agent
     */
    getAgent(agentId: string): ConnectedAgent | undefined {
        return this.agents.get(agentId);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍦ㄧ嚎 Agent 鏁伴噺
     */
    getOnlineCount(): number {
        return this.agents.size;
    }

    /**
     * 鍏抽棴缃戝叧
     */
    close(): void {
        this.io.close();
        logger.info('Agent Gateway closed');
    }
}
-- File: device-gateway.ts
/**
 * FluxGuard - ESP32 璁惧 WebSocket 缃戝叧
 * 
 * 澶勭悊 ESP32 纭欢缃戝叧鐨?WebSocket 杩炴帴
 * - 璁惧娉ㄥ唽
 * - 蹇冭烦鐩戞帶
 * - 鏁版嵁鎺ユ敹
 * - 鍛戒护涓嬪彂
 */

import { Server as HttpServer } from 'http';
import { Server, Socket } from 'socket.io';
import logger from '../utils/logger';
import {
    registerDevice,
    updateDeviceStatus,
    setDeviceOffline,
    getCodebook,
} from '../api/routes/devices.routes';

interface DeviceSocket extends Socket {
    deviceId?: string;
}

export class DeviceGateway {
    private io: Server;
    private deviceSockets: Map<string, DeviceSocket> = new Map();

    constructor(httpServer: HttpServer) {
        this.io = new Server(httpServer, {
            path: '/ws/device',
            cors: {
                origin: '*',
                methods: ['GET', 'POST'],
            },
        });

        this.setupEventHandlers();
        logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Initialized on /ws/device');
    }

    private setupEventHandlers() {
        this.io.on('connection', (socket: DeviceSocket) => {
            logger.info('[DeviceGateway] New device connection', { socketId: socket.id });

            // 璁惧娉ㄥ唽
            socket.on('register', (data: any) => {
                this.handleRegister(socket, data);
            });

            // 蹇冭烦
            socket.on('heartbeat', (data: any) => {
                this.handleHeartbeat(socket, data);
            });

            // 鏁版嵁涓婃姤
            socket.on('data', (data: any) => {
                this.handleData(socket, data);
            });

            // 鍛婅
            socket.on('alert', (data: any) => {
                this.handleAlert(socket, data);
            });

            // 鏈煡閿欒涓婃姤
            socket.on('unknown_error', (data: any) => {
                this.handleUnknownError(socket, data);
            });

            // 瀵嗙爜鏈悓姝ヨ姹?
            socket.on('codebook_sync', () => {
                this.handleCodebookSync(socket);
            });

            // 鏂紑杩炴帴
            socket.on('disconnect', (reason: string) => {
                this.handleDisconnect(socket, reason);
            });
        });
    }

    // ============================================
    // 浜嬩欢澶勭悊鍣?
    // ============================================

    private handleRegister(socket: DeviceSocket, data: any) {
        const {
            device_id,
            version,
            chip_model,
            flash_size,
            free_heap,
            sdk_version,
            api_key,
        } = data;

        // 楠岃瘉 API Key (绠€鍖栫増锛屽疄闄呭簲鏌ユ暟鎹簱)
        if (!api_key) {
            socket.emit('register_error', { error: 'Invalid API key' });
            socket.disconnect();
            return;
        }

        socket.deviceId = device_id;
        this.deviceSockets.set(device_id, socket);

        // 娉ㄥ唽鍒拌澶囩鐞?
        registerDevice({
            deviceId: device_id,
            version,
            chipModel: chip_model,
            flashSize: flash_size,
            freeHeap: free_heap,
            sdkVersion: sdk_version,
            mode: 'online',
        });

        // 鍙戦€佹敞鍐屾垚鍔?+ 瀵嗙爜鏈?
        socket.emit('register_success', {
            message: 'Device registered successfully',
            codebook: getCodebook(),
        });

        // 骞挎挱鍒?Dashboard
        this.io.emit('device:online', {
            deviceId: device_id,
            version,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        });

        logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Device registered', { deviceId: device_id, version });
    }

    private handleHeartbeat(socket: DeviceSocket, data: any) {
        if (!socket.deviceId) return;

        const { uptime, free_heap, wifi_rssi } = data;

        updateDeviceStatus(socket.deviceId, {
            cpuUsage: data.cpu_usage || 0,
            memoryUsage: 100 - (free_heap / (4 * 1024 * 1024) * 100), // 鍋囪 4MB 鎬诲唴瀛?
            uptime,
            wifiRssi: wifi_rssi,
        });

        socket.emit('heartbeat_ack', { timestamp: Date.now() });
    }

    private handleData(socket: DeviceSocket, data: any) {
        if (!socket.deviceId) return;

        const { payload, timestamp } = data;

        // 瑙ｅ帇瀵嗙爜鏈紪鐮?(濡傛灉鏈?
        let decodedPayload = payload;
        if (typeof payload === 'string') {
            try {
                const parsed = JSON.parse(payload);
                if (parsed.codes) {
                    const codebook = getCodebook();
                    parsed.errors = parsed.codes.map((code: string) => codebook[code] || code);
                    delete parsed.codes;
                    decodedPayload = parsed;
                }
            } catch {
                // 淇濇寔鍘熸牱
            }
        }

        // 璁板綍鏃ュ織
        logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Data received', {
            deviceId: socket.deviceId,
            timestamp,
            dataSize: JSON.stringify(decodedPayload).length,
        });

        // 骞挎挱鍒?Dashboard
        this.io.emit('device:data', {
            deviceId: socket.deviceId,
            data: decodedPayload,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        });
    }

    private handleAlert(socket: DeviceSocket, data: any) {
        if (!socket.deviceId) return;

        const { alert_type, message, timestamp } = data;

        logger.warn('[DeviceGateway] Alert from device', {
            deviceId: socket.deviceId,
            alertType: alert_type,
            message,
        });

        // 骞挎挱鍒?Dashboard
        this.io.emit('device:alert', {
            deviceId: socket.deviceId,
            alertType: alert_type,
            message,
            timestamp: timestamp || new Date().toISOString(),
        });
    }

    private handleUnknownError(socket: DeviceSocket, data: any) {
        if (!socket.deviceId) return;

        const { message, timestamp } = data;

        logger.error('[DeviceGateway] Unknown error from device', {
            deviceId: socket.deviceId,
            message,
        });

        // 璁板綍鍒板璁℃棩蹇?
        // TODO: 淇濆瓨鍒版暟鎹簱锛岀敤浜庡悗缁垎鏋愬拰娣诲姞鍒板瘑鐮佹湰

        // 骞挎挱鍒?Dashboard
        this.io.emit('device:unknown_error', {
            deviceId: socket.deviceId,
            message,
            timestamp: timestamp || new Date().toISOString(),
        });
    }

    private handleCodebookSync(socket: DeviceSocket) {
        socket.emit('codebook_sync', { codebook: getCodebook() });
        logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Codebook synced', { deviceId: socket.deviceId });
    }

    private handleDisconnect(socket: DeviceSocket, reason: string) {
        if (socket.deviceId) {
            this.deviceSockets.delete(socket.deviceId);
            setDeviceOffline(socket.deviceId);

            // 骞挎挱鍒?Dashboard
            this.io.emit('device:offline', {
                deviceId: socket.deviceId,
                reason,
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            });

            logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Device disconnected', {
                deviceId: socket.deviceId,
                reason,
            });
        }
    }

    // ============================================
    // 鍛戒护涓嬪彂
    // ============================================

    /**
     * 鍙戦€佸懡浠ゅ埌鎸囧畾璁惧
     */
    public sendCommand(deviceId: string, command: string, params?: any): boolean {
        const socket = this.deviceSockets.get(deviceId);

        if (!socket || !socket.connected) {
            logger.warn('[DeviceGateway] Cannot send command, device offline', { deviceId });
            return false;
        }

        socket.emit('command', { cmd: command, params, timestamp: Date.now() });

        logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Command sent', {
            deviceId,
            command,
            params,
        });

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * 骞挎挱鍛戒护鍒版墍鏈夊湪绾胯澶?
     */
    public broadcastCommand(command: string, params?: any): number {
        let count = 0;

        this.deviceSockets.forEach((socket, deviceId) => {
            if (socket.connected) {
                socket.emit('command', { cmd: command, params, timestamp: Date.now() });
                count++;
            }
        });

        logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Broadcast command', {
            command,
            deviceCount: count,
        });

        return count;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍦ㄧ嚎璁惧鏁伴噺
     */
    public getOnlineCount(): number {
        let count = 0;
        this.deviceSockets.forEach((socket) => {
            if (socket.connected) count++;
        });
        return count;
    }

    /**
     * 鍏抽棴缃戝叧
     */
    public close() {
        this.io.close();
        logger.info('[DeviceGateway] Closed');
    }
}

export default DeviceGateway;

/**
 * FluxGuard Agent CLI - 鍛戒护琛屽惎鍔ㄥ叆鍙?
 * 
 * 鐢ㄦ硶:
 *   npm run agent                    # 浣跨敤榛樿鏈嶅姟鍣ㄥ湴鍧€
 *   npm run agent -- --server=http://your-server:8080
 */

import { startAgent } from './agent';
import logger from './utils/logger';

// 瑙ｆ瀽鍛戒护琛屽弬鏁?
function parseArgs(): { serverUrl: string } {
    const args = process.argv.slice(2);
    let serverUrl = process.env.FLUXGUARD_SERVER || 'http://localhost:8080';

    for (const arg of args) {
        if (arg.startsWith('--server=')) {
            serverUrl = arg.replace('--server=', '');
        }
    }

    return { serverUrl };
}
